        function isSuperAdminAccount(account) {
            return String(account || '').trim().toLowerCase() === 'dsuensx';
        }

        function togglePasswordVisibility(inputId, buttonEl) {
            const input = document.getElementById(inputId); if (!input) return;
            const icon = buttonEl?.querySelector('i'); const showing = input.type === 'text';
            input.type = showing ? 'password' : 'text';
            if (icon) icon.className = showing ? 'fa-solid fa-eye' : 'fa-solid fa-eye-slash';
            buttonEl?.setAttribute('aria-label', showing ? '顯示密碼' : '隱藏密碼');
        }

        function getPunishmentItems(p) {
            if (!p) return [];
            if (Array.isArray(p.items)) return p.items.map(String).filter(Boolean);
            if (Array.isArray(p.item)) return p.item.map(String).filter(Boolean);
            const raw = String(p.item || '').trim();
            return raw ? raw.split(/[、,，]/).map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean) : [];
        }

        function isSuspendedUser(account) {
            const key = String(account || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            if (!key) return false;
            return (appData.policePunishments || []).some(p => String(p.memberId || '').trim().toLowerCase() === key && String(p.type || 'punishment').toLowerCase() === 'punishment' && getPunishmentItems(p).includes('停職調查'));
        }

        function ensureReportRemarks(report) {
            if (!report) return [];
            if (Array.isArray(report.remarks)) return report.remarks;
            const legacy = String(report.remark || '').trim();
            if (!legacy || legacy === '尚未批示' || legacy === '無') return [];
            return [{ authorName: '既有批示', authorAccount: '', role: '', text: legacy, timestamp: report.timestamp || Date.now(), time: report.time || '' }];
        }

        function formatDateTime(timestamp, fallback = '') {
            let value = 0;
            if (timestamp && typeof timestamp.toMillis === 'function') value = timestamp.toMillis();
            else if (timestamp instanceof Date) value = timestamp.getTime();
            else if (typeof timestamp === 'string' && timestamp.trim()) {
                const parsed = Date.parse(timestamp);
                if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) value = parsed;
                else if (/^\d+$/.test(timestamp.trim())) value = Number(timestamp.trim());
            } else {
                value = Number(timestamp);
            }
            if (Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0) {
                return new Date(value).toLocaleString('zh-TW', { year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit', hour12: false });
            }
            return fallback || '--';
        }


        // ===== 修復：補回遺失的管理中心／公告函式 =====
        let announcementTimer=null,announcementTransitionTimer=null,announcementIndex=0,announcementRenderToken=0;
function latestDutyCount(rows){const latest=new Map();(rows||[]).forEach(r=>{const key=String(r.account||r.name||r.id||'');if(!key)return;const old=latest.get(key);if(!old||Number(r.timestamp||0)>Number(old.timestamp||0))latest.set(key,r);});return [...latest.values()].filter(r=>r.status==='上班').length;}

window.renderAdminOverview = function renderAdminOverview() {
            const users = appData?.users || [];
            const policeReports = appData?.policeReports || [];
            const fireReports = appData?.fireReports || [];
            const pd=document.getElementById('stat-police-duty'),fd=document.getElementById('stat-fire-duty'),ad=document.getElementById('stat-admin-duty');
            const total=document.getElementById('stat-total-users'),pr=document.getElementById('stat-police-reports'),fr=document.getElementById('stat-fire-reports');
            if(total) total.textContent = users.length;
            if(pr) pr.textContent = policeReports.length;
            if(fr) fr.textContent = fireReports.length;
            if(pd) pd.textContent=latestDutyCount(appData?.policeClocks || []);
            if(fd) fd.textContent=latestDutyCount(appData?.fireClocks || []);
            if(ad) ad.textContent=latestDutyCount(appData?.adminClocks || []);
        };

function renderAdminClocks(){const t=document.getElementById('admin-clocks-tbody');if(!t)return;const rows=[...(appData.adminClocks||[])].sort((a,b)=>b.timestamp-a.timestamp);t.innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(c=>`<tr><td class="p-4 font-medium text-white">${c.name||'--'} (${c.account||'--'})</td><td class="p-4"><span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs ${c.status==='上班'?'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400':'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400'}">${c.status}</span></td><td class="p-4 text-slate-300 text-xs">${c.time||formatDateTime(c.timestamp)}</td><td class="p-4 text-slate-400 text-xs">${c.remark||'無'}</td><td class="p-4 text-right"><button onclick="deleteAdminClock('${c.docId||c.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-red-600/20 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs">刪除</button></td></tr>`).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="5" class="p-6 text-center text-slate-500">尚無管理中心打卡紀錄。</td></tr>';}

function openAdminClockModal(status){if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='admin'){showToast('管理中心打卡僅限最高管理員。','warning','權限不足');return;}document.getElementById('admin-clock-status').value=status;document.getElementById('admin-clock-remark').value='';document.getElementById('admin-clock-user-preview').textContent=`${currentUser.name} (${currentUser.account})`;document.getElementById('modal-admin-clock').classList.remove('hidden');}

async function submitAdminClock(){if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='admin')return;const status=document.getElementById('admin-clock-status').value,remark=document.getElementById('admin-clock-remark').value.trim()||'無',now=Date.now(),id=`ADMCLK-${now}`,obj={id,account:currentUser.account,name:currentUser.name,status,remark,time:formatDateTime(now),timestamp:now};try{await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'adminClocks',id),obj);appData.adminClocks.unshift({docId:id,...obj});closeModal('modal-admin-clock');renderAdminClocks();renderAdminOverview();showToast('管理中心打卡成功。','success','打卡成功');}catch(e){showToast('管理中心打卡失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}}

async function deleteAdminClock(id){if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='admin')return;if(!(await showConfirm('確定刪除此筆管理中心打卡紀錄嗎？','刪除管理中心打卡紀錄','確定刪除')))return;try{await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'adminClocks',id));appData.adminClocks=appData.adminClocks.filter(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)!==String(id));await logAdminAction('刪除管理中心打卡紀錄',id);renderAdminClocks();renderAdminOverview();showSubmitSuccessAnimation('移除成功','管理中心打卡紀錄已成功移除。');}catch(e){showToast('刪除失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}}

async function saveMemo() {
            const text = document.getElementById('admin-memo-textarea').value.trim();
            appData.adminMemo = text;
            try {
                await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db, 'systemSettings', 'adminMemo'), { content: text });
                showToast('管理員共同備忘錄已成功儲存。', 'success', '儲存成功');
            } catch (e) {
                showToast('儲存失敗：' + e.message, 'error', '操作失敗');
            }
        }

function announcementExpiry(duration, now) {
            if (duration === '7d') return now + 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
            if (duration === 'manual') return 0;
            return now + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
        }

function getActiveAnnouncements() {
            const now = Date.now();
            return (appData.announcements || [])
                .filter(a => a && a.active !== false && (!a.expiresAt || Number(a.expiresAt) > now))
                .sort((a,b) => Number(a.createdAt || 0) - Number(b.createdAt || 0));
        }

function stopAnnouncementRotation(){
            if(announcementTimer){clearTimeout(announcementTimer);announcementTimer=null;}
            if(announcementTransitionTimer){clearTimeout(announcementTransitionTimer);announcementTransitionTimer=null;}
        }

function renderSiteAnnouncements(){
            const bar=document.getElementById('site-announcement-bar');
            const track=document.getElementById('announcement-track');
            if(!bar||!track)return;
            const rows=getActiveAnnouncements();
            const token=++announcementRenderToken;
            stopAnnouncementRotation();

            if(!rows.length){
                bar.classList.add('hidden');
                track.innerHTML='';
                announcementIndex=0;
                return;
            }

            bar.classList.remove('hidden');
            if(announcementIndex>=rows.length)announcementIndex=0;

            // 每次重新渲染前先完整清除舊 DOM，避免背景分頁／閒置後殘留舊動畫狀態。
            track.innerHTML=rows.map((a,i)=>`
                <div class="announcement-item ${i===announcementIndex?'active':''}" data-announcement-index="${i}">
                    <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"><i class="fa-solid fa-bullhorn"></i></div>
                    <div class="announcement-content">
                        <div class="announcement-text text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-200 font-semibold">${escapeHtml(a.text||'')}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="announcement-publisher text-xs text-slate-500">${escapeHtml(a.publisher||'')}</div>
                </div>`).join('');

            if(rows.length<=1)return;

            // 使用遞迴 setTimeout，不使用 setInterval，避免瀏覽器閒置後計時器與動畫累積。
            const scheduleNext=()=>{
                if(token!==announcementRenderToken||document.hidden)return;
                announcementTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
                    if(token!==announcementRenderToken||document.hidden)return;
                    const items=[...track.querySelectorAll('.announcement-item')];
                    if(items.length!==rows.length){renderSiteAnnouncements();return;}
                    const oldIndex=announcementIndex;
                    const nextIndex=(announcementIndex+1)%rows.length;
                    const oldItem=items[oldIndex];
                    const newItem=items[nextIndex];
                    if(!oldItem||!newItem){renderSiteAnnouncements();return;}

                    // 先保證只有目前項目可見，避免閒置恢復時兩則同時停留。
                    items.forEach(item=>item.classList.remove('active','exit-up'));
                    oldItem.classList.add('active');
                    announcementIndex=nextIndex;

                    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
                        if(token!==announcementRenderToken||document.hidden)return;
                        oldItem.classList.remove('active');
                        oldItem.classList.add('exit-up');
                        newItem.classList.add('active');
                        announcementTransitionTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
                            if(token!==announcementRenderToken)return;
                            oldItem.classList.remove('exit-up');
                            announcementTransitionTimer=null;
                            scheduleNext();
                        },500);
                    });
                },5000);
            };
            scheduleNext();
        }

// 分頁從背景／閒置狀態恢復時，重新建立公告動畫，避免瀏覽器計時器節流造成重疊。
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{
            if(document.hidden){
                stopAnnouncementRotation();
                return;
            }
            if(document.getElementById('announcement-track'))renderSiteAnnouncements();
        });

function renderAdminAnnouncements() {
            const c = document.getElementById('admin-announcements-list');
            if (!c) return;
            const rows = [...(appData.announcements || [])].sort((a,b) => Number(b.createdAt || 0) - Number(a.createdAt || 0));
            c.innerHTML = rows.length ? rows.map(a => {
                const expired = a.expiresAt && Number(a.expiresAt) <= Date.now();
                const active = a.active !== false && !expired;
                const key = String(a.docId || a.id || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'");
                return `<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center gap-4">
                    <div class="flex-1 min-w-0"><div class="text-sm font-semibold text-white whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">${escapeHtml(a.text || '')}</div>
                    <div class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">發布人：${escapeHtml(a.publisher || '--')} ／ ${a.duration==='manual'?'手動下架':a.duration==='7d'?'7 天':'1 天'} ／ ${formatDateTime(a.createdAt,a.time)}</div></div>
                    <div class="announcement-admin-actions"><span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs ${active?'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400':'bg-slate-800 text-slate-500'}">${active?'發布中':'已下架'}</span>
                    ${active ? `<button type="button" onclick="unpublishAnnouncement('${key}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs">手動下架</button>` : `<button type="button" onclick="republishAnnouncement('${key}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-amber-600/20 text-amber-300 rounded-lg text-xs">重新發布</button>`}
                    <button type="button" onclick="deleteAnnouncement('${key}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-red-600/15 text-red-300 hover:bg-red-600/25 rounded-lg text-xs">移除</button></div></div>`;
            }).join('') : '<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-8 text-center text-slate-500">尚未建立公告。</div>';
        }

async function publishAnnouncement() {
            if (!currentUser || currentUser.role !== 'admin') { showToast('只有最高管理員可以發布公告。','warning','權限不足'); return; }
            const text = document.getElementById('announcement-text-input')?.value.trim() || '';
            const publisher = document.getElementById('announcement-publisher-input')?.value.trim() || '';
            const duration = document.getElementById('announcement-duration-input')?.value || '1d';
            if (!text || !publisher) { showToast('請填寫公告內容與發布人。','warning','資料不完整'); return; }
            const now = Date.now(), id = `ANN-${now}`;
            const obj = { id, text, publisher, duration, createdAt:now, time:formatDateTime(now), expiresAt:announcementExpiry(duration,now), active:true, createdBy:currentUser.account };
            try {
                await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'announcements',id),obj);
                appData.announcements.push({docId:id,...obj});
                const input = document.getElementById('announcement-text-input'); if (input) input.value='';
                renderAdminAnnouncements(); renderSiteAnnouncements();
                showToast('公告已發布至首頁。','success','發布成功');
            } catch(e) { showToast('公告發布失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗'); }
        }

async function deleteAnnouncement(id) {
            if (!currentUser || currentUser.role !== 'admin') { showToast('只有最高管理員可以移除網站公告。','warning','權限不足'); return; }
            const a=(appData.announcements||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id)); if(!a)return;
            if (!(await showConfirm(`確定要永久移除這則網站公告嗎？\\n\\n${a.text||''}\\n\\n移除後將無法恢復。`,'移除網站公告','確定移除'))) return;
            try { await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'announcements',a.docId||a.id)); appData.announcements=appData.announcements.filter(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)!==String(id)); await logAdminAction('移除網站公告',id); renderAdminAnnouncements(); renderSiteAnnouncements(); showToast('公告已永久移除。','success','移除成功'); }
            catch(e){ showToast('公告移除失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗'); }
        }

async function unpublishAnnouncement(id) {
            const a=(appData.announcements||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id)); if(!a)return;
            try { await fm.updateDoc(fm.doc(db,'announcements',a.docId||a.id),{active:false,unpublishedAt:Date.now(),unpublishedBy:currentUser.account}); a.active=false; await logAdminAction('手動下架網站公告',id); renderAdminAnnouncements(); renderSiteAnnouncements(); showToast('公告已下架。','success','操作完成'); }
            catch(e){ showToast('下架失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗'); }
        }

async function republishAnnouncement(id) {
            const a=(appData.announcements||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id)); if(!a)return;
            const now=Date.now(), expires=announcementExpiry(a.duration,now);
            try { await fm.updateDoc(fm.doc(db,'announcements',a.docId||a.id),{active:true,createdAt:now,time:formatDateTime(now),expiresAt:expires}); a.active=true;a.createdAt=now;a.time=formatDateTime(now);a.expiresAt=expires; await logAdminAction('重新發布網站公告',id); renderAdminAnnouncements(); renderSiteAnnouncements(); showToast('公告已重新發布。','success','操作完成'); }
            catch(e){ showToast('重新發布失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗'); }
        }

        async function loadAllDataFromCloud() {
            try {
                const usersSnap = await fm.getDocs(fm.collection(db, 'users'));
                if (!usersSnap.empty) {
                    appData.users = usersSnap.docs.map(d => {
                        const data = d.data();
                        let units = data.units || (data.unit ? [data.unit] : ['admin']);
                        return { docId: d.id, ...data, units, permissions: Array.isArray(data.permissions) && data.permissions.length ? data.permissions : (data.role ? [data.role] : []) };
                    });
                } else {
                    // users 集合為空時保持為空，不自動建立 DSUENSX 或任何部門預設帳號。
                    // 現有 Firebase 帳號資料永遠以雲端為準，不因重新發布網站而重設。
                    appData.users = [];
                }
                // 不自動建立或重設任何萬能帳號；使用者帳號完全以 Firebase 雲端資料為準。

                const safeCollectionDocs = async (collectionName, retries = 2) => {
                    let lastError = null;
                    for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= retries; attempt++) {
                        try {
                            return { ok: true, docs: (await fm.getDocs(fm.collection(db, collectionName))).docs };
                        } catch (err) {
                            lastError = err;
                            if (attempt < retries) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 350 * (attempt + 1)));
                        }
                    }
                    console.warn(`雲端集合 ${collectionName} 讀取失敗，保留目前資料並繼續同步：`, lastError);
                    return { ok: false, docs: [], error: lastError };
                };
                const policeRepSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('policeReports');
                if (policeRepSnap.ok) appData.policeReports = policeRepSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() })).map(r => ({ ...r, remarks: ensureReportRemarks(r) }));
                const policePendingSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('policePendingReports');
                if (policePendingSnap.ok) appData.policePendingReports = policePendingSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() })).map(r => ({ ...r, remarks: ensureReportRemarks(r) }));
                const reportNotifySnap = await safeCollectionDocs('reportNotifications');
                if (reportNotifySnap.ok) appData.reportNotifications = reportNotifySnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() }));

                const punishSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('policePunishments');
                if (punishSnap.ok) appData.policePunishments = punishSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() }));

                const citizenSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('citizenRecords');
                if (citizenSnap.ok) appData.citizenRecords = citizenSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() }));
                const vehicleSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('vehicleRecords');
                if (vehicleSnap.ok) appData.vehicleRecords = vehicleSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() }));
                const wantedPendingSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('wantedPendingRecords');
                if (wantedPendingSnap.ok) appData.wantedPendingRecords = wantedPendingSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() }));
                const wantedSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('wantedRecords');
                if (wantedSnap.ok) appData.wantedRecords = wantedSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() }));

                const firePendingSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('firePendingReports');
                if (firePendingSnap.ok) appData.firePendingReports = firePendingSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() })).map(r => ({ ...r, remarks: ensureReportRemarks(r) }));
                const fireRepSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('fireReports');
                if (fireRepSnap.ok) appData.fireReports = fireRepSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() })).map(r => ({ ...r, remarks: ensureReportRemarks(r) }));

                const emsSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('emsReports');
                if (emsSnap.ok) appData.emsReports = emsSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() })).map(r => ({ ...r, remarks: ensureReportRemarks(r) }));
                const emsPendingSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('emsPendingReports');
                if (emsPendingSnap.ok) appData.emsPendingReports = emsPendingSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() })).map(r => ({ ...r, remarks: ensureReportRemarks(r) }));

                const policeClockSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('policeClocks');
                if (policeClockSnap.ok) appData.policeClocks = policeClockSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() }));

                const fireClockSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('fireClocks');
                if (fireClockSnap.ok) appData.fireClocks = fireClockSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() }));

                const logsSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('adminLogs');
                if (logsSnap.ok) appData.adminLogs = logsSnap.docs.map(d => ({ docId: d.id, ...d.data() }));

                const sessionsSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('loginSessions');
                if (sessionsSnap.ok) appData.loginSessions = sessionsSnap.docs.map(d=>({docId:d.id,...d.data()}));
                const feedbackSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('feedbackReports');
                if (feedbackSnap.ok) appData.feedbackReports = feedbackSnap.docs.map(d=>({docId:d.id,...d.data()}));

                const annSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('announcements');
                if (annSnap.ok) appData.announcements = annSnap.docs.map(d=>({docId:d.id,...d.data()}));
                const blockedSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('blockedDevices');
                if (blockedSnap.ok) appData.blockedDevices = blockedSnap.docs.map(d=>({docId:d.id,...d.data()}));
                const loginFailureSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('loginFailures');
                if (loginFailureSnap.ok) appData.loginFailures = loginFailureSnap.docs.map(d=>({docId:d.id,...d.data()}));
                const adminClockSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('adminClocks');
                if (adminClockSnap.ok) appData.adminClocks = adminClockSnap.docs.map(d=>({docId:d.id,...d.data()}));

                const academyCodeSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('academyCodes');
                if (academyCodeSnap.ok) appData.academyCodes = academyCodeSnap.docs.map(d=>({docId:d.id,...d.data()})).filter(x=>x.code);

                const settingsSnap = await safeCollectionDocs('systemSettings');
                if (settingsSnap.ok) {
                    const rulesDoc = settingsSnap.docs.find(d => d.id === 'policeViolationRules');
                    if (rulesDoc && Array.isArray(rulesDoc.data().rules)) {
                        appData.policeViolationRules = rulesDoc.data().rules.map(x => [String(x.name || x[0] || ''), Number(x.minutes ?? x[1] ?? 0)]).filter(x => x[0] && Number.isFinite(x[1]));
                    }
                    if (!appData.policeViolationRules.length) {
                        appData.policeViolationRules = POLICE_VIOLATION_DEFAULTS.map(x => [...x]);
                        try { await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db, 'systemSettings', 'policeViolationRules'), { rules: appData.policeViolationRules, updatedAt: Date.now(), updatedBy: currentUser?.account || 'system' }); } catch (e) { console.warn('預設刑期表寫入失敗，略過：', e); }
                    }
                    POLICE_VIOLATION_RULES = appData.policeViolationRules.map(x => [...x]);
                    const lookupLocksDoc = settingsSnap.docs.find(d => d.id === 'lookupLocks');
                    if (lookupLocksDoc) {
                        const v = lookupLocksDoc.data();
                        appData.lookupLocks = { citizen: v.citizen !== false, vehicles: v.vehicles !== false, wanted: v.wanted !== false };
                    }

                    const memoDoc = settingsSnap.docs.find(d => d.id === 'adminMemo');
                    if (memoDoc) {
                        appData.adminMemo = memoDoc.data().content || '';
                    } else {
                        appData.adminMemo = '1. 本週重點：加強轄區夜間巡邏與消防安檢。\n2. 各分局/大隊長請於每日下班前完成同仁打卡與案件審核。';
                        try { await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db, 'systemSettings', 'adminMemo'), { content: appData.adminMemo, updatedAt: Date.now(), updatedBy: currentUser?.account || 'system' }); } catch (e) { console.warn('預設備忘錄寫入失敗，略過：', e); }
                    }
                } else {
                    // 新增集合權限不足時，不清空既有本機資料，也不讓整個雲端同步中斷。
                    POLICE_VIOLATION_RULES = (appData.policeViolationRules?.length ? appData.policeViolationRules : POLICE_VIOLATION_DEFAULTS).map(x => [...x]);
                    if (!appData.policeViolationRules.length) appData.policeViolationRules = POLICE_VIOLATION_DEFAULTS.map(x => [...x]);
                }

                updateAccessLocks();
                renderPoliceReports();
                renderPoliceReview();
                renderMyProfile();
                renderPolicePunishments();
                renderPoliceClocks();
                renderLookupSystems();
                renderLookupLockSettings();
                renderFireReports();
                renderEMSReports();
                renderFireClocks();
                renderAdminUsers();
                renderAdminLogs();
                renderAdminStorage();
                if (typeof window.renderAdminOverview === 'function') window.renderAdminOverview();
                renderAdminAnnouncements();
                renderAdminClocks();
                renderSiteAnnouncements();
                const ap=document.getElementById('announcement-publisher-input'); if(ap && !ap.value) ap.value=currentUser?.name||'';
                populateMemberSelects();
                restoreAcademySession();
                renderAdminAcademyCodes();
            } catch (e) {
                console.error("雲端資料同步讀取失敗", e);
            }
        }

        // 即時資料同步：不再每 10 秒整批讀取 Firestore。
        // 使用 onSnapshot，只有資料「新增／修改／刪除」時才同步，降低免費讀取額度消耗。
        let realtimeUnsubscribers = [];
        function stopRealtimeListeners() {
            realtimeUnsubscribers.forEach(unsub => { try { if (typeof unsub === 'function') unsub(); } catch(e) {} });
            realtimeUnsubscribers = [];
        }
        function startRealtimeListeners() {
            stopRealtimeListeners();
            if (!db || !fm || !currentUser) return;

            const refreshVisibleData = () => {
                renderPersonalNotifications();
                if (currentUser) {
                    if (currentUser.role === 'admin' || userHasUnit('police')) renderPoliceReports();
                    if (currentUser.role === 'admin' || userHasUnit('fire')) renderFireReports();
                }
                if (canReviewPoliceReports()) renderPoliceReview();
                if (canReviewFireReports()) renderFireReview();
            };

            const subscribe = (collectionName, mapper, onChange) => {
                try {
                    const unsub = fm.onSnapshot(fm.collection(db, collectionName), snap => {
                        onChange(snap.docs.map(d => mapper(d)));
                        refreshVisibleData();
                    }, err => console.warn(`即時監聽 ${collectionName} 失敗：`, err));
                    realtimeUnsubscribers.push(unsub);
                } catch(e) { console.warn(`建立 ${collectionName} 即時監聽失敗：`, e); }
            };

            const subscribeNotifications = () => {
                try {
                    const ref = fm.query(fm.collection(db, 'reportNotifications'), fm.where('recipientAccount', '==', currentUser.account));
                    const unsub = fm.onSnapshot(ref, snap => {
                        appData.reportNotifications = snap.docs.map(d => ({docId:d.id, ...d.data()}));
                        refreshVisibleData();
                    }, err => console.warn('個人通知即時監聽失敗：', err));
                    realtimeUnsubscribers.push(unsub);
                } catch(e) { console.warn('建立個人通知即時監聽失敗：', e); }
            };

            // 正式案件：只監聽目前帳號有權瀏覽的單位。
            if (currentUser.role === 'admin' || userHasUnit('police')) {
                subscribe('policeReports', d => ({docId:d.id, ...d.data()}), rows => { appData.policeReports = rows.map(r => ({...r, remarks:ensureReportRemarks(r)})); renderAdminStorage(); });
            }
            if (currentUser.role === 'admin' || userHasUnit('fire')) {
                subscribe('fireReports', d => ({docId:d.id, ...d.data()}), rows => { appData.fireReports = rows.map(r => ({...r, remarks:ensureReportRemarks(r)})); renderAdminStorage(); });
            }
            if (currentUser.role === 'admin' || userHasUnit('police')) {
                subscribe('citizenRecords', d => ({docId:d.id, ...d.data()}), rows => { appData.citizenRecords = rows; renderLookupSystems(); renderAdminStorage(); });
                subscribe('vehicleRecords', d => ({docId:d.id, ...d.data()}), rows => { appData.vehicleRecords = rows; renderLookupSystems(); renderAdminStorage(); });
                subscribe('wantedPendingRecords', d => ({docId:d.id, ...d.data()}), rows => { appData.wantedPendingRecords = rows; updateReviewTabBadges(); renderPoliceReview(); renderPersonalNotifications(); });
                subscribe('wantedRecords', d => ({docId:d.id, ...d.data()}), rows => { appData.wantedRecords = rows; renderLookupSystems(); updateWantedDiscordBotStatus(); });
            }
            if (currentUser.role === 'admin') {
                try {
                    const unsub = fm.onSnapshot(fm.doc(db,'systemSettings','lookupLocks'), snap => {
                        if (snap.exists()) { const v=snap.data(); appData.lookupLocks={citizen:v.citizen!==false,vehicles:v.vehicles!==false,wanted:v.wanted!==false}; renderLookupSystems(); }
                    }, err => console.warn('查詢頻道設定即時監聽失敗：',err));
                    realtimeUnsubscribers.push(unsub);
                } catch(e) { console.warn('建立查詢頻道設定監聽失敗：',e); }
            }

            // 待審案件：寄件者需要自己的案件；高層／高階／管理中心則需要看到對應單位的全部待審案件。
            const canPoliceReviewNow = canReviewPoliceReports();
            const canFireReviewNow = canReviewFireReports();
            if (canPoliceReviewNow || userHasUnit('police')) {
                subscribe('policePendingReports', d => ({docId:d.id, ...d.data()}), rows => { appData.policePendingReports = rows.map(r => ({...r, remarks:ensureReportRemarks(r)})); });
            }
            if (canFireReviewNow || userHasUnit('fire')) {
                subscribe('firePendingReports', d => ({docId:d.id, ...d.data()}), rows => { appData.firePendingReports = rows.map(r => ({...r, remarks:ensureReportRemarks(r)})); });
                subscribe('emsPendingReports', d => ({docId:d.id, ...d.data()}), rows => { appData.emsPendingReports = rows.map(r => ({...r, remarks:ensureReportRemarks(r)})); });
            }

            // 個人通知只查目前登入者，避免每次都把整個通知集合下載。
            subscribeNotifications();

            // 網站公告即時同步：只在公告新增／修改／刪除時更新，避免每 5~10 秒整批讀取。
            try {
                const unsubAnnouncements = fm.onSnapshot(fm.collection(db, 'announcements'), snap => {
                    appData.announcements = snap.docs.map(d => ({docId:d.id, ...d.data()}));
                    renderAdminAnnouncements();
                    renderSiteAnnouncements();
                }, err => console.warn('網站公告即時監聽失敗：', err));
                realtimeUnsubscribers.push(unsubAnnouncements);
            } catch(e) { console.warn('建立網站公告即時監聽失敗：', e); }
        }

        function populateMemberSelects() {
            const fireSelect = document.getElementById('fire-calc-member');
            const punishMemberSelect = document.getElementById('punish-member-select');

            if (fireSelect) fireSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- 請選擇消防隊員 --</option>';
            if (punishMemberSelect) punishMemberSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- 請選擇被獎懲員警 --</option>';

            appData.users.forEach(u => {
                const opt = document.createElement('option');
                opt.value = u.account;
                opt.textContent = `${u.name} (${u.account})`;
                const units = u.units || [u.unit];
                
                if (fireSelect && units.includes('fire')) fireSelect.appendChild(opt.cloneNode(true));
                if (punishMemberSelect && units.includes('police')) {
                    const punishOpt = opt.cloneNode(true);
                    punishOpt.textContent = `${u.name} (${u.staffNumber || u.account})`;
                    punishMemberSelect.appendChild(punishOpt);
                }
            });

            if (punishMemberSelect) {
                punishMemberSelect.onchange = (e) => {
                    const selectedAcc = e.target.value;
                    const foundU = appData.users.find(u => u.account === selectedAcc);
                    const idInput = document.getElementById('punish-member-id');
                    if (foundU && idInput) {
                        idInput.value = foundU.staffNumber || '';
                    }
                };
            }
        }

        function switchTab(tabId) {
            if (tabId === 'admin' && !canAccessManagementCenter()) {
                showToast('目前帳號沒有管理中心權限。', 'warning', '權限不足');
                tabId = 'home';
            }
            ['home', 'police', 'fire', 'notifications', 'profile', 'academy', 'admin'].forEach(t => {
                const tabEl = document.getElementById(`tab-${t}`);
                if (tabEl) tabEl.classList.add('hidden');
                
                const navBtn = document.getElementById(`nav-${t}`);
                if (navBtn) {
                    navBtn.className = "nav-btn px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800";
                }
            });
            
            const targetTab = document.getElementById(`tab-${tabId}`);
            if (targetTab) targetTab.classList.remove('hidden');

            const activeNav = document.getElementById(`nav-${tabId}`);
            if (activeNav) {
                activeNav.className = "nav-btn px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition text-blue-400 bg-blue-500/10";
            }
            
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
            
            if (tabId === 'notifications') renderPersonalNotifications();
            if (tabId === 'profile') renderMyProfile();
            if (tabId === 'academy') {
                renderAcademySession();
                renderAcademyCache();
            }
            if (tabId === 'admin') {
                if (isAcademyInstructor() && getEffectiveRole(currentUser) !== 'admin') {
                    switchAdminSubTab('academy');
                } else {
                    switchAdminSubTab('users');
                    renderAdminUsers();
                    renderAdminLogs();
                    renderAdminStorage();
                    if (typeof window.renderAdminOverview === 'function') window.renderAdminOverview();
                    refreshAdminOnlineUsers();
                    renderAdminDevices();
                    renderAdminFeedback();
                    renderAdminAcademyCodes();
                    const memoInput = document.getElementById('admin-memo-textarea');
                    if (memoInput) memoInput.value = appData.adminMemo || '';
                }
            }
        }

        function getUniquePendingRows(rows, keyFn){
            const map=new Map();
            (rows||[]).forEach((row)=>{
                const key=String(keyFn(row)||'').trim();
                if(!key) return;
                const prev=map.get(key);
                const currentTime=Number(row.submittedAt||row.updatedAt||row.timestamp||0);
                const prevTime=Number(prev?.submittedAt||prev?.updatedAt||prev?.timestamp||0);
                if(!prev || currentTime>=prevTime) map.set(key,row);
            });
            return Array.from(map.values());
        }

        function updateReviewTabBadges(){
            const policeBadge=document.getElementById('police-review-tab-badge');
            const fireBadge=document.getElementById('fire-review-tab-badge');
            const policeRows=getUniquePendingRows((appData.policePendingReports||[]).filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='pending'),r=>r.caseId||r.docId);
            const wantedRows=getUniquePendingRows((appData.wantedPendingRecords||[]).filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='pending'),r=>r.id||r.docId);
            const fireRows=getUniquePendingRows((appData.firePendingReports||[]).filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='pending'),r=>r.caseId||r.docId);
            const emsRows=getUniquePendingRows((appData.emsPendingReports||[]).filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='pending'),r=>r.id||r.docId);
            const policeCount=canReviewPoliceReports()?policeRows.length+wantedRows.length:0;
            const fireCount=canReviewFireReports()?fireRows.length+emsRows.length:0;
            if(policeBadge){policeBadge.textContent=String(policeCount);policeBadge.classList.toggle('hidden',policeCount<=0);}
            if(fireBadge){fireBadge.textContent=String(fireCount);fireBadge.classList.toggle('hidden',fireCount<=0);}
        }

        function switchPoliceSubTab(sub) {
            if (sub === 'review' && !canReviewPoliceReports()) {
                showToast('高層審核系統僅限警察高層、高階成員與管理中心使用。', 'warning', '權限不足');
                sub = 'reports';
            }
            ['reports', 'review', 'punishments', 'clocks', 'hours', 'citizen', 'vehicles', 'wanted'].forEach(s => {
                const el = document.getElementById(`police-sub-${s}`);
                if (el) el.classList.add('hidden');
                const btn = document.getElementById(`btn-police-${s}`);
                if (btn) btn.className = "police-sub-btn px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold transition text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-900 whitespace-nowrap";
            });
            const target = document.getElementById(`police-sub-${sub}`);
            if (target) target.classList.remove('hidden');
            const activeBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-police-${sub}`);
            if (activeBtn) activeBtn.className = "police-sub-btn px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold transition bg-blue-600 text-white whitespace-nowrap";
            if(sub==='review') renderPoliceReview();
        }

        function switchFireSubTab(sub) {
            if (sub === 'review' && !canReviewFireReports()) {
                showToast('高層審核系統僅限消防高層、高階成員與管理中心使用。', 'warning', '權限不足');
                sub = 'reports';
            }
            ['reports', 'review', 'ems', 'clocks', 'hours'].forEach(s => {
                const el = document.getElementById(`fire-sub-${s}`);
                if (el) el.classList.add('hidden');
                const btn = document.getElementById(`btn-fire-${s}`);
                if (btn) btn.className = "fire-sub-btn px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold transition text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-900 whitespace-nowrap";
            });
            const target = document.getElementById(`fire-sub-${sub}`);
            if (target) target.classList.remove('hidden');
            const activeBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-fire-${sub}`);
            if (activeBtn) activeBtn.className = "fire-sub-btn px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold transition bg-red-600 text-white whitespace-nowrap";
            if(sub==='review') renderFireReview();
        }

        function switchAdminSubTab(sub){
            const tabs=['users','announcements','clock','logs','storage','memo','overview','online','devices','feedback','academy','jail-rules','lookup-locks'];
            const academyOnly = !!currentUser && getEffectiveRole(currentUser) !== 'admin' && hasUserPermission(currentUser,'academyInstructor');
            const allowed = !academyOnly || sub === 'academy';
            tabs.forEach(x=>{
                document.getElementById(`admin-sub-${x}`)?.classList.add('hidden');
                document.getElementById(`btn-admin-${x}`)?.classList.remove('bg-purple-600','text-white');
            });
            const denied=document.getElementById('admin-sub-permission-denied');
            if(denied) denied.classList.toggle('hidden',allowed);
            if(!allowed){
                const title=document.getElementById('admin-permission-denied-title');
                const msg=document.getElementById('admin-permission-denied-message');
                if(title) title.textContent='權限不足';
                if(msg) msg.textContent='警專教官帳號僅可使用「專生考核系統」分頁，其他管理中心功能無權限。';
                return;
            }
            document.getElementById(`admin-sub-${sub}`)?.classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById(`btn-admin-${sub}`)?.classList.add('bg-purple-600','text-white');
            if(sub==='online' && getEffectiveRole(currentUser)==='admin') refreshAdminOnlineUsers();
            if(sub==='devices' && getEffectiveRole(currentUser)==='admin') renderAdminDevices();
            if(sub==='feedback' && getEffectiveRole(currentUser)==='admin') renderAdminFeedback();
            if(sub==='storage' && getEffectiveRole(currentUser)==='admin') renderAdminStorage();
            if(sub==='users' && getEffectiveRole(currentUser)==='admin') renderAdminUsers();
            if(sub==='logs' && getEffectiveRole(currentUser)==='admin') renderAdminLogs();
            if(sub==='academy' && canAccessManagementCenter()) renderAdminAcademyCodes();
            if(sub==='jail-rules' && getEffectiveRole(currentUser)==='admin') renderAdminJailRules();
            if(sub==='announcements' && getEffectiveRole(currentUser)==='admin') renderAdminAnnouncements();
            if(sub==='clock' && getEffectiveRole(currentUser)==='admin') renderAdminClocks();
        }

        function closeModal(modalId) {
            const m = document.getElementById(modalId);
            if (m) m.classList.add('hidden');
        }

        let appConfirmResolver = null;
        function showConfirm(message, title='確認操作', confirmText='確定', cancelText='取消') {
            return new Promise(resolve => {
                const overlay=document.getElementById('app-confirm-overlay');
                const titleEl=document.getElementById('app-confirm-title');
                const msgEl=document.getElementById('app-confirm-message');
                const ok=document.getElementById('app-confirm-ok');
                const cancel=document.getElementById('app-confirm-cancel');
                if(!overlay||!ok||!cancel){ resolve(false); return; }
                if(appConfirmResolver) appConfirmResolver(false);
                appConfirmResolver=resolve;
                titleEl.textContent=title; msgEl.textContent=message; ok.textContent=confirmText; cancel.textContent=cancelText;
                overlay.classList.remove('show'); void overlay.offsetWidth; overlay.classList.add('show'); overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
                setTimeout(()=>ok.focus(),80);
            });
        }
        function finishConfirm(value){
            const overlay=document.getElementById('app-confirm-overlay');
            if(overlay){overlay.classList.remove('show');overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
            const resolve=appConfirmResolver; appConfirmResolver=null; if(resolve) resolve(value);
        }
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
            document.getElementById('app-confirm-ok')?.addEventListener('click',()=>finishConfirm(true));
            document.getElementById('app-confirm-cancel')?.addEventListener('click',()=>finishConfirm(false));
            document.getElementById('app-confirm-overlay')?.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.id==='app-confirm-overlay')finishConfirm(false);});
        });

        function showToast(message, type = 'info', title = '') {
            if (type === 'success') {
                showSubmitSuccessAnimation(title || '操作成功', message);
                return;
            }
            const container = document.getElementById('toast-container');
            if (!container) return;

            const safeType = ['success', 'error', 'warning', 'info'].includes(type) ? type : 'info';
            const toast = document.createElement('div');
            toast.className = `app-toast ${safeType}`;

            const iconMap = {
                success: 'fa-circle-check',
                error: 'fa-circle-xmark',
                warning: 'fa-triangle-exclamation',
                info: 'fa-circle-info'
            };
            const defaultTitleMap = {
                success: '操作成功',
                error: '操作失敗',
                warning: '權限提示',
                info: '系統提示'
            };
            const safeTitle = title || defaultTitleMap[safeType];

            const icon = document.createElement('div');
            icon.className = 'app-toast-icon';
            icon.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid ${iconMap[safeType]}"></i>`;

            const content = document.createElement('div');
            content.className = 'min-w-0 flex-1';
            const titleEl = document.createElement('div');
            titleEl.className = 'text-sm font-bold text-white';
            titleEl.textContent = safeTitle;
            const messageEl = document.createElement('div');
            messageEl.className = 'text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-300 mt-0.5 break-words';
            messageEl.textContent = message;
            content.appendChild(titleEl);
            content.appendChild(messageEl);

            const closeBtn = document.createElement('button');
            closeBtn.className = 'app-toast-close p-1';
            closeBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', '關閉提示');
            closeBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i>';
            closeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => removeToast());

            toast.appendChild(icon);
            toast.appendChild(content);
            toast.appendChild(closeBtn);
            container.appendChild(toast);

            requestAnimationFrame(() => toast.classList.add('show'));

            const timer = setTimeout(removeToast, 3600);
            function removeToast() {
                clearTimeout(timer);
                if (!toast.isConnected) return;
                toast.classList.remove('show');
                toast.classList.add('hide');
                setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 260);
            }
        }


        function safeSessionGet(key) {
            try { const v=sessionStorage.getItem(key); if(v!==null) return v; } catch (e) {}
            try { return localStorage.getItem(key); } catch (e) { return null; }
        }
        function safeSessionSet(key, value) {
            try { sessionStorage.setItem(key, value); return; } catch (e) {}
            try { localStorage.setItem(key, value); } catch (e) {}
        }
        function safeSessionRemove(key) {
            try { sessionStorage.removeItem(key); } catch (e) {}
            try { localStorage.removeItem(key); } catch (e) {}
        }

        function saveBrowserSession() {
            if (!currentUser) return;
            safeSessionSet('ntpcrp_session', JSON.stringify({ account: currentUser.account, loginAt: Date.now(), lastActive: Date.now() }));
        }

        async function restoreBrowserSession() {
            const raw = safeSessionGet('ntpcrp_session');
            if (!raw) return false;
            try {
                const saved = JSON.parse(raw);
                const lastActive = Number(saved.lastActive || saved.loginAt || 0);
                // 保留「10 分鐘內重新整理不登出」：刷新只要仍在 10 分鐘活動期限內即可恢復。
                if (!saved.account || !lastActive || Date.now() - lastActive > 10 * 60 * 1000) {
                    safeSessionRemove('ntpcrp_session');
                    return false;
                }
                const found = (appData.users || []).find(u => String(u.account).toLowerCase() === String(saved.account).toLowerCase());
                if (!found) { safeSessionRemove('ntpcrp_session'); return false; }
                currentUser = found;
                const sessionPayload = { account: currentUser.account, loginAt: Number(saved.loginAt || Date.now()), lastActive: Date.now() };
                safeSessionSet('ntpcrp_session', JSON.stringify(sessionPayload));
                updateUserBadge();
                updateAccessLocks();
                renderMyProfile();
                try { await updateCurrentUserPresence(true); } catch (e) {}
                clearInterval(window.__presenceTimer);
                window.__presenceTimer = setInterval(() => {
                    if (currentUser) {
                        const latest = { account: currentUser.account, loginAt: sessionPayload.loginAt, lastActive: Date.now() };
                        safeSessionSet('ntpcrp_session', JSON.stringify(latest));
                        try { updateCurrentUserPresence(true); } catch (e) {}
                    }
                }, 30000);
                if (currentUser.role === 'admin') startAdminPresenceRefresh();
                if (isAcademyInstructor(currentUser) && getEffectiveRole(currentUser) !== 'admin') {
                    setTimeout(() => switchTab('admin'), 0);
                }
                return true;
            } catch (e) {
                console.warn('瀏覽器登入工作階段恢復失敗：', e);
                safeSessionRemove('ntpcrp_session');
                return false;
            }
        }

        function calculateUserTotalDutyHours(account) {
            const allClocks = [
                ...(appData.policeClocks || []).map(x => ({ ...x, dept: 'police' })),
                ...(appData.fireClocks || []).map(x => ({ ...x, dept: 'fire' }))
            ];
            let totalMinutes = 0;
            ['police', 'fire'].forEach(dept => {
                const clocks = allClocks.filter(c => c.dept === dept && String(c.account || '').toLowerCase() === String(account || '').toLowerCase()).sort((a,b) => Number(a.timestamp || 0) - Number(b.timestamp || 0));
                let onDuty = null;
                clocks.forEach(c => {
                    if (c.status === '上班') onDuty = Number(c.timestamp || 0) || null;
                    else if (c.status === '下班' && onDuty) {
                        const diff = Number(c.timestamp || 0) - onDuty;
                        if (diff > 0) totalMinutes += diff / 60000;
                        onDuty = null;
                    }
                });
                if (onDuty && Date.now() > onDuty) totalMinutes += (Date.now() - onDuty) / 60000;
            });
            return (totalMinutes / 60).toFixed(1);
        }

        function getUserReportCount(account) {
            const key = String(account || '').toLowerCase();
            const matches = list => (list || []).filter(r => {
                const owner = r.registeredByAccount || r.authorAccount || r.authorId;
                return String(owner || '').toLowerCase() === key;
            }).length;
            return matches(appData.policeReports) + matches(appData.fireReports) + matches(appData.emsReports);
        }

        async function removePendingReportNotifications(caseId, recipientAccount) {
            if (!fm || !db || !caseId || !recipientAccount) return;
            const key = String(recipientAccount).trim().toLowerCase();
            const matches = (appData.reportNotifications || []).filter(n =>
                (n.type === 'report' || n.type === 'wanted') && String(n.caseId || '') === String(caseId) &&
                String(n.recipientAccount || '').trim().toLowerCase() === key &&
                String(n.status || '') === 'pending'
            );
            for (const n of matches) {
                try { await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db, 'reportNotifications', n.docId)); } catch (e) {}
            }
            if (matches.length) {
                const ids = new Set(matches.map(n => String(n.docId)));
                appData.reportNotifications = (appData.reportNotifications || []).filter(n => !ids.has(String(n.docId)));
            }
        }

        function renderPersonalNotifications(){
            const host=document.getElementById('notification-tab-list');
            const historyHost=document.getElementById('notification-rejected-history');
            const tabCount=document.getElementById('notification-tab-count');
            const profileHost=document.getElementById('profile-notification-list');
            const profileBadge=document.getElementById('profile-notification-count');
            const navBadge=document.getElementById('nav-notification-badge');
            const mobileBadge=document.getElementById('mobile-nav-notification-badge');
            const bellIcons=[document.getElementById('nav-notification-bell'),document.getElementById('mobile-nav-notification-bell')].filter(Boolean);
            if(!currentUser){
                if(host) host.innerHTML='<div class="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center py-10">登入後即可查看個人案件通知。</div>';
                if(historyHost) historyHost.innerHTML='<div class="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center py-8">登入後即可查看退回歷史案件。</div>';
                if(tabCount) tabCount.textContent='0 則通知';
                if(profileHost) profileHost.innerHTML='<div class="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center py-6">登入後即可查看個人案件通知。</div>';
                if(profileBadge) profileBadge.textContent='0 則';
                if(navBadge){navBadge.classList.add('hidden');navBadge.classList.remove('inline-flex');}
                if(mobileBadge){mobileBadge.classList.add('hidden');mobileBadge.classList.remove('inline-flex');}
                bellIcons.forEach(i=>i.classList.remove('notification-bell-active'));
                return;
            }
            const key=String(currentUser.account||'').toLowerCase();
            const rawNotes=(appData.reportNotifications||[]).filter(n=>String(n.recipientAccount||'').toLowerCase()===key).sort((a,b)=>(b.timestamp||0)-(a.timestamp||0));
            // 同一案件只允許一張案件通知：不論 type/status 是否因舊資料而不同，都只保留最新一筆。
            // 這會同時處理「案件已重新送件」重複兩張、退件通知重複兩張，以及舊審核中通知殘留的情況。
            const seenReportCases=new Set();
            const notes=rawNotes.filter(n=>{
                if(!n.caseId) return true;
                const reportKey=`${String(n.recipientAccount||'').toLowerCase()}:${String(n.caseId)}`;
                if(seenReportCases.has(reportKey)) return false;
                seenReportCases.add(reportKey);
                return true;
            });
            const minePending=[...(appData.policePendingReports||[]).map(r=>({...r,__dept:'police'})), ...(appData.firePendingReports||[]).map(r=>({...r,__dept:'fire'})), ...(appData.emsPendingReports||[]).map(r=>({...r,__dept:'ems',caseId:r.id||r.docId,title:r.title||`EMS救護報告 ${r.id||r.docId}`}))].filter(r=>String(r.registeredByAccount||r.authorId||'').toLowerCase()===key);
            const rejected=minePending.filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='rejected').sort((a,b)=>(b.updatedAt||b.reviewedAt||b.submittedAt||0)-(a.updatedAt||a.reviewedAt||a.submittedAt||0));
            if(currentUser && (appData.wantedPendingRecords||[]).some(r=>r.createdByAccount===currentUser.account && r.reviewStatus==='rejected')){
                (appData.wantedPendingRecords||[]).filter(r=>r.createdByAccount===currentUser.account && r.reviewStatus==='rejected').forEach(r=>rejected.push({...r,__dept:'wanted',caseId:r.id||r.docId,title:`通緝申請｜${r.name||'未命名'}`}));
            }
            const pending=minePending.filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='pending').sort((a,b)=>(b.updatedAt||b.submittedAt||0)-(a.updatedAt||a.submittedAt||0));
            const reviewerPending=[];
            if(canReviewPoliceReports()){
                (appData.policePendingReports||[]).filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='pending').forEach(r=>reviewerPending.push({...r,__dept:'police'}));
                (appData.wantedPendingRecords||[]).filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='pending').forEach(r=>reviewerPending.push({...r,__dept:'wanted',caseId:r.id||r.docId,title:r.title||`通緝申請 ${r.id||r.docId}`}));
            }
            if(canReviewFireReports()){
                (appData.firePendingReports||[]).filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='pending').forEach(r=>reviewerPending.push({...r,__dept:'fire'}));
                (appData.emsPendingReports||[]).filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='pending').forEach(r=>reviewerPending.push({...r,__dept:'ems',caseId:r.id||r.docId,title:r.title||`EMS救護報告 ${r.id||r.docId}`}));
            }
            reviewerPending.sort((a,b)=>(b.submittedAt||b.updatedAt||b.timestamp||0)-(a.submittedAt||a.updatedAt||a.timestamp||0));
            const unread=notes.filter(n=>n.read!==true).length;
            const reviewerUnread=reviewerPending.length;
            const totalMessages=notes.length + pending.length + rejected.length + reviewerUnread;
            if(tabCount) tabCount.textContent=`${totalMessages} 則通知`;
            if(profileBadge) profileBadge.textContent=`${notes.length + reviewerUnread} 則`;
            [navBadge,mobileBadge].forEach(b=>{if(!b)return; const badgeCount=unread+rejected.length+reviewerUnread; b.textContent=String(badgeCount); b.classList.toggle('hidden',badgeCount<=0); b.classList.toggle('inline-flex',badgeCount>0);});
            const hasMessages=totalMessages>0;
            bellIcons.forEach(i=>i.classList.toggle('notification-bell-active',hasMessages));

            const finalizedCaseKeys=new Set(notes.filter(n=>n.type==='report' && n.caseId && (n.status==='approved'||n.status==='rejected')).map(n=>`${n.department||''}:${n.caseId}`));
            const noteCaseKeys=new Set(notes.filter(n=>n.type==='report' && n.caseId && n.status==='pending').map(n=>`${n.department||''}:${n.caseId}`));
            const statusCards=pending.filter(r=>!finalizedCaseKeys.has(`${r.__dept||''}:${r.caseId||''}`) && !noteCaseKeys.has(`${r.__dept||''}:${r.caseId||''}`)).map(r=>`<div class="rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-950/10 p-5"><div class="flex items-start gap-3"><i class="fa-solid fa-hourglass-half text-amber-400 mt-1"></i><div class="flex-1 min-w-0"><div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><span class="font-bold text-white">${r.title||'未命名案件'}</span><span class="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-500">${r.caseId||'--'}</span><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-semibold bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300">審核中</span></div><div class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-2">送件時間：${formatDateTime(r.submittedAt||r.timestamp)}</div></div></div></div>`).join('');
            const reviewerCards=reviewerPending.map(r=>{const isEMS=r.__dept==='ems'; const isWanted=r.__dept==='wanted'; const isFire=r.__dept==='fire'||isEMS; const deptName=isWanted?'通緝':(isEMS?'EMS':(isFire?'消防':'警察')); const icon=isWanted?'fa-handcuffs':(isEMS?'fa-truck-medical':(isFire?'fa-fire-flame-curved':'fa-shield-halved')); const color=isWanted?'rose':(isEMS?'rose':(isFire?'red':'blue')); return `<div class="rounded-2xl border border-${color}-500/35 bg-${color}-950/15 p-5 shadow-lg"><div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start justify-between gap-4"><div class="flex items-start gap-3 min-w-0"><div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-${color}-500/15 border border-${color}-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-${color}-400 shrink-0"><i class="fa-solid ${icon}"></i></div><div class="min-w-0"><div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><span class="font-bold text-white">${r.title||'未命名案件'}</span><span class="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-500">${r.caseId||'--'}</span><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-semibold bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300">${deptName}待審</span></div><div class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">寄件人：${r.authorName||'--'}${r.registeredByAccount?`（${r.registeredByAccount}）`:''}</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">送件時間：${formatDateTime(r.submittedAt||r.timestamp)}</div></div></div><button type="button" onclick="${isWanted?'viewPendingWantedDetail':(isEMS?'viewPendingEMSReportDetail':(isFire?'viewPendingFireReportDetail':'viewPendingPoliceReportDetail'))}('${r.caseId}')" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap">查看並審核</button></div></div>`;}).join('');
            const noteCards=notes.map(n=>{const rejectedNote=n.status==='rejected',approved=n.status==='approved'; const cls=rejectedNote?'border-red-500/40 bg-red-950/25':approved?'border-emerald-500/25 bg-emerald-950/15':'border-amber-500/25 bg-amber-950/10'; const icon=rejectedNote?'fa-circle-xmark text-red-400':approved?'fa-circle-check text-emerald-400':'fa-hourglass-half text-amber-400'; return `<div class="rounded-2xl border ${cls} p-5"><div class="flex items-start gap-3"><i class="fa-solid ${icon} mt-1"></i><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><span class="font-bold text-white">${n.title||'案件通知'}</span><span class="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-500">${n.caseId||''}</span><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-semibold ${rejectedNote?'bg-red-500/15 text-red-300':approved?'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300':'bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300'}">${rejectedNote?'退件':approved?'審核完成':'案件通知'}</span></div><p class="text-sm text-slate-300 mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap">${n.message||''}</p><div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 mt-2"><span class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">${formatDateTime(n.timestamp)}</span><button type="button" onclick="removePersonalNotification('${n.docId}')" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-red-600/20 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-300 text-[11px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash mr-1"></i>移除通知</button></div></div></div></div>`;}).join('');
            if(host) host.innerHTML=(reviewerCards+statusCards+noteCards)||'<div class="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center py-10">目前沒有案件審核通知。</div>';

            const historyCards=rejected.map(r=>{ const editAction=r.__dept==='wanted'?`openLookupPost('wanted','${r.caseId}')`:r.__dept==='ems'?`openEMSReportModal('${r.caseId}')`:`openEditReportModal('${r.__dept}-pending-report','${r.caseId}')`; const removeAction=r.__dept==='wanted'?`deleteLookupPendingRecord('wanted','${r.caseId}')`:r.__dept==='ems'?`removeRejectedReport('ems','${r.caseId}')`:`removeRejectedReport('${r.__dept}','${r.caseId}')`; return `<div class="rounded-2xl border-2 border-red-500/40 bg-red-950/35 p-5 shadow-lg shadow-red-950/20"><div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start justify-between gap-3"><div class="flex items-start gap-3"><div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-red-500/15 border border-red-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-red-400 shrink-0"><i class="fa-solid fa-rotate-left"></i></div><div><div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><h4 class="font-bold text-white">${r.title||'未命名案件'}</h4><span class="text-[11px] font-mono text-red-300">${r.caseId||'--'}</span><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-red-500/20 text-red-300">已退件</span></div><div class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">退件時間：${formatDateTime(r.reviewedAt||r.updatedAt)}</div></div></div><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2"><button type="button" onclick="${editAction}" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-red-600/20 hover:bg-red-600/30 text-red-200 text-xs font-semibold min-h-[42px]">重新編輯送件</button><button type="button" onclick="${removeAction}" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-red-600/20 text-slate-300 hover:text-red-300 text-xs font-semibold min-h-[42px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash mr-1"></i>移除案件</button></div></div><div class="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-red-950/50 border border-red-500/20 p-4"><div class="text-[11px] text-red-300 font-semibold mb-1">退件原因</div><div class="text-sm text-red-100 whitespace-pre-wrap">${r.rejectionReason||'未提供退件原因'}</div></div><div class="mt-4 text-sm text-slate-300 line-clamp-3 whitespace-pre-wrap">${r.content||''}</div></div>`; }).join('');
            if(historyHost) historyHost.innerHTML=historyCards||'<div class="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center py-8">目前沒有被退回的歷史案件。</div>';

            if(profileHost){
                // 個人資訊頁使用與「個人通知」相同的去重後資料，直接建立卡片陣列，避免
                // 在 DOM 尚未完成切換或舊資料異常時誤判成「沒有通知」。
                const profileItems=[];
                rejected.slice(0,3).forEach(r=>{
                    profileItems.push(`<div class="rounded-2xl border border-red-500/40 bg-red-950/25 p-4"><div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><div class="font-bold text-red-200">案件 ${r.caseId||'--'} 已退件</div><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-semibold bg-red-500/15 text-red-300">退件</span></div><div class="text-xs text-red-300 mt-1">${r.rejectionReason||'未提供退件原因'}</div><div class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-2">${formatDateTime(r.reviewedAt||r.updatedAt||r.submittedAt)}</div></div>`);
                });
                notes.slice(0,5).forEach(n=>{
                    const rejectedNote=n.status==='rejected', approved=n.status==='approved';
                    const cls=rejectedNote?'border-red-500/40 bg-red-950/25':approved?'border-emerald-500/25 bg-emerald-950/15':'border-amber-500/25 bg-amber-950/10';
                    const badge=rejectedNote?'bg-red-500/15 text-red-300':approved?'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300':'bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300';
                    const label=rejectedNote?'退件':approved?'審核完成':'審核中';
                    profileItems.push(`<div class="rounded-2xl border ${cls} p-4"><div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><div class="font-bold text-slate-200">${n.title||'案件通知'}</div>${n.caseId?`<span class="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-500">${n.caseId}</span>`:''}<span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-semibold ${badge}">${label}</span></div><p class="text-sm text-slate-300 mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap">${n.message||'案件狀態已更新。'}</p><span class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">${formatDateTime(n.timestamp)}</span></div>`);
                });
                profileHost.innerHTML=profileItems.length?profileItems.join(''):'<div class="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center py-6">目前沒有案件審核通知。</div>';
            }
        }

        async function removePersonalNotification(docId){
            if(!currentUser || !docId){return;}
            const n=(appData.reportNotifications||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId)===String(docId));
            if(!n || String(n.recipientAccount||'').toLowerCase()!==String(currentUser.account||'').toLowerCase()){showToast('只能移除自己的通知。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            if(!(await showConfirm('確定要移除這則個人通知嗎？','移除通知','確定移除'))) return;
            try{ await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'reportNotifications',docId)); appData.reportNotifications=appData.reportNotifications.filter(x=>String(x.docId)!==String(docId)); renderPersonalNotifications(); showToast('通知已移除。','success','移除成功'); }catch(e){showToast('移除通知失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}
        }

        async function deleteAllPersonalNotifications(){
            if(!currentUser || !currentUser.account){showToast('請先登入系統。','warning','需要登入');return;}
            const account=String(currentUser.account).trim().toLowerCase();
            const localNotes=(appData.reportNotifications||[]).filter(n=>String(n.recipientAccount||'').trim().toLowerCase()===account);
            if(!localNotes.length){showToast('目前沒有可刪除的個人通知。','info','沒有通知');return;}
            if(!(await showConfirm(`確定要一鍵刪除目前帳號的全部 ${localNotes.length} 則個人通知嗎？\n\n此操作只會刪除通知，不會刪除案件資料或退回歷史案件。`,'一鍵刪除所有通知','全部刪除'))) return;
            try{
                let deleted=0;
                for(const n of localNotes){
                    if(!n.docId) continue;
                    await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'reportNotifications',n.docId));
                    deleted++;
                }
                appData.reportNotifications=(appData.reportNotifications||[]).filter(n=>String(n.recipientAccount||'').trim().toLowerCase()!==account);
                renderPersonalNotifications();
                showToast(`已刪除 ${deleted} 則個人通知。`,'success','刪除完成');
            }catch(e){
                showToast('一鍵刪除通知失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');
                try{ await loadUserData(); renderPersonalNotifications(); }catch(_){}
            }
        }

        async function removeRejectedReport(dept,caseId){
            if(!currentUser){showToast('請先登入系統。','warning','需要登入');return;}
            const isEMS=dept==='ems';
            const list=isEMS?appData.emsPendingReports:(dept==='police'?appData.policePendingReports:appData.firePendingReports);
            const r=(list||[]).find(x=>String(x.caseId||x.id||x.docId)===String(caseId));
            if(!r || r.reviewStatus!=='rejected' || String(r.registeredByAccount||r.authorId||'').toLowerCase()!==String(currentUser.account||'').toLowerCase()){showToast('只能移除自己被退件的案件。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            if(!(await showConfirm(`確定要移除退件案件「${caseId}」嗎？移除後將無法再重新送件。`,'移除退件案件','確定移除'))) return;
            try{const col=isEMS?'emsPendingReports':(dept==='police'?'policePendingReports':'firePendingReports');const removeDocId=await resolveReportDocId(col,r,caseId);if(!removeDocId)throw new Error('找不到退件案件的 Firestore 文件 ID');await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,col,removeDocId));if(isEMS)appData.emsPendingReports=appData.emsPendingReports.filter(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)!==String(caseId));else if(dept==='police')appData.policePendingReports=appData.policePendingReports.filter(x=>x.caseId!==caseId);else appData.firePendingReports=appData.firePendingReports.filter(x=>x.caseId!==caseId);renderPersonalNotifications();showToast('退件案件已移除。','success','移除成功');}catch(e){showToast('退件案件移除失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}
        }

        function renderMyProfile() {
            const lock = document.getElementById('profile-login-lock');
            const content = document.getElementById('profile-content');
            if (!lock || !content) return;
            if (!currentUser) {
                lock.classList.remove('hidden');
                content.classList.add('hidden');
                return;
            }
            lock.classList.add('hidden');
            content.classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('profile-account').textContent = currentUser.account || '--';
            document.getElementById('profile-staff-number').textContent = currentUser.staffNumber || currentUser.account || '--';
            document.getElementById('profile-units').textContent = getUnitDisplayNames(currentUser);
            document.getElementById('profile-total-hours').textContent = `${calculateUserTotalDutyHours(currentUser.account)} 小時`;
            document.getElementById('profile-total-reports').textContent = getUserReportCount(currentUser.account);
            renderPersonalNotifications();
        }

        function academyChecksum(type, expiresAt, randomPart) {
            const source = `${type}|${expiresAt}|${randomPart}|NTPCRP-ACADEMY`;
            let hash = 2166136261;
            for (let i = 0; i < source.length; i++) {
                hash ^= source.charCodeAt(i);
                hash = Math.imul(hash, 16777619);
            }
            return (hash >>> 0).toString(36).toUpperCase().slice(-5).padStart(5, '0');
        }

        function parseAcademyCode(rawCode) {
            const code = String(rawCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
            const match = code.match(/^NTP-(P|F)-([0-9A-Z]+)-([0-9A-Z]{6})-([0-9A-Z]{5})$/);
            if (!match) return null;
            const type = match[1] === 'P' ? 'police' : 'fire';
            const expiresAt = parseInt(match[2], 36);
            const randomPart = match[3];
            const checksum = match[4];
            if (!expiresAt || expiresAt <= Date.now()) return null;
            if (academyChecksum(type, expiresAt, randomPart) !== checksum) return null;
            return { code, type, expiresAt };
        }

        async function loadAcademyCodes() {
            let cloudRows = Array.isArray(appData.academyCodes) ? appData.academyCodes.filter(x => x && x.code) : [];
            try {
                if (fm && db) {
                    const snap = await fm.getDocs(fm.collection(db, 'academyCodes'));
                    cloudRows = snap.docs.map(d => ({docId:d.id, ...d.data()})).filter(x => x && x.code);
                }
            } catch (e) {}
            let localRows = [];
            try { localRows = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ntpcrp_academy_codes') || '[]'); } catch (e) { localRows = []; }
            const merged = [...cloudRows];
            localRows.forEach(row => { if(row && row.code && !merged.some(x=>x.code===row.code)) merged.push(row); });
            appData.academyCodes = merged;
            if (fm && db && isManagementUser()) {
                for (const row of localRows) {
                    if (!row?.code || cloudRows.some(x=>x.code===row.code)) continue;
                    try { await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'academyCodes',row.code), row); } catch(e) {}
                }
            }
            return merged.filter(x => x && x.code && Number(x.expiresAt) > 0 || x && x.code);
        }

        function saveAcademyCodes(rows) {
            appData.academyCodes = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
            try { localStorage.setItem('ntpcrp_academy_codes', JSON.stringify(appData.academyCodes.slice(-30))); } catch (e) {}
        }

        function isManagementUser() { return !!currentUser && ['admin','high','senior'].includes(currentUser.role); }

        async function removeAcademyCode(code, silent = false) {
            if (!isManagementUser()) { showToast('只有管理層可以刪除考核代碼。','warning','權限不足'); return; }
            const row = (appData.academyCodes || []).find(x => x.code === code);
            try {
                if (fm && db) await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db, 'academyCodes', code));
                appData.academyCodes = (appData.academyCodes || []).filter(x => x.code !== code);
                try { localStorage.setItem('ntpcrp_academy_codes', JSON.stringify(appData.academyCodes.slice(-30))); } catch(e) {}
                if (academyExamSession?.code === code) logoutAcademySession(true);
                renderAdminAcademyCodes();
                renderManagementAcademyCodes();
                if (!silent) showSubmitSuccessAnimation('代碼已移除', '專生考核代碼已成功刪除。');
            } catch (e) { showToast('刪除考核代碼失敗：' + e.message, 'error', '操作失敗'); }
        }

        async function generateAcademyCode(type) {
            if (!currentUser || currentUser.role !== 'admin') {
                showToast('只有最高管理員可以生成專生考核代碼。', 'warning', '權限不足');
                return;
            }
            const now = Date.now();
            const rows = (await loadAcademyCodes()).filter(x => Number(x.expiresAt) > now);
            const activeCount = rows.filter(x => x.type === type).length;
            if (activeCount >= 5) {
                showToast(`${type === 'police' ? '警務' : '消防'}考核代碼目前已達 5 個上限，請先刪除或等待代碼過期。`, 'warning', '已達生成上限');
                return;
            }
            const expiresAt = now + 30 * 60 * 1000;
            const randomPart = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8).toUpperCase().padEnd(6, 'X');
            const code = `NTP-${type === 'police' ? 'P' : 'F'}-${expiresAt.toString(36).toUpperCase()}-${randomPart}-${academyChecksum(type, expiresAt, randomPart)}`;
            const obj = { code, type, expiresAt, createdAt: now, creator: currentUser.account };
            try {
                if (fm && db) await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db, 'academyCodes', code), obj);
                appData.academyCodes = [...(appData.academyCodes || []).filter(x => x.code !== code), {docId:code, ...obj}];
                saveAcademyCodes(appData.academyCodes);
                renderAdminAcademyCodes();
                renderManagementAcademyCodes();
                try { navigator.clipboard?.writeText(code); } catch (e) {}
                showToast(`${type === 'police' ? '警務' : '消防'}考核代碼已生成：${code}`, 'success', '考核代碼生成成功');
            } catch (e) { showToast('考核代碼生成失敗：' + e.message, 'error', '操作失敗'); }
        }

        function renderAcademyCodeCards(host, rows, management = false) {
            if (!host) return;
            if (!rows.length) { host.innerHTML = '<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-8 text-center text-slate-500">目前沒有可瀏覽的考核代碼。</div>'; return; }
            host.innerHTML = rows.map(row => {
                const remain = Math.max(0, Number(row.expiresAt) - Date.now());
                const mins = Math.floor(remain / 60000), secs = Math.floor((remain % 60000) / 1000), expired = remain <= 0;
                return `<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"><span class="text-xs font-bold ${row.type === 'police' ? 'text-blue-400' : 'text-red-400'}">${row.type === 'police' ? '警務考核' : '消防考核'}</span><span class="text-[11px] ${expired ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-emerald-400'}">${expired ? '已過期' : `剩餘 ${mins}分 ${String(secs).padStart(2,'0')}秒`}</span></div>
                    <div class="mt-3 font-mono text-sm text-white break-all">${escapeHtml(row.code||'--')}</div>
                    <div class="mt-2 text-[11px] text-slate-500">生成：${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(row.createdAt))}　有效至：${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(row.expiresAt))}　生成者：${escapeHtml(row.creator || '--')}</div>
                    ${management ? `<button type="button" onclick="removeAcademyCode('${row.code}')" class="mt-3 w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-red-600/10 hover:bg-red-600/20 border border-red-500/20 text-red-400 text-xs"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash mr-1"></i>刪除考核代碼</button>` : ''}
                </div>`;
            }).join('');
        }

        async function renderAdminAcademyCodes() {
            const host = document.getElementById('admin-academy-code-list');
            if (!host) return;
            const rows = (await loadAcademyCodes()).sort((a,b) => Number(b.createdAt || 0) - Number(a.createdAt || 0));
            renderAcademyCodeCards(host, rows, true);
        }

        async function openAcademyCodeManagement() {
            if (!isManagementUser()) { showToast('只有管理層可以查看考核代碼。','warning','權限不足'); return; }
            const modal = document.getElementById('modal-academy-code-management');
            if (modal) modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            await renderManagementAcademyCodes();
        }

        async function renderManagementAcademyCodes() {
            const host = document.getElementById('management-academy-code-list');
            if (!host) return;
            const rows = (await loadAcademyCodes()).sort((a,b) => Number(b.createdAt || 0) - Number(a.createdAt || 0));
            renderAcademyCodeCards(host, rows, isManagementUser());
        }

        function generateAcademyCaseId(dept) {
            const prefix = dept === 'police' ? 'SIM-P' : 'SIM-F';
            const date = new Date();
            const key = `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
            const list = dept === 'police' ? academyExamCache.policeReports : academyExamCache.fireReports;
            return `${prefix}-${key}-${String(list.length + 1).padStart(3,'0')}`;
        }

        function saveAcademyCache() {
