            try { sessionStorage.setItem('ntpcrp_academy_cache', JSON.stringify(academyExamCache)); } catch (e) {}
        }

        function restoreAcademyCache() {
            try {
                const raw = sessionStorage.getItem('ntpcrp_academy_cache');
                if (raw) {
                    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
                    if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') academyExamCache = {
                        policeReports: Array.isArray(parsed.policeReports) ? parsed.policeReports : [],
                        fireReports: Array.isArray(parsed.fireReports) ? parsed.fireReports : [],
                        emsReports: Array.isArray(parsed.emsReports) ? parsed.emsReports : []
                    };
                }
            } catch (e) {}
        }

        function saveAcademySession() {
            if (academyExamSession) safeSessionSet('ntpcrp_academy_session', JSON.stringify(academyExamSession));
        }

        function restoreAcademySession() {
            restoreAcademyCache();
            const raw = safeSessionGet('ntpcrp_academy_session');
            if (!raw) return;
            try {
                const saved = JSON.parse(raw);
                const parsed = parseAcademyCode(saved.code);
                if (!parsed || parsed.type !== saved.type) {
                    safeSessionRemove('ntpcrp_academy_session');
                    return;
                }
                academyExamSession = parsed;
                renderAcademySession();
                renderAcademyCache();
            } catch (e) {
                safeSessionRemove('ntpcrp_academy_session');
            }
        }

        function renderAcademySession() {
            const lock = document.getElementById('academy-lock-overlay');
            const content = document.getElementById('academy-content');
            if (!lock || !content) return;
            const managementVisible=!isManagementUser(); const manageBtn=document.getElementById('academy-code-manage-btn'); if(manageBtn) manageBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', managementVisible); const manageBtn2=document.getElementById('academy-code-manage-btn-content'); if(manageBtn2) manageBtn2.classList.toggle('hidden', managementVisible);
            if (!academyExamSession || Number(academyExamSession.expiresAt) <= Date.now()) {
                if (academyExamSession) logoutAcademySession(true);
                lock.classList.remove('hidden');
                content.classList.add('hidden');
                return;
            }
            lock.classList.add('hidden');
            content.classList.remove('hidden');
            const isPolice = academyExamSession.type === 'police';
            document.getElementById('academy-type-badge').textContent = isPolice ? '警務考核' : '消防考核';
            document.getElementById('academy-title').textContent = isPolice ? '警專警務案件考核' : '警專消防救護案件考核';
            document.getElementById('academy-police-report-btn').classList.toggle('hidden', !isPolice);
            document.getElementById('academy-fire-report-btn').classList.toggle('hidden', isPolice);
            document.getElementById('academy-ems-report-btn').classList.toggle('hidden', isPolice);
            const remain = Math.max(0, academyExamSession.expiresAt - Date.now());
            const mins = Math.floor(remain / 60000), secs = Math.floor((remain % 60000) / 1000);
            document.getElementById('academy-expiry').textContent = `代碼剩餘：${mins}分 ${String(secs).padStart(2,'0')}秒`;
        }

        async function handleAcademyCodeLogin() {
            await cloudDataReadyPromise;
            const input = document.getElementById('academy-code-input');
            const status = document.getElementById('academy-code-status');
            const rawCode = String(input?.value || '').trim().toUpperCase();
            const parsed = parseAcademyCode(rawCode);
            const cloudRow = (appData.academyCodes || []).find(x => String(x.code || '').toUpperCase() === rawCode);
            if (!parsed || !cloudRow || Number(cloudRow.expiresAt) <= Date.now()) {
                if (status) status.textContent = '考核代碼無效、格式錯誤或已過期。';
                showToast('考核代碼無效或已過期。', 'error', '登入失敗');
                return;
            }
            academyExamSession = parsed;
            academyExamCache = { policeReports: [], fireReports: [], emsReports: [] };
            saveAcademySession();
            saveAcademyCache();
            if (input) input.value = '';
            if (status) status.textContent = '';
            renderAcademySession();
            renderAcademyCache();
            showToast(`已進入${parsed.type === 'police' ? '警務' : '消防'}專生考核。`, 'success', '考核登入成功');
        }

        function logoutAcademySession(silent = false) {
            academyExamSession = null;
            academyExamCache = { policeReports: [], fireReports: [], emsReports: [] };
            safeSessionRemove('ntpcrp_academy_session');
            safeSessionRemove('ntpcrp_academy_cache');
            renderAcademySession();
            renderAcademyCache();
            if (!silent) showToast('已結束本次專生考核。', 'success', '考核結束');
        }

        function renderAcademyCache() {
            const host = document.getElementById('academy-cache-list');
            if (!host) return;
            if (!academyExamSession) { host.innerHTML = ''; return; }
            const rows = [
                ...(academyExamCache.policeReports || []).map(r => ({ type:'警務', id:r.caseId, title:r.title, content:r.content, time:r.time })),
                ...(academyExamCache.fireReports || []).map(r => ({ type:'消防', id:r.caseId, title:r.title, content:r.content, time:r.time })),
                ...(academyExamCache.emsReports || []).map(r => ({ type:'EMS', id:r.id, title:`${r.patient || '傷患'} EMS`, content:r.treatment, time:r.time }))
            ];
            host.innerHTML = rows.length ? rows.map(r => `<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-xl">
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"><span class="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-400">${escapeHtml(r.type||'--')}</span><span class="text-xs text-slate-500">${escapeHtml(r.time || '--')}</span></div>
                <div class="mt-2 font-mono text-sm text-slate-300">${escapeHtml(r.id || '--')}</div>
                <h4 class="mt-2 font-bold text-white">${escapeHtml(r.title || '未命名考核案件')}</h4>
                <p class="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400 whitespace-pre-wrap line-clamp-3">${escapeHtml(r.content || '--')}</p>
                <button type="button" onclick="viewAcademyReportDetail('${r.type}','${r.id}')" class="mt-4 px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-500/10 hover:bg-indigo-500/20 border border-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-300 rounded-xl text-xs">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-up-right-and-down-left-from-center mr-1"></i>檢視完整報告
                </button>
            </div>`).join('') : '<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-8 text-center text-slate-500">目前尚無考核案件，請使用上方模板開始模擬。</div>';
        }

        function viewAcademyReportDetail(type, id) {
            let r = null;
            if (type === 'EMS') r = (academyExamCache.emsReports || []).find(x => String(x.id) === String(id));
            else {
                const list = type === '警務' ? academyExamCache.policeReports : academyExamCache.fireReports;
                r = (list || []).find(x => String(x.caseId) === String(id));
            }
            if (!r) { showToast('找不到考核案件資料。', 'error', '資料不存在'); return; }
            if (type === 'EMS') {
                document.getElementById('ems-detail-id').textContent = r.id || id;
                document.getElementById('ems-detail-title').textContent = `${r.patient || '傷患'} 的EMS救護出勤報告`;
                const fields=[['主訴',r.chiefComplaint],['派遣類別',r.dispatchCategory],['傷患 RP 名稱 / ID',r.patient],['出勤單位 / 車輛',r.unitVehicle],['GCS評分 E',r.gcsE],['派遣時間',r.dispatchTime],['抵達時間',r.arrivalTime],['離開時間',r.leaveTime],['到院時間',r.hospitalTime],['返隊時間',r.returnTime],['填寫人',`${r.authorName||'專生考生'} (${r.authorId||'警專專生'})`],['建立時間',r.time||formatDateTime(r.timestamp)]];
                document.getElementById('ems-detail-fields').innerHTML=fields.map(([k,v])=>`<div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3"><span class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 block">${escapeHtml(k)}</span><span class="text-sm text-slate-200 break-words">${escapeHtml(v||'--')}</span></div>`).join('');
                document.getElementById('ems-detail-treatment').textContent=r.treatment||'--';
                const action=document.getElementById('ems-detail-action-bar'); if(action) action.innerHTML='';
                document.getElementById('modal-ems-detail').classList.remove('hidden');
                return;
            }
            const dept = type === '警務' ? 'police' : 'fire';
            document.getElementById('detail-case-badge').textContent=r.caseId||id;
            document.getElementById('detail-title').textContent=r.title||'未命名案件';
            document.getElementById('detail-author').textContent=`${r.authorName||'--'} (${r.authorId||'--'})`;
            document.getElementById('detail-registered-account').textContent=r.registeredByAccount||'警專專生';
            document.getElementById('detail-suspect-name').textContent=r.suspectName||'--';
            document.getElementById('detail-suspect-id').textContent=r.suspectId||'--';
            document.getElementById('detail-suspect-vehicle').textContent=r.suspectVehicle||'--';
            document.getElementById('detail-suspect-name-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden',dept!=='police');
            document.getElementById('detail-suspect-id-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden',dept!=='police');
            document.getElementById('detail-suspect-vehicle-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden',dept!=='police');
            const extra=document.getElementById('detail-police-extra-wrap'); if(extra) extra.classList.toggle('hidden',dept!=='police');
            if(dept==='police'){document.getElementById('detail-postal-code').textContent=r.postalCode||'未填';document.getElementById('detail-command').textContent=r.command||'未填';document.getElementById('detail-support-units').textContent=(r.supportUnits||[]).join('、')||'無';}
            const jailSummary=document.getElementById('detail-jail-summary');if(jailSummary){const hasJail=dept==='police';jailSummary.classList.toggle('hidden',!hasJail);jailSummary.innerHTML=hasJail?`<div class="text-xs text-slate-400">違反條例</div><div class="text-sm text-slate-200 mt-1">${(r.violations||[]).join('、')||'未選擇'}</div><div class="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-300 mt-2">總監獄時間：${formatJailMinutes(r.jailMinutes)}${r.organizedCrime?'（組織犯罪加重⅓）':''}</div>`:'';}
            document.getElementById('detail-time').textContent=r.time||formatDateTime(r.timestamp);
            document.getElementById('detail-content').textContent=r.content||'';
            document.getElementById('detail-remark').innerHTML='專生考核案件：本報告僅保留於本次考核瀏覽器快取，未寫入正式案件資料庫。';
            const links=getReportVideoLinks(r),vidWrap=document.getElementById('detail-video-wrapper'),vidBox=document.getElementById('detail-video-links');
            const safeLinks=links.map(safeHttpUrl).filter(Boolean); if(safeLinks.length){vidWrap.classList.remove('hidden');vidBox.innerHTML=safeLinks.map((url,i)=>`<a href="${escapeHtml(url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 hover:underline"><i class="fa-solid fa-video"></i><span>開啟影片 ${i+1}</span></a>`).join('');} else {vidWrap.classList.add('hidden');vidBox.innerHTML='';}
            const actionBar=document.getElementById('detail-action-bar'); if(actionBar) actionBar.innerHTML='';
            document.getElementById('modal-report-detail').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function showSubmitSuccessAnimation(title='送出成功', message='案件資料已成功送出。') {
            const overlay = document.getElementById('submit-success-animation');
            if (!overlay) return;
            const titleEl = document.getElementById('submit-success-title');
            const messageEl = document.getElementById('submit-success-message');
            if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = title;
            if (messageEl) messageEl.textContent = message;
            clearTimeout(window.__submitSuccessTimer);
            overlay.classList.remove('show');
            // 不使用 offsetWidth 強制同步 reflow，改用下一幀重啟動畫，降低手機掉幀。
            requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                requestAnimationFrame(() => overlay.classList.add('show'));
            });
            window.__submitSuccessTimer = setTimeout(() => overlay.classList.remove('show'), 1450);
        }

        const SECURITY_ACTION_COOLDOWN=new Map();
        function securityActionAllowed(key,cooldownMs=700){const now=Date.now(),last=SECURITY_ACTION_COOLDOWN.get(key)||0;if(now-last<cooldownMs)return false;SECURITY_ACTION_COOLDOWN.set(key,now);return true;}
        async function handleLogin(){if(!securityActionAllowed('login',900))return;await cloudDataReadyPromise;const device=getDeviceInfo();if(isCurrentDeviceBlocked()){showToast('因異常行為 預防性封鎖 請洽政府信箱','error','裝置已被封鎖');return;}const acc=document.getElementById('login-username').value.trim(),pass=document.getElementById('login-password').value.trim();if(!acc||!pass){showToast('請完整輸入帳號與密碼！','error','資料不完整');return;}const found=appData.users.find(u=>isSuperAdminAccount(acc)?isSuperAdminAccount(u.account)&&u.pass===pass:u.account===acc&&u.pass===pass);if(!found){const autoBlocked=await registerLoginFailure(device);if(autoBlocked){showToast('因異常行為 預防性封鎖 請洽政府信箱','error','裝置已被封鎖');}else{showToast('帳號或密碼錯誤，請重新確認後再試。','error','登入失敗');}return;}await clearLoginFailureRecord(getDeviceKey(device));currentUser=found;saveBrowserSession();closeModal('modal-login');updateUserBadge();updateAccessLocks();renderMyProfile();await recordLoginSession(currentUser);await pruneOldLoginSessions();if(currentUser.role==='admin')startAdminPresenceRefresh();clearInterval(window.__presenceTimer);window.__presenceTimer=setInterval(()=>updateCurrentUserPresence(true),30000);showToast(`歡迎回來，${currentUser.name} (${currentUser.account})！`,'success','登入成功');startRealtimeListeners();if(isAcademyInstructor(currentUser)&&getEffectiveRole(currentUser)!=='admin')switchTab('academy');else switchTab('home');}
        async function logout(){await updateCurrentUserPresence(false);clearInterval(window.__presenceTimer);window.__presenceTimer=null;clearInterval(window.__profileSessionTimer);window.__profileSessionTimer=null;currentUser=null;stopAdminPresenceRefresh();stopRealtimeListeners();sessionStorage.removeItem('ntpcrp_session');updateUserBadge();updateAccessLocks();showToast('已成功登出系統。','success','已登出');switchTab('home');}

        function updateUserBadge() {
            const badge = document.getElementById('user-badge');
            const loginBtn = document.getElementById('login-modal-open-btn');
            if (!badge || !loginBtn) return;

            if (currentUser) {
                loginBtn.classList.add('hidden');
                badge.classList.remove('hidden');
                badge.classList.add('flex');
                document.getElementById('user-badge-name').textContent = `${currentUser.name} (${currentUser.account})`;
                const dot = document.getElementById('user-badge-role');
                if (currentUser.role === 'admin') dot.className = "w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-purple-500";
                else if (currentUser.role === 'high') dot.className = "w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-amber-500";
                else if (currentUser.role === 'senior') dot.className = "w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-cyan-500";
                else dot.className = "w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500";
            } else {
                loginBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
                badge.classList.add('hidden');
                badge.classList.remove('flex');
            }
        }

        function updateAccessLocks() {
            const policeLock = document.getElementById('police-lock-overlay');
            const policeContent = document.getElementById('police-content-container');
            const fireLock = document.getElementById('fire-lock-overlay');
            const fireContent = document.getElementById('fire-content-container');
            const adminLock = document.getElementById('admin-lock-overlay');
            const adminContent = document.getElementById('admin-content-container');
            const punishmentPublishBtn = document.getElementById('police-punish-modal-btn');
            const punishmentTabBtn = document.getElementById('btn-police-punishments');
            const policeReviewBtn = document.getElementById('btn-police-review');
            const fireReviewBtn = document.getElementById('btn-fire-review');
            const adminNav = document.getElementById('nav-admin');
            const adminMobileNav = document.getElementById('mobile-nav-admin');

            let isPolice = false;
            let isFire = false;
            if (currentUser) {
                if (currentUser.role === 'admin') {
                    isPolice = true;
                    isFire = true;
                } else {
                    const units = currentUser.units || [currentUser.unit];
                    isPolice = units.includes('police');
                    isFire = units.includes('fire');
                }
            }

            if (policeLock && policeContent) {
                policeLock.classList.toggle('hidden', isPolice);
                policeContent.classList.toggle('hidden', !isPolice);
            }
            if (fireLock && fireContent) {
                fireLock.classList.toggle('hidden', isFire);
                fireContent.classList.toggle('hidden', !isFire);
            }

            const isAdmin = !!currentUser && currentUser.role === 'admin';
            const canManage = canAccessManagementCenter();
            if (adminLock && adminContent) {
                adminLock.classList.toggle('hidden', canManage);
                adminContent.classList.toggle('hidden', !canManage);
                const lockTitle = adminLock.querySelector('h3');
                const lockMsg = adminLock.querySelector('p');
                if (lockTitle) lockTitle.textContent = '管理中心權限受限';
                if (lockMsg) lockMsg.textContent = '需要管理中心權限；警專教官僅可使用「專生考核系統」分頁。';
            }
            if (adminNav) adminNav.classList.toggle('hidden', !canManage);
            if (adminMobileNav) adminMobileNav.classList.toggle('hidden', !canManage);
            const createUserBtn = document.getElementById('open-create-user-modal-btn');
            if (createUserBtn) createUserBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !isAdmin);

            if (punishmentPublishBtn) punishmentPublishBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !canUsePolicePunishment());
            renderLookupSystems();
            // 所有已進入警務系統的員警都可以預覽獎懲；發布按鈕另行限制。
            if (punishmentTabBtn) punishmentTabBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
            const canReviewPolice = !!currentUser && (currentUser.role === 'admin' || ((currentUser.role === 'high' || currentUser.role === 'senior') && userHasUnit('police')));
            const canReviewFire = !!currentUser && (currentUser.role === 'admin' || ((currentUser.role === 'high' || currentUser.role === 'senior') && userHasUnit('fire')));
            if (policeReviewBtn) policeReviewBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !canReviewPolice);
            if (fireReviewBtn) fireReviewBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !canReviewFire);
            updateReviewTabBadges();
            if (!canReviewPolice && document.getElementById('police-sub-review') && !document.getElementById('police-sub-review').classList.contains('hidden')) switchPoliceSubTab('reports');
            if (!canReviewFire && document.getElementById('fire-sub-review') && !document.getElementById('fire-sub-review').classList.contains('hidden')) switchFireSubTab('reports');
        }

        function escapeHtml(value){return String(value??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));}
        function safeHttpUrl(value){try{const u=new URL(String(value||''),window.location.href);return (u.protocol==='https:'||u.protocol==='http:')?u.href:'';}catch(e){return '';}}
        function openClockModal(dept, isRetro) {
            if (!currentUser) {
                showToast('請先登入系統後再進行打卡。', 'warning', '需要登入');
                document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.remove('hidden');
                return;
            }

            if (isSuspendedUser(currentUser.account)) {
                showToast('你目前於停職調查階段，無法使用值班打卡功能，直到該獎懲被更改或移除。', 'warning', '停職調查限制');
                return;
            }

            document.getElementById('clock-dept-type').value = dept;
            document.getElementById('clock-is-retro').value = isRetro ? 'true' : 'false';
            document.getElementById('clock-id').value = currentUser.account;
            document.getElementById('clock-name').value = currentUser.name;
            document.getElementById('clock-id').readOnly = true;
            document.getElementById('clock-name').readOnly = true;

            // 自動判斷下一次打卡：最後一次為上班 → 預設下班；最後一次為下班／尚無紀錄 → 預設上班。
            const clockList = dept === 'police' ? (appData.policeClocks || []) : (appData.fireClocks || []);
            const myLatestClock = [...clockList]
                .filter(c => String(c.account || '').trim() === String(currentUser.account || '').trim())
                .sort((a,b) => Number(b.timestamp || 0) - Number(a.timestamp || 0))[0];
            const statusEl = document.getElementById('clock-status');
            if (statusEl) statusEl.value = myLatestClock?.status === '上班' ? '下班' : '上班';
            // 每次開啟打卡視窗都依目前最新紀錄重新決定預設狀態：上班後下一張固定預設下班。
            if (statusEl) statusEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', {bubbles:true}));

            const dateWrapper = document.getElementById('clock-date-field-wrapper');
            const titleEl = document.getElementById('clock-modal-title');

            if (isRetro) {
                if (dateWrapper) dateWrapper.classList.remove('hidden');
                if (titleEl) titleEl.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-clock-rotate-left text-indigo-500"></i><span>補打卡登記 (自訂日期與時分)</span>`;
                const now = new Date();
                const yyyy = now.getFullYear();
                const mm = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
                const dd = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
                const hh = String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
                const min = String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
                document.getElementById('clock-retro-date').value = `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}`;
                document.getElementById('clock-retro-time').value = `${hh}:${min}`;
            } else {
                if (dateWrapper) dateWrapper.classList.add('hidden');
                if (titleEl) titleEl.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-clock text-blue-500"></i><span>${dept === 'police' ? '員警' : '消防隊員'}上下班打卡</span>`;
            }

            document.getElementById('modal-clock').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        const REWARD_ITEMS = ['嘉獎x1','嘉獎x2','嘉獎x3','嘉獎x4','嘉獎x5','小功x1','小功x2','小功x3','小功x4','小功x5','大功x1','大功x2','大功x3','大功x4','大功x5'];
        const PUNISHMENT_ITEMS = ['警告x1','警告x2','警告x3','警告x4','警告x5','小過x1','小過x2','小過x3','小過x4','小過x5','大過x1','大過x2','大過x3','大過x4','大過x5','停職調查'];

        function normalizeSelectedItems(selected) {
            const legacy={申誡一次:'警告x1',申誡二次:'警告x2',記過一次:'小過x1',記過二次:'小過x2',大過一次:'大過x1',停職處分:'停職調查'};
            const arr=Array.isArray(selected)?selected:String(selected||'').split(/[、,，]/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
            return arr.map(x=>legacy[x]||x).filter(Boolean);
        }
        function getSelectedPunishmentItems(containerId='punish-item') {
            const c=document.getElementById(containerId); return c?Array.from(c.querySelectorAll('input[data-punish-item]:checked')).map(x=>x.value):[];
        }
        function populatePunishmentItemOptions(containerId='punish-item', type='', selected=[]) {
            const c=document.getElementById(containerId); if(!c)return;
            const selectedSet=new Set(normalizeSelectedItems(selected));
            if(!type){c.innerHTML='<div class="text-xs text-slate-500">請先選擇獎懲類型</div>';return;}
            const items=type==='reward'?REWARD_ITEMS:PUNISHMENT_ITEMS;
            c.innerHTML='<div class="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">'+items.map(item=>`<label class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/70 px-3 py-2 cursor-pointer"><input type="checkbox" data-punish-item value="${item}" ${selectedSet.has(item)?'checked':''} class="w-4 h-4 rounded border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 text-amber-500"><span class="text-xs text-slate-200">${item}</span></label>`).join('')+'</div>';
        }

        function isTotalManagementUser(u=currentUser){ return !!u && getEffectiveRole(u)==='admin'; }
        function isPoliceUser(u=currentUser){ return !!u && (getEffectiveRole(u)==='admin' || (u.units||[u.unit]).includes('police')); }
        function lookupLocked(type){ return appData.lookupLocks?.[type] !== false; }
        function lookupAccessible(type,u=currentUser){ return isTotalManagementUser(u) || !lookupLocked(type); }
        function getLastFive(value){ const s=String(value||'').replace(/\D/g,''); return s.length>=5?s.slice(-5):String(value||'').trim().slice(-5); }
        function lookupTypeLabel(type){ return type==='citizen'?'市民身份':type==='vehicles'?'車輛': '通緝'; }
        function renderLookupSystems(){
            const citizenCount=document.getElementById('citizen-total-count');
            if(citizenCount) citizenCount.innerHTML=`<i class="fa-solid fa-users mr-1.5"></i>當前所有市民：${(appData.citizenRecords||[]).length} 人`;
            const map=[['citizen','citizen'],['vehicles','vehicles'],['wanted','wanted']];
            map.forEach(([type,key])=>{
                const accessible=lookupAccessible(type);
                const lockedView=document.getElementById(`${type}-locked-view`);
                const unlockedView=document.getElementById(`${type}-unlocked-view`);
                // 同時使用 hidden class + inline display，避免頁面重新渲染／切換分頁後殘留「開發階段」畫面。
                if(lockedView){
                    lockedView.classList.toggle('hidden',accessible);
                    lockedView.style.display=accessible?'none':'';
                }
                if(unlockedView){
                    unlockedView.classList.toggle('hidden',!accessible);
                    unlockedView.style.display=accessible?'':'none';
                }
                if(accessible && type==='wanted') searchWantedRecords();
            });
            const admin=isTotalManagementUser();
            const police=isPoliceUser();
            document.getElementById('open-citizen-post-btn')?.classList.toggle('hidden',!(admin&&lookupAccessible('citizen')));
            document.getElementById('open-vehicle-post-btn')?.classList.toggle('hidden',!(admin&&lookupAccessible('vehicles')));
            document.getElementById('open-wanted-post-btn')?.classList.toggle('hidden',!(police&&lookupAccessible('wanted')));
            renderLookupLockSettings();
        }
        function renderLookupLockSettings(){
            const ids=[['citizen','lock-citizen'],['vehicles','lock-vehicles'],['wanted','lock-wanted']];
            ids.forEach(([k,id])=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el){el.checked=lookupLocked(k);el.disabled=!isTotalManagementUser();}});
            document.getElementById('save-lookup-locks-btn')?.classList.toggle('hidden',!isTotalManagementUser());
        }
        async function saveLookupLockSettings(){
            if(!isTotalManagementUser()){showToast('只有總管理權限可以修改上鎖頻道設定。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            const next={citizen:!!document.getElementById('lock-citizen')?.checked,vehicles:!!document.getElementById('lock-vehicles')?.checked,wanted:!!document.getElementById('lock-wanted')?.checked};
            try{await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'systemSettings','lookupLocks'),{...next,updatedAt:Date.now(),updatedBy:currentUser.account},{merge:true});appData.lookupLocks=next;await logAdminAction('編輯查詢頻道鎖定設定','lookupLocks',`市民:${next.citizen?'上鎖':'解鎖'}；車輛:${next.vehicles?'上鎖':'解鎖'}；通緝:${next.wanted?'上鎖':'解鎖'}`,{discord:true});renderLookupSystems();showToast('查詢頻道鎖定設定已儲存。','success','儲存成功');}catch(e){showToast('儲存失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}
        }
        function lookupInput(label,id,placeholder='',extra=''){return `<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 mb-2">${label}</label><input id="${id}" ${extra} placeholder="${placeholder}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"></div>`;}
        function lookupTextarea(label,id,placeholder='',rows=4){return `<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 mb-2">${label}</label><textarea id="${id}" rows="${rows}" placeholder="${placeholder}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 resize-y"></textarea></div>`;}
        function openLookupPost(type, id=''){
            if(!lookupAccessible(type)){showToast('此查詢系統目前已上鎖，顯示開發階段。','warning','功能尚未開放');return;}
            const policeLimitedEdit=isPoliceUser()&&!isTotalManagementUser()&&(type==='citizen'||type==='vehicles')&&!!id;
            const canCreate=policeLimitedEdit ? true : ((type==='citizen'||type==='vehicles')?isTotalManagementUser():isPoliceUser()); if(!canCreate){showToast('目前帳號沒有建立此類資料的權限。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            let existing=(type==='citizen'?appData.citizenRecords:type==='vehicles'?appData.vehicleRecords:appData.wantedRecords).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id));
            if(type==='wanted'&&!existing) existing=(appData.wantedPendingRecords||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id));
            if(type==='wanted'&&existing&&existing.reviewStatus==='rejected'&&existing.createdByAccount!==currentUser.account&&!isTotalManagementUser()){showToast('只有原發布帳號或管理中心可以修改退件的通緝申請。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            document.getElementById('lookup-post-type').value=type;document.getElementById('lookup-post-id').value=existing?(existing.docId||existing.id):'';
            document.getElementById('lookup-post-title').textContent=`${existing?'編輯':'建立'}${lookupTypeLabel(type)}資料`;
            let html='';
            if(type==='citizen'){
                if(policeLimitedEdit){
                    html+='<div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/5 border border-blue-500/20 text-sm text-blue-200">警察權限：僅可新增備註及修改駕照資訊，其他市民資料不可修改。</div>';
                    html+='<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 mb-2">新增備註</label><textarea id="lookup-police-note" rows="4" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-blue-500" placeholder="輸入要新增的備註"></textarea></div>';
                    html+='<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 mb-2">駕照資訊</label><div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2"><label class="p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" id="license-car" class="mr-2">小客車駕照</label><label class="p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" id="license-heavy" class="mr-2">大客車/大貨車駕照</label><label class="p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" id="license-boat" class="mr-2">船隻駕照</label></div><p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-2">取消勾選代表移除該駕照；系統會自動記錄操作者、時間與移除的駕照。</p></div>';
                }else{
                html+=lookupInput('RP名稱','lookup-name','例如：王小明');
                html+=lookupInput('證件末五碼','lookup-id5','請輸入5碼數字','maxlength="5" inputmode="numeric"');
                html+='<div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">'+lookupInput('性別','lookup-gender','例如：男／女／其他')+lookupInput('生日','lookup-birthday','請輸入生日，例如：2000-01-01','type="text" maxlength="10" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="bday"')+'</div>';
                html+='<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 mb-2">駕照狀況（非必填）</label><div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2"><label class="p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" id="license-car" class="mr-2">小客車駕照</label><label class="p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" id="license-heavy" class="mr-2">大客車/大貨車駕照</label><label class="p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" id="license-boat" class="mr-2">船隻駕照</label></div><p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-2">未勾選任何項目時顯示「無」。</p></div>';
                html+=lookupTextarea('備注','lookup-note','可填寫其他補充資訊，多行內容可直接換行',4);html+='<label class="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl border border-red-500/20 bg-red-500/5 text-sm text-red-300"><input type="checkbox" id="lookup-deceased" class="w-4 h-4">死亡證明／已死亡</label>';
                }
            }else if(type==='vehicles'){
                if(policeLimitedEdit){
                    html+='<div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/5 border border-blue-500/20 text-sm text-blue-200">警察權限：僅可新增備註，車輛基本資料不可修改。</div>';
                    html+='<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 mb-2">新增備註</label><textarea id="lookup-police-note" rows="4" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-blue-500" placeholder="輸入要新增的車輛備註"></textarea></div>';
                }else{

                html+=lookupInput('車輛型號','lookup-model','例如：Toyota Crown');
                html+='<div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">'+lookupInput('車輛所有人（非必填）','lookup-owner','輸入證件末五碼後自動帶入RP名稱，可不填')+lookupInput('證件末五碼','lookup-id5','請輸入5碼數字','maxlength="5" inputmode="numeric"')+'</div>';
                html+='<p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 -mt-2"><i class="fa-solid fa-wand-magic-sparkles mr-1 text-cyan-400"></i>輸入5碼證件末五碼後，系統會自動從市民身份資料帶入對應RP名稱；若查無市民資料，RP名稱可留白。</p>';
                html+=lookupInput('車牌','lookup-plate','例如：ABC-1234');html+=lookupInput('車色','lookup-color','例如：黑色');html+=lookupTextarea('備注','lookup-note','可填寫車輛備注或其他補充資訊，多行內容可直接換行',4);html+='<label class="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl border border-red-500/20 bg-red-500/5 text-sm text-red-300"><input type="checkbox" id="lookup-decommissioned" class="w-4 h-4">註銷牌照／已註銷</label>';
                }
            }else{
                html+=lookupInput('被通緝人RP名稱','lookup-name','例如：王小明');html+=lookupInput('證件末五碼','lookup-id5','請輸入5碼數字','maxlength="5" inputmode="numeric"');html+=lookupInput('通緝原因','lookup-reason','請輸入通緝原因');html+=lookupInput('所違反條例','lookup-law','例如：妨害公務、搶劫');html+=lookupInput('發布單位','lookup-publish-unit','將依發布帳號所屬警察單位自動帶入');
            }
            document.getElementById('lookup-post-fields').innerHTML=html;
            if(existing){
                if(type==='citizen'){
                    const setVal=(id,val)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value=val||'';};
                    setVal('lookup-name',existing.name);setVal('lookup-id5',existing.id5);setVal('lookup-gender',existing.gender);setVal('lookup-birthday',existing.birthday);
                    const ls=Array.isArray(existing.licenses)?existing.licenses:[];
                    const car=document.getElementById('license-car'),heavy=document.getElementById('license-heavy'),boat=document.getElementById('license-boat');
                    if(car)car.checked=ls.includes('小客車駕照'); if(heavy)heavy.checked=ls.includes('大客車/大貨車駕照'); if(boat)boat.checked=ls.includes('船隻駕照');
                    setVal('lookup-note',existing.note); const note=document.getElementById('lookup-police-note'); if(note)note.value='';
                    const deceased=document.getElementById('lookup-deceased'); if(deceased)deceased.checked=!!existing.deceased;
                }
                if(type==='vehicles'){
                    const setVal=(id,val)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value=val||'';};
                    setVal('lookup-model',existing.model);setVal('lookup-owner',existing.owner);setVal('lookup-id5',existing.id5);setVal('lookup-plate',existing.plate);setVal('lookup-color',existing.color);setVal('lookup-note',existing.note);
                    const note=document.getElementById('lookup-police-note'); if(note)note.value='';
                    const decommissioned=document.getElementById('lookup-decommissioned'); if(decommissioned)decommissioned.checked=!!existing.decommissioned;
                }
                if(type==='wanted'){document.getElementById('lookup-name').value=existing.name||'';document.getElementById('lookup-id5').value=existing.id5||'';document.getElementById('lookup-reason').value=existing.reason||'';document.getElementById('lookup-law').value=existing.law||'';document.getElementById('lookup-publish-unit').value=existing.publishUnit||existing.department||getPolicePunishmentDepartment(currentUser)||'';}
            }
            if(type==='wanted'&&!existing){const el=document.getElementById('lookup-publish-unit');if(el)el.value=getPolicePunishmentDepartment(currentUser)||'';}
            if(type==='vehicles'){
                const idEl=document.getElementById('lookup-id5'), ownerEl=document.getElementById('lookup-owner');
                const syncVehicleOwner=()=>{
                    const id5=String(idEl?.value||'').replace(/\D/g,'').slice(0,5); if(idEl)idEl.value=id5;
                    if(!ownerEl)return;
                    const citizen=(appData.citizenRecords||[]).find(r=>getLastFive(r.id5)===id5);
                    if(citizen?.name){ownerEl.value=citizen.name;ownerEl.dataset.autoFilled='1';ownerEl.classList.add('border-cyan-500/60');}
                    else if(ownerEl.dataset.autoFilled==='1'){ownerEl.value='';ownerEl.dataset.autoFilled='0';ownerEl.classList.remove('border-cyan-500/60');}
                };
                idEl?.addEventListener('input',syncVehicleOwner); idEl?.addEventListener('change',syncVehicleOwner); syncVehicleOwner();
            }
            document.getElementById('modal-lookup-post').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        async function saveLookupPost(){
            const type=document.getElementById('lookup-post-type').value,id=document.getElementById('lookup-post-id').value;
            if(!lookupAccessible(type)){showToast('此查詢系統目前已上鎖。','warning','功能尚未開放');return;}
            const policeLimitedEdit=isPoliceUser()&&!isTotalManagementUser()&&(type==='citizen'||type==='vehicles')&&!!id;
            const canCreate=policeLimitedEdit ? true : ((type==='citizen'||type==='vehicles')?isTotalManagementUser():isPoliceUser());if(!canCreate){showToast('權限不足。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            const now=Date.now();
            const approvedCol=type==='citizen'?'citizenRecords':type==='vehicles'?'vehicleRecords':'wantedRecords';
            const list=type==='citizen'?appData.citizenRecords:type==='vehicles'?appData.vehicleRecords:appData.wantedRecords;
            let existing=list.find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id));
            let existingPending=null;
            if(type==='wanted'&&!existing) existingPending=(appData.wantedPendingRecords||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id));
            if(existingPending) existing=existingPending;
            const col=(type==='wanted' && !existingPending && !id)?'wantedPendingRecords':(type==='wanted' && existingPending?'wantedPendingRecords':approvedCol);
            let data={};
            if(policeLimitedEdit){
                if(!existing){showToast('找不到要修改的資料。','error','資料不存在');return;}
                const addedNote=document.getElementById('lookup-police-note')?.value.trim()||'';
                if(type==='citizen'){
                    const oldLicenses=Array.isArray(existing.licenses)?existing.licenses:[];
                    const licenses=[];
                    if(document.getElementById('license-car')?.checked)licenses.push('小客車駕照');
                    if(document.getElementById('license-heavy')?.checked)licenses.push('大客車/大貨車駕照');
                    if(document.getElementById('license-boat')?.checked)licenses.push('船隻駕照');
                    const added=licenses.filter(x=>!oldLicenses.includes(x));
                    const removed=oldLicenses.filter(x=>!licenses.includes(x));
                    if(!added.length&&!removed.length&&!addedNote){showToast('沒有任何變更。','warning','未變更');return;}
                    data={updatedAt:now,cloudSyncedAt:now,updatedByAccount:currentUser.account,updatedByName:currentUser.name};
                    if(addedNote){data.note=[existing.note||'',`【${formatDateTime(now)}｜${currentUser.account}】 ${addedNote}`].filter(Boolean).join('\n');data.noteHistory=[...(existing.noteHistory||[]),{at:now,account:currentUser.account,by:currentUser.name||currentUser.account,note:addedNote}];}
                    data.licenses=licenses;
                    const hist=[...(existing.licenseHistory||[])];
                    if(added.length)hist.push({licenses:added,action:'新增',at:now,by:currentUser.name||currentUser.account,account:currentUser.account});
                    if(removed.length)hist.push({licenses:removed,action:'移除',at:now,by:currentUser.name||currentUser.account,account:currentUser.account});
                    data.licenseHistory=hist;
                }else{
                    if(!addedNote){showToast('請輸入要新增的備註。','warning','資料不完整');return;}
                    data={updatedAt:now,cloudSyncedAt:now,updatedByAccount:currentUser.account,updatedByName:currentUser.name,note:[existing.note||'',`【${formatDateTime(now)}｜${currentUser.account}】 ${addedNote}`].filter(Boolean).join('\n'),noteHistory:[...(existing.noteHistory||[]),{at:now,account:currentUser.account,by:currentUser.name||currentUser.account,note:addedNote}]};
                }
                try{await fm.updateDoc(fm.doc(db,col,id),data);Object.assign(existing,data);existing.docId=id;renderLookupSystems();type==='citizen'?searchCitizenRecords():searchVehicleRecords();showToast('資料已更新，並保留操作紀錄。','success','更新成功');document.getElementById('modal-lookup-post').classList.add('hidden');}catch(e){showToast('更新失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}return;
            }
            if(type==='citizen'){
                const name=document.getElementById('lookup-name').value.trim(),id5=document.getElementById('lookup-id5').value.trim(),gender=document.getElementById('lookup-gender').value.trim(),birthday=document.getElementById('lookup-birthday').value,note=document.getElementById('lookup-note')?.value.trim()||'',deceased=!!document.getElementById('lookup-deceased')?.checked,licenses=[];
                if(document.getElementById('license-car')?.checked)licenses.push('小客車駕照');
                if(document.getElementById('license-heavy')?.checked)licenses.push('大客車/大貨車駕照');
                if(document.getElementById('license-boat')?.checked)licenses.push('船隻駕照');
                if(!name||!/^[0-9]{5}$/.test(id5)||!gender||!birthday){showToast('請完整填寫RP名稱、證件末五碼、性別與生日。','warning','資料不完整');return;}
                data={name,id5,gender,birthday,licenses,note,deceased,updatedAt:now,cloudSyncedAt:now,updatedByAccount:currentUser.account,updatedByName:currentUser.name};
                if(existing&&String(existing.name||'')!==name){data.nameHistory=[...(existing.nameHistory||[]),{from:existing.name||'',to:name,at:now,by:currentUser.name||currentUser.account}];}
                const oldLicenses=new Set(existing?.licenses||[]),newLicenses=licenses.filter(x=>!oldLicenses.has(x));
                if(newLicenses.length){data.licenseHistory=[...(existing?.licenseHistory||[]),{licenses:newLicenses,at:now,by:currentUser.name||currentUser.account,account:currentUser.account}];}
                if(!existing){data.createdAt=now;data.createdByAccount=currentUser.account;data.createdByName=currentUser.name;if(licenses.length){data.licenseHistory=[{licenses:[...licenses],at:now,by:currentUser.name||currentUser.account,account:currentUser.account}];}}
            }else if(type==='vehicles'){
                const model=document.getElementById('lookup-model').value.trim(),ownerInput=document.getElementById('lookup-owner').value.trim(),id5=document.getElementById('lookup-id5').value.trim(),plate=document.getElementById('lookup-plate').value.trim(),color=document.getElementById('lookup-color').value.trim(),note=document.getElementById('lookup-note')?.value.trim()||'',decommissioned=!!document.getElementById('lookup-decommissioned')?.checked;
                const linkedCitizen=(appData.citizenRecords||[]).find(r=>getLastFive(r.id5)===id5);
                const owner=linkedCitizen?.name||ownerInput;
                if(!model||!/^[0-9]{5}$/.test(id5)||!plate||!color){showToast('請填寫車輛型號、證件末五碼、車牌與車色。RP名稱非必填，輸入證件末五碼後會自動帶入。','warning','資料不完整');return;}
                data={model,owner,id5,plate,color,note,decommissioned,updatedAt:now,cloudSyncedAt:now,updatedByAccount:currentUser.account,updatedByName:currentUser.name};
                if(!existing){data.createdAt=now;data.createdByAccount=currentUser.account;data.createdByName=currentUser.name;}
            }else{
                const name=document.getElementById('lookup-name').value.trim(),id5=document.getElementById('lookup-id5').value.trim(),reason=document.getElementById('lookup-reason').value.trim(),law=document.getElementById('lookup-law').value.trim(),publishUnit=document.getElementById('lookup-publish-unit').value.trim();
                if(!name||!/^[0-9]{5}$/.test(id5)||!reason||!law||!publishUnit){showToast('請完整填寫通緝資料，包含發布單位。','warning','資料不完整');return;}
                data={name,id5,reason,law,publishUnit,updatedAt:now,cloudSyncedAt:now,updatedByAccount:currentUser.account,updatedByName:currentUser.name};
                if(!existing){data.createdAt=now;data.createdByAccount=currentUser.account;data.createdByName=currentUser.name;data.reviewStatus='pending';data.reviewedAt=0;data.reviewedBy='';data.rejectionReason='';data.submittedAt=now;data.discordDmStatus='not_requested';data.discordDmError='';data.discordDmSentAt=0;data.discordUserId='';data.discordNickname='';data.discordMatchStatus='not_checked';}
                else if(existingPending){data.reviewStatus='pending';data.reviewedAt=0;data.reviewedBy='';data.rejectionReason='';data.submittedAt=now;data.discordDmStatus='not_requested';data.discordDmError='';data.discordDmSentAt=0;data.discordUserId='';data.discordNickname='';data.discordMatchStatus='not_checked';}
            }
            try{
                const docId=existing?(existing.docId||existing.id):`${type}-${now}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`;
                const writeData={...(existing||{}),...data,createdByAccount:existing?.createdByAccount||currentUser.account,createdByName:existing?.createdByName||currentUser.name};
                await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,col,docId),writeData,{merge:true});
                if(type==='wanted'){
                    const targetList=col==='wantedPendingRecords'?appData.wantedPendingRecords:appData.wantedRecords;
                    if(existing){Object.assign(existing,{docId,...writeData});}
                    else targetList.unshift({docId,...writeData});
                    if(col==='wantedPendingRecords'){
                        await removePendingReportNotifications(docId,writeData.createdByAccount);
                        const n={type:'wanted',department:'police',caseId:docId,recipientAccount:writeData.createdByAccount,recipientAccountNormalized:String(writeData.createdByAccount||'').trim().toLowerCase(),status:'pending',title:'通緝申請已送出，等待警察高層審核',message:`通緝申請 ${writeData.name||docId} 已送入警察高層審核系統。`,timestamp:Date.now(),read:false};
                        const nr=fm.doc(fm.collection(db,'reportNotifications')); await fm.setDoc(nr,n); appData.reportNotifications.unshift({docId:nr.id,...n});
                        sendDiscordWantedNotification(writeData);
                        renderPoliceReview(); renderPersonalNotifications(); renderMyProfile();
                    }
                }else{
                    if(existing){Object.assign(existing,{docId,...data,createdByAccount:existing.createdByAccount||currentUser.account,createdByName:existing.createdByName||currentUser.name});
                        if(type==='citizen'&&data.nameHistory?.length){
                            const latestNameChange=data.nameHistory[data.nameHistory.length-1];
                            const oldName=String(latestNameChange.from||'');
                            const newName=String(latestNameChange.to||data.name||'');
                            const changedVehicles=(appData.vehicleRecords||[]).filter(v=>getLastFive(v.id5)===getLastFive(data.id5));
                            if(oldName!==newName&&changedVehicles.length){
                                await Promise.all(changedVehicles.map(async v=>{
                                    const vehicleDocId=v.docId||v.id||'';
                                    if(!vehicleDocId)return;
                                    const vehicleUpdate={owner:newName,updatedAt:now,cloudSyncedAt:now,updatedByAccount:currentUser.account,updatedByName:currentUser.name};
                                    await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'vehicleRecords',vehicleDocId),vehicleUpdate,{merge:true});
                                    Object.assign(v,vehicleUpdate);
                                }));
                                await logAdminAction('市民改名同步更新車輛擁有人',docId,`證件末五碼 ${data.id5}：${oldName} → ${newName}，同步更新 ${changedVehicles.length} 筆車輛資料`,{discord:true});
                            }
                        }
                        await logAdminAction(`編輯${lookupTypeLabel(type)}資料`,docId, type==='citizen'&&data.nameHistory?`名稱異動：${data.nameHistory[data.nameHistory.length-1].from} → ${data.nameHistory[data.nameHistory.length-1].to}`+(data.licenseHistory?.length?`；新增駕照：${data.licenseHistory[data.licenseHistory.length-1].licenses.join('、')}`:'') : (type==='citizen'&&data.licenseHistory?.length?`新增駕照：${data.licenseHistory[data.licenseHistory.length-1].licenses.join('、')}`:''), {discord:true});
                    }
                    else list.unshift({docId,...data,createdByAccount:currentUser.account,createdByName:currentUser.name});
                }
                closeModal('modal-lookup-post');renderLookupSystems();if(type==='citizen'){const el=document.getElementById('citizen-search-input');if(el)el.value=data.id5||existing?.id5||'';searchCitizenRecords();}if(type==='vehicles'){const el=document.getElementById('vehicle-search-input');if(el)el.value=data.plate||existing?.plate||'';searchVehicleRecords();}if(type==='wanted'){const el=document.getElementById('wanted-search-input');if(el)el.value=data.id5||existing?.id5||'';searchWantedRecords();}showToast(type==='wanted'?'通緝申請已送出，等待警察高層審核。':`${existing?'修改':'建立'}${lookupTypeLabel(type)}資料成功，已同步雲端資料庫。`,'success',type==='wanted'?'送審成功':'雲端同步成功');
            }catch(e){showToast('雲端寫入失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}
        }
        function lookupKeyword(value){return String(value??'').trim().toLowerCase();}
        function lookupTextMatch(values,q){if(!q)return false;return values.some(v=>String(v??'').toLowerCase().includes(q));}
        function recordMatchesCitizen(r,q){return lookupTextMatch([r.name,r.id5,r.gender,r.birthday,r.note],q);}
        function renderCitizenCard(r){const suspectCases=(appData.policeReports||[]).filter(x=>getLastFive(x.suspectId)===getLastFive(r.id5));const linkedVehicles=(appData.vehicleRecords||[]).filter(v=>getLastFive(v.id5)===getLastFive(r.id5));const wantedRecords=(appData.wantedRecords||[]).filter(w=>getLastFive(w.id5)===getLastFive(r.id5));const isWanted=wantedRecords.length>0;const cardBg=isWanted?'bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-950/95 via-red-950/90 to-slate-950 border-blue-500/70':(r.deceased?'bg-red-950/80 border-red-500/60':'bg-slate-900 border-slate-800');return `<div class="${cardBg} border rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-xl"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><h4 class="text-lg font-bold text-white">${escapeHtml(r.name||'--')}</h4>${isWanted?'<span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-red-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-lg"><i class="fa-solid fa-handcuffs mr-1"></i>通緝狀態</span>':''}${r.deceased?'<span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-red-600 text-white text-xs font-bold"><i class="fa-solid fa-skull-crossbones mr-1"></i>死亡證明</span>':''}</div><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono">證件末五碼：${escapeHtml(r.id5||'--')}</p><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">發布帳號：${escapeHtml(r.createdByAccount||'--')}</p></div><span class="text-xs text-slate-400">${escapeHtml(r.gender||'--')}｜${escapeHtml(r.birthday||'--')}</span></div><div class="mt-4 text-sm text-slate-300"><span class="text-slate-500">駕照狀況：</span>${escapeHtml((r.licenses||[]).length?(r.licenses||[]).join('、'):'無')}</div>${linkedVehicles.length?`<div class="mt-3 p-3 rounded-xl bg-cyan-500/5 border border-cyan-500/15 text-sm"><span class="text-xs text-cyan-300 block mb-2">名下車輛（依證件末五碼自動比對）</span><div class="space-y-1">${linkedVehicles.map(v=>`<div class="text-slate-300">${escapeHtml(v.model||'未知型號')}｜車牌：<span class="font-mono text-cyan-300">${escapeHtml(v.plate||'--')}</span></div>`).join('')}</div></div>`:''}${r.note?`<div class="mt-3 p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-sm"><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block mb-1">備注</span><span class="text-slate-300 whitespace-pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(r.note)}</span></div>`:''}<div class="lookup-card-actions flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-4 w-full"><button type="button" class="px-3 py-2 bg-blue-600/15 text-blue-300 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px] flex-1 sm:flex-none" onclick="viewLookupDetail('citizen','${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')">放大檢視</button>${isTotalManagementUser()?`<button type="button" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px] flex-1 sm:flex-none" onclick="openLookupPost('citizen','${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')">編輯</button>`:''}${isTotalManagementUser()?`<button type="button" class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/15 text-red-300 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px] flex-1 sm:flex-none" onclick="deleteLookupRecord('citizen','${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')">移除資料</button>`:''}${isPoliceUser()?`<button type="button" class="px-3 py-2 bg-emerald-600/15 text-emerald-300 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px] flex-1 sm:flex-none" onclick="openLookupPost('citizen','${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')">新增備註／修改駕照</button>`:''}</div>${suspectCases.length?`<div class="mt-4 p-3 rounded-xl bg-red-500/5 border border-red-500/15 text-xs text-red-200">曾被登記為嫌疑人：${suspectCases.length} 件</div>`:''}</div>`;}
        function searchCitizenRecords(){if(!lookupAccessible('citizen'))return;const q=lookupKeyword(document.getElementById('citizen-search-input')?.value||'');const filter=document.getElementById('citizen-deceased-filter')?.value||'all';const c=document.getElementById('citizen-results');if(filter==='deceased'){const rows=appData.citizenRecords.filter(r=>!!r.deceased);c.innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(renderCitizenCard).join(''):'<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center text-slate-500">目前沒有死亡證明資料。</div>';return;}if(!q){c.innerHTML='<div class="bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-8 text-center text-slate-500"><i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass text-2xl mb-2"></i><div>請輸入搜尋條件後再查詢</div><div class="text-xs mt-1">「全部」與「不顯示死亡證明」需輸入搜尋條件後才會列出資料。</div></div>';return;}let rows=appData.citizenRecords.filter(r=>{const statusOk=filter==='all'||(filter==='alive'?!r.deceased:true);return statusOk&&recordMatchesCitizen(r,q);});c.innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(renderCitizenCard).join(''):'<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center text-slate-500">查無符合條件的資料。</div>'; }

whitespace-pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(r.note)}</span></div>`:''}</div>${ownerWanted?'<span class="shrink-0 px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-red-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-lg"><i class="fa-solid fa-handcuffs mr-1"></i>車主通緝中</span>':''}${r.decommissioned?'<span class="shrink-0 px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-red-600 text-white text-xs font-bold"><i class="fa-solid fa-ban mr-1"></i>註銷牌照</span>':''}</div><div class="lookup-card-actions flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-4 w-full"><button type="button" class="px-3 py-2 bg-cyan-600/15 text-cyan-300 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px] flex-1 sm:flex-none" onclick="viewLookupDetail('vehicles','${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')">放大檢視</button>${isTotalManagementUser()?`<button type="button" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px] flex-1 sm:flex-none" onclick="openLookupPost('vehicles','${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')">編輯</button>`:''}${isTotalManagementUser()?`<button type="button" class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/15 text-red-300 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px] flex-1 sm:flex-none" min-h-[44px] flex-1 sm:flex-none" onclick="openLookupPost('vehicles','${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')">新增備註</button>`:''}</div></div>`}).join(''):'<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center text-slate-500">查無資料。</div>';}
        function getWantedDiscordStatusHtml(r){
            const status=String(r.discordDmStatus||'not_requested');
            if(status==='sent') return '<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 text-xs"><i class="fa-solid fa-check"></i>已私訊</span>';
            if(status==='pending'||status==='processing') return '<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 text-xs"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner"></i>等待 Bot</span>';
            if(status==='not_found'||status==='match_failed') return '<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 text-xs"><i class="fa-solid fa-user-xmark"></i>找不到 Discord</span>';
            if(status==='failed') return '<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 text-red-300 text-xs"><i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation"></i>私訊失敗</span>';
            return '<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-slate-400 text-xs"><i class="fa-solid fa-clock"></i>尚未通知</span>';
        }
        function getWantedDiscordManualButtonHtml(r){
            const last=Number(r.discordManualNotifyAt||0);
            const remain=24*60*60*1000-(Date.now()-last);
            if(last && remain>0){
                const hours=Math.floor(remain/3600000);
                const mins=Math.ceil((remain%3600000)/60000);
                return `<button type="button" disabled class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800/80 text-slate-500 rounded-lg text-xs cursor-not-allowed" title="同一名通緝對象 24 小時內只能手動通知一次"><i class="fa-solid fa-clock mr-1"></i>今日已通知（${hours}小時${mins}分後）</button>`;
            }
            return `<button type="button" class="px-3 py-2 bg-indigo-600/15 hover:bg-indigo-600/25 text-indigo-300 border border-indigo-500/20 rounded-lg text-xs" onclick="sendManualWantedDiscordNotification('${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')"><i class="fa-brands fa-discord mr-1"></i>發送 DISCORD 通知</button>`;
        }
        async function sendManualWantedDiscordNotification(id){
            if(!lookupAccessible('wanted')){showToast('此查詢系統目前已上鎖。','warning','功能尚未開放');return;}
            if(!isPoliceUser()){showToast('只有警察帳號可以發送通緝 Discord 通知。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            const r=(appData.wantedRecords||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id));
            if(!r){showToast('找不到這筆通緝資料。','error','資料不存在');return;}
            const last=Number(r.discordManualNotifyAt||0), remain=24*60*60*1000-(Date.now()-last);
            if(last && remain>0){
                const hours=Math.floor(remain/3600000), mins=Math.ceil((remain%3600000)/60000);
                showToast(`同一名通緝對象 24 小時內只能通知一次，請 ${hours} 小時 ${mins} 分後再試。`,'warning','今日已通知');
                return;
            }
            if(!(await showConfirm(`確定要透過 Discord Bot 私訊「${r.name||'此使用者'}」通緝通知嗎？此功能 24 小時內只能使用一次。`,'發送 Discord 通知','確定發送')))return;
            try{
                const now=Date.now();
                const docId=r.docId||r.id;
                const patch={
                    discordDmStatus:'pending',
                    discordNotifyRequestedAt:now,
                    discordNotifyRequestedBy:currentUser.account,
                    discordNotifySource:'manual',
                    discordManualNotifyAt:now,
                    discordDmError:'',
                    updatedAt:now
                };
                await fm.updateDoc(fm.doc(db,'wantedRecords',docId),patch);
                Object.assign(r,patch);
                await logAdminAction('手動發送通緝 Discord 通知',docId,`被通緝人：${r.name||'--'}；證件末五碼：${r.id5||'--'}`);
                renderLookupSystems();
                showToast('已送出 Discord Bot 通知請求，Bot 將私訊該使用者。','success','通知已排程');
            }catch(e){
                showToast('Discord 通知請求失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');
            }
        }
        function updateWantedDiscordBotStatus(){
            const el=document.getElementById('wanted-discord-bot-status'); if(!el)return;
            const rows=appData.wantedRecords||[]; const sent=rows.filter(r=>r.discordDmStatus==='sent').length; const pending=rows.filter(r=>['pending','processing'].includes(r.discordDmStatus)).length; const failed=rows.filter(r=>['failed','not_found','match_failed'].includes(r.discordDmStatus)).length;
            el.textContent=pending?`Bot待處理 ${pending} 件`:failed?`需人工確認 ${failed} 件`:`已通知 ${sent} 件`;
            el.className='inline-flex items-center px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs '+(pending?'bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-amber-300':failed?'bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 text-rose-300':'bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300');
        }
        function searchWantedRecords(){if(!lookupAccessible('wanted'))return;updateWantedDiscordBotStatus();const q=lookupKeyword(document.getElementById('wanted-search-input')?.value||'');const c=document.getElementById('wanted-results');if(!q){const rows=appData.wantedRecords||[];c.innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(r=>`<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-rose-500/15 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-xl"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><h4 class="text-lg font-bold text-white">${escapeHtml(r.name||'--')}</h4><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono">證件末五碼：${escapeHtml(r.id5||'--')}</p><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">發布帳號：${escapeHtml(r.createdByAccount||'--')}</p><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">發布單位：<span class="text-cyan-300">${escapeHtml(r.publishUnit||r.department||'--')}</span></p></div><span class="self-start inline-flex items-center whitespace-nowrap px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/20 text-xs font-semibold leading-none">通緝中</span>${getWantedDiscordStatusHtml(r)}</div><div class="mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm"><div><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block">通緝原因</span>${escapeHtml(r.reason||'--')}</div><div><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block">所違反條例</span>${escapeHtml(r.law||'--')}</div></div><div class="flex
