onclick="viewLookupDetail('wanted','${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')">點進去預覽全部</button>${isPoliceUser()?getWantedDiscordManualButtonHtml(r):''}${isPoliceUser()?`<button class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/15 text-red-300 rounded-lg text-xs" onclick="deleteLookupRecord('wanted','${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')">刪除</button>`:''}${isTotalManagementUser()?`<button class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs" onclick="openLookupPost('wanted','${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')">編輯</button>`:''}</div></div>`).join(''):'<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center text-slate-500">目前沒有已核准的通緝資料。</div>';return;}const rows=appData.wantedRecords.filter(r=>lookupTextMatch([r.name,r.id5,r.reason,r.law,r.publishUnit,r.department,r.createdByAccount,r.createdByName],q));c.innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(r=>`<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-rose-500/15 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-xl"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><h4 class="text-lg font-bold text-white">${escapeHtml(r.name||'--')}</h4><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono">證件末五碼：${escapeHtml(r.id5||'--')}</p><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">發布帳號：${escapeHtml(r.createdByAccount||'--')}</p><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">發布單位：<span class="text-cyan-300">${escapeHtml(r.publishUnit||r.department||'--')}</span></p></div><span class="self-start inline-flex items-center whitespace-nowrap px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/20 text-xs font-semibold leading-none">通緝中</span>${getWantedDiscordStatusHtml(r)}</div><div class="mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm"><div><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block">通緝原因</span>${escapeHtml(r.reason||'--')}</div><div><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block">所違反條例</span>${escapeHtml(r.law||'--')}</div></div><div class="flex gap-2 mt-4"><button class="px-3 py-2 bg-rose-600/15 text-rose-300 rounded-lg text-xs" onclick="viewLookupDetail('wanted','${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')">點進去預覽全部</button>${isPoliceUser()?getWantedDiscordManualButtonHtml(r):''}${isPoliceUser()?`<button class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/15 text-red-300 rounded-lg text-xs" onclick="deleteLookupRecord('wanted','${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')">刪除</button>`:''}${isTotalManagementUser()?`<button class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs" onclick="openLookupPost('wanted','${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')">編輯</button>`:''}</div></div>`).join(''):'<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center text-slate-500">查無通緝資料。</div>';}
        function viewLookupDetail(type,id){
            const list=type==='citizen'?appData.citizenRecords:type==='vehicles'?appData.vehicleRecords:appData.wantedRecords;
            const r=list.find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id));
            if(!r)return;
            const c=document.getElementById('lookup-detail-content');
            document.getElementById('lookup-detail-title').textContent=`${lookupTypeLabel(type)}詳細資料`;
            let html='';
            if(type==='citizen'){
                const cases=(appData.policeReports||[]).filter(x=>getLastFive(x.suspectId)===getLastFive(r.id5));const linkedVehicles=(appData.vehicleRecords||[]).filter(v=>getLastFive(v.id5)===getLastFive(r.id5));
                html=`${r.deceased?`<div class="sm:col-span-2 bg-red-900/60 border border-red-500/50 rounded-xl p-4 text-red-200 font-bold"><i class="fa-solid fa-skull-crossbones mr-2"></i>死亡證明：此市民已標記為死亡</div>`:''}<div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div class="bg-slate-950 rounded-xl p-4"><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block">RP名稱</span><strong class="text-white">${escapeHtml(r.name||'--')}</strong></div>
                    <div class="bg-slate-950 rounded-xl p-4"><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block">證件末五碼</span><strong class="text-white font-mono">${escapeHtml(r.id5||'--')}</strong></div>
                    <div class="bg-slate-950 rounded-xl p-4"><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block">性別</span>${escapeHtml(r.gender||'--')}</div>
                    <div class="bg-slate-950 rounded-xl p-4"><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block">生日</span>${escapeHtml(r.birthday||'--')}</div>
                    <div class="sm:col-span-2 bg-slate-950 rounded-xl p-4"><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block">駕照狀況</span>${escapeHtml((r.licenses||[]).length?r.licenses.join('、'):'無')}</div>
                    <div class="sm:col-span-2 bg-slate-950 rounded-xl p-4"><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block">發布帳號</span><span class="text-slate-200">${escapeHtml(r.createdByAccount||'--')}</span></div>
                    <div class="sm:col-span-2 bg-slate-950 rounded-xl p-4"><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block">備注</span><span class="text-slate-200 whitespace-pre-wrap">${escapeHtml(r.note||'無')}</span></div>
                </div>`;
                if(linkedVehicles.length)html+=`<div class="bg-cyan-500/5 border border-cyan-500/20 rounded-2xl p-5 mt-4"><h4 class="font-bold text-cyan-300 mb-3">名下車輛（自動偵測）</h4><div class="space-y-2">${linkedVehicles.map(v=>`<div class="bg-slate-950 rounded-xl p-3 text-sm"><div class="text-slate-200">車輛型號：${escapeHtml(v.model||'--')}</div><div class="text-cyan-300 font-mono mt-1">車牌：${escapeHtml(v.plate||'--')}</div></div>`).join('')}</div></div>`;
                else html+='<div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 mt-4 text-sm text-slate-500">未偵測到相同證件末五碼的車輛資料。</div>';
                if(cases.length)html+=`<div class="bg-red-500/5 border border-red-500/20 rounded-2xl p-5 mt-4"><h4 class="font-bold text-red-300 mb-3">曾被登記為嫌疑人（${cases.length} 件）</h4><div class="space-y-3">${cases.map(x=>`<div class="bg-slate-950 rounded-xl p-3"><div class="font-mono text-blue-300">${escapeHtml(x.caseId||'--')}</div><div class="text-sm text-slate-300 mt-1">曾犯／涉及罪刑：${escapeHtml(Array.isArray(x.violations)&&x.violations.length?x.violations.join('、'):(x.content||x.title||'案件未記載條例'))}</div></div>`).join('')}</div></div>`;
                else html+='<div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 mt-4 text-sm text-slate-500">查無曾被登記為嫌疑人的案件。</div>';
                if((r.nameHistory||[]).length)html+=`<div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 mt-4"><h4 class="font-bold text-slate-200 mb-3">名稱異動紀錄</h4>${r.nameHistory.map(h=>`<div class="text-xs text-slate-400 py-2 border-b border-slate-800 last:border-0">${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(h.at))}｜${escapeHtml(h.from||'--')} → ${escapeHtml(h.to||'--')}｜${escapeHtml(h.by||'--')}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;
                if((r.licenseHistory||[]).length)html+=`<div class="bg-slate-950 border border-emerald-500/15 rounded-2xl p-5 mt-4"><h4 class="font-bold text-emerald-300 mb-3">駕照發布紀錄</h4>${r.licenseHistory.map(h=>`<div class="text-xs text-slate-400 py-2 border-b border-slate-800 last:border-0">${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(h.at))}｜帳號 ${escapeHtml(h.account||'--')}（${escapeHtml(h.by||h.account||'--')}）${h.action==='移除'?`移除駕照：${escapeHtml((h.licenses||[]).join('、'))}`:`新增駕照：${escapeHtml((h.licenses||[]).join('、'))}`}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;
                else html+='<div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 mt-4 text-sm text-slate-500">尚無駕照異動紀錄。</div>';
                if((r.noteHistory||[]).length)html+=`<div class="bg-slate-950 border border-amber-500/15 rounded-2xl p-5 mt-4"><h4 class="font-bold text-amber-300 mb-3">備註新增紀錄</h4>${r.noteHistory.map(h=>`<div class="text-xs text-slate-400 py-2 border-b border-slate-800 last:border-0">${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(h.at))}｜${escapeHtml(h.by||h.account||'--')}：${escapeHtml(h.note||'')}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;
            }else if(type==='vehicles'){
                html=`${r.decommissioned?'<div class="mb-4 bg-red-900/60 border border-red-500/50 rounded-xl p-4 text-red-200 font-bold"><i class="fa-solid fa-ban mr-2"></i>註銷牌照：此車輛牌照已標記為註銷</div>':''}<div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">${[['車輛型號',r.model],['車輛所有人',r.owner],['證件末五碼',r.id5],['車牌',r.plate],['車色',r.color],['發布帳號',r.createdByAccount||'--'],['備注',r.note||'無']].map(([k,v])=>`<div class="bg-slate-950 rounded-xl p-4"><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block">${escapeHtml(k)}</span><span class="text-slate-200">${escapeHtml(v||'--')}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
                if((r.noteHistory||[]).length)html+=`<div class="bg-slate-950 border border-amber-500/15 rounded-2xl p-5 mt-4"><h4 class="font-bold text-amber-300 mb-3">備註新增紀錄</h4>${r.noteHistory.map(h=>`<div class="text-xs text-slate-400 py-2 border-b border-slate-800 last:border-0">${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(h.at))}｜${escapeHtml(h.by||h.account||'--')}：${escapeHtml(h.note||'')}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;
            }else{
                html=`<div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">${[['被通緝人RP名稱',r.name],['證件末五碼',r.id5],['通緝原因',r.reason],['所違反條例',r.law],['發布單位',r.publishUnit||r.department||'--'],['發布帳號',r.createdByAccount||'--']].map(([k,v])=>`<div class="bg-slate-950 rounded-xl p-4"><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block">${escapeHtml(k)}</span><span class="text-slate-200">${escapeHtml(v||'--')}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
            }
            c.innerHTML=html;
            document.getElementById('modal-lookup-detail').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        async function deleteLookupRecord(type,id){if(!lookupAccessible(type)){showToast('此查詢系統目前已上鎖。','warning','功能尚未開放');return;}const canDelete=isTotalManagementUser();if(!canDelete){showToast('目前帳號沒有刪除權限。','warning','權限不足');return;}if(!(await showConfirm(`確定要刪除這筆${lookupTypeLabel(type)}資料嗎？刪除後會寫入管理中心 LOG。`,'刪除資料','確定刪除')))return;const col=type==='citizen'?'citizenRecords':type==='vehicles'?'vehicleRecords':'wantedRecords';try{await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,col,id));const list=type==='citizen'?appData.citizenRecords:type==='vehicles'?appData.vehicleRecords:appData.wantedRecords;const idx=list.findIndex(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id));const target=idx>=0?list[idx]:null;if(idx>=0)list.splice(idx,1);await logAdminAction(`刪除${lookupTypeLabel(type)}資料`,id,target?JSON.stringify({name:target.name||target.owner||'',id5:target.id5||''}):'',{discord:true});renderLookupSystems();if(type==='citizen')searchCitizenRecords();if(type==='vehicles')searchVehicleRecords();if(type==='wanted')searchWantedRecords();showToast('資料已從雲端資料庫刪除，並已記錄管理中心 LOG。','success','雲端同步成功');}catch(e){showToast('刪除失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}}

        function getPolicePunishmentDepartment(user = currentUser) {
            const raw = String(user?.policeDivision || '').trim();
            if (!raw) return '';
            if (raw.includes('新北市政府警察局')) return raw;
            return `新北市政府警察局${raw}`;
        }

        function openPunishModal() {
            if (!currentUser) {
                showToast('請先登入系統，才能使用警用獎懲功能。', 'warning', '需要登入');
                return;
            }
            if (!canUsePolicePunishment()) {
                showToast('警用獎懲僅限警察高層與管理處使用。', 'warning', '權限不足');
                return;
            }
            document.getElementById('punish-author-name').value = currentUser.name;
            document.getElementById('punish-author-position').value = currentUser.role === 'admin' ? '系統管理員 / 長官' : '分局長 / 督察組長';
            const punishDepartmentEl = document.getElementById('punish-department');
            if (punishDepartmentEl) punishDepartmentEl.value = getPolicePunishmentDepartment(currentUser);
            const typeEl = document.getElementById('punish-type');
            if (typeEl) typeEl.value = '';
            populatePunishmentItemOptions('punish-item', '');
            document.getElementById('punish-reason').value = '';
            document.getElementById('modal-punishment').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function generateNextPunishmentId(dateObj = new Date()) {
            const yyyy = dateObj.getFullYear();
            const mm = String(dateObj.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
            const dd = String(dateObj.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
            const dateKey = `${yyyy}${mm}${dd}`;
            const prefix = `PUN-${dateKey}`;

            let maxSerial = 0;
            (appData.policePunishments || []).forEach(p => {
                const punishmentId = String(p.id || p.docId || '');
                const match = punishmentId.match(new RegExp(`^${prefix}(\\d+)$`));
                if (match) maxSerial = Math.max(maxSerial, Number(match[1]) || 0);
            });

            return `${prefix}${String(maxSerial + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`;
        }

        async function submitPunishment() {
            if(!canUsePolicePunishment()){showToast('權限不足：警用獎懲僅限警察高層與管理處發布。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            const memberId=document.getElementById('punish-member-id').value.trim(),memberAcc=document.getElementById('punish-member-select').value,type=document.getElementById('punish-type').value,items=getSelectedPunishmentItems('punish-item'),department=(document.getElementById('punish-department').value.trim() || getPolicePunishmentDepartment(currentUser)),authorName=document.getElementById('punish-author-name').value.trim(),authorPos=document.getElementById('punish-author-position').value.trim(),reason=document.getElementById('punish-reason').value.trim();
            if(!['reward','punishment'].includes(type)||!items.length){showToast('請先選擇獎懲類型，並至少勾選一個獎懲項目。','warning','資料不完整');return;}
            if(!memberId||!reason||!authorName){showToast('請填寫完整獎懲資訊。','error','資料不完整');return;}
            const now=new Date(),punishId=generateNextPunishmentId(now),foundU=appData.users.find(u=>u.account===memberAcc),resolvedMemberId=memberId||(foundU?.staffNumber||''),newPunish={id:punishId,memberId:resolvedMemberId,memberAccount:memberAcc||'',memberName:foundU?foundU.name:'未知員警',type,items,item:items.join('、'),department,authorName,authorPos,authorAccount:currentUser.account,authorRole:currentUser.role,reason,time:formatDateTime(now.getTime()),timestamp:now.getTime()};
            try{await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'policePunishments',punishId),newPunish);appData.policePunishments.unshift({docId:punishId,...newPunish});closeModal('modal-punishment');renderPolicePunishments();updateAccessLocks();showToast('警用獎懲公告已成功發布。','success','發布成功');}catch(e){showToast('雲端寫入失敗：'+e.message,'error','發布失敗');}
        }

        function openReportModal(dept) {
            const academyMode = !!academyExamSession && academyExamSession.type === dept;
            if (!currentUser && !academyMode) {
                showToast('請先登入系統。', 'warning', '需要登入');
                return;
            }
            if (currentUser && isSuspendedUser(currentUser.account)) {
                showToast('你目前於停職調查階段，無法使用案件報告功能，直到該獎懲被更改或移除。', 'warning', '停職調查限制');
                return;
            }

            currentActiveDepartmentForReport = dept;
            const titleEl = document.getElementById('report-modal-title');
            if (titleEl) titleEl.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-file-pen ${dept === 'police' ? 'text-blue-500' : 'text-red-500'}"></i><span>填寫${dept === 'police' ? '警察' : '消防'}案件報告</span>`;

            const caseInput = document.getElementById('report-case-id');
            if (caseInput) caseInput.value = academyMode ? generateAcademyCaseId(dept) : generateNextCaseId(dept);
            const reportIdInput = document.getElementById('report-id');
            if (reportIdInput) reportIdInput.value = academyMode ? '警專專生' : (currentUser.staffNumber || currentUser.account);
            const reportAuthorInput = document.getElementById('report-author');
            if (reportAuthorInput) reportAuthorInput.value = academyMode ? '專生考生' : (currentUser.name || '');

            [1,2,3].forEach(n => {
                const el = document.getElementById(`report-video-link-${n}`);
                if (el) el.value = '';
            });
            ['report-postal-code','report-command'].forEach(id => {
                const el=document.getElementById(id);
                if(el) el.value='';
            });
            document.querySelectorAll('#report-support-units input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(cb => cb.checked=false);
            const violationSearch=document.getElementById('report-violation-search');if(violationSearch)violationSearch.value='';const organized=document.getElementById('report-organized-crime');if(organized)organized.checked=false;renderViolationOptions('report');
            const supportSummary=document.getElementById('report-support-summary');
            if(supportSummary) supportSummary.textContent='選擇支援單位';

            const suspectWrap = document.getElementById('police-suspect-fields');
            const extraWrap = document.getElementById('police-report-extra-fields');
            if (suspectWrap) suspectWrap.classList.toggle('hidden', dept !== 'police');
            if (extraWrap) extraWrap.classList.toggle('hidden', dept !== 'police');
            const violationWrap=document.getElementById('police-violation-fields');
            if(violationWrap) violationWrap.classList.toggle('hidden',dept !== 'police');

            if (dept === 'police') {
                ['report-suspect-id','report-suspect-name','report-suspect-vehicle'].forEach(id=>{
                    const el=document.getElementById(id); if(el) el.value='';
                });
            }
            const modal=document.getElementById('modal-report');
            if(modal) modal.classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        async function submitClock() {
            if (window.__clockSubmitting) return;
            window.__clockSubmitting = true;
            const btn=document.getElementById('submit-clock-btn');
            if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.classList.add('is-busy-action');}
            try{
                if(!currentUser){showToast('請先登入系統後再進行打卡。','warning','需要登入');return;}
                const dept=document.getElementById('clock-dept-type').value;
                const isRetro=document.getElementById('clock-is-retro').value==='true';
                if(dept==='police'&&!userHasUnit('police')){showToast('只有警察身分可以建立警務打卡。','warning','權限不足');return;}
                if(dept==='fire'&&!userHasUnit('fire')){showToast('只有消防身分可以建立消防打卡。','warning','權限不足');return;}
                if(isSuspendedUser(currentUser.account)){showToast('你目前於停職調查階段，無法使用值班打卡功能，直到該獎懲被更改或移除。','warning','停職調查限制');return;}
                const idVal=String(currentUser.account||'').trim();
                const nameVal=String(currentUser.name||'').trim();
                const statusVal=document.getElementById('clock-status').value==='下班'?'下班':'上班';
                const vehicleVal=String(document.getElementById('clock-vehicle').value||'').trim().slice(0,80);
                if(!idVal||!nameVal){showToast('目前登入帳號資料不完整，請重新登入。','error','資料錯誤');return;}
                let timeStr='',ts=0;
                if(isRetro){
                    const dateVal=document.getElementById('clock-retro-date').value,timeVal=document.getElementById('clock-retro-time').value;
                    if(!dateVal||!timeVal){showToast('請完整選擇補打卡的日期與時分。','warning','資料不完整');return;}
                    ts=new Date(`${dateVal}T${timeVal}:00`).getTime();
                    if(!Number.isFinite(ts)){showToast('補打卡日期或時間格式無效。','warning','資料錯誤');return;}
                    timeStr=`${dateVal} ${timeVal}:00 (補打卡)`;
                }else{const now=new Date();ts=now.getTime();timeStr=now.toLocaleString('zh-TW',{hour12:false});}
                const clockObj={id:'CLK-'+Date.now(),account:idVal,name:nameVal,status:statusVal,vehicle:vehicleVal||'無',time:timeStr,timestamp:ts,remark:'無'};
                const collectionName=dept==='police'?'policeClocks':'fireClocks';
                await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,collectionName,clockObj.id),clockObj);
                if(dept==='police')appData.policeClocks.unshift({docId:clockObj.id,...clockObj});else appData.fireClocks.unshift({docId:clockObj.id,...clockObj});
                closeModal('modal-clock');
                if(dept==='police')renderPoliceClocks();else renderFireClocks();
                renderAdminOverview();renderMyProfile();
                showSubmitSuccessAnimation('打卡成功',`${statusVal}打卡已完成。`);
            }catch(e){showToast('打卡寫入失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}
            finally{window.__clockSubmitting=false;if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.classList.remove('is-busy-action');}}
        }

        function generateNextCaseId(dept = currentActiveDepartmentForReport) {
            const now = new Date();
            const yyyy = now.getFullYear();
            const mm = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
            const dd = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
            const dateKey = `${yyyy}${mm}${dd}`;
            const prefix = dept === 'police' ? `NTPD-${dateKey}` : `NTFD-${dateKey}`;

            // 警察、消防各自使用獨立流水號：NTPD-YYYYMMDD001 / NTFD-YYYYMMDD001。
            const reports = dept === 'police' ? (appData.policeReports || []) : (appData.fireReports || []);
            const pending = dept === 'police' ? (appData.policePendingReports || []) : (appData.firePendingReports || []);
            const used = new Set();
            [...reports, ...pending].forEach(report => {
                const caseId = String(report.caseId || '');
                const match = caseId.match(new RegExp(`^${prefix}(\\d+)$`));
                if (match) used.add(Number(match[1]) || 0);
            });
            let next = 1;
            while (used.has(next)) next++;
            return `${prefix}${String(next).padStart(3, '0')}`;
        }

        async function renumberDailyCaseReports(dateKey, dept) {
            if (dept !== 'police' && dept !== 'fire') return new Map();
            const prefix = dept === 'police' ? `NTPD-${dateKey}` : `NTFD-${dateKey}`;
            const finalCollection = dept === 'police' ? 'policeReports' : 'fireReports';
            const pendingCollection = dept === 'police' ? 'policePendingReports' : 'firePendingReports';
            const finalList = dept === 'police' ? (appData.policeReports || []) : (appData.fireReports || []);
            const pendingList = dept === 'police' ? (appData.policePendingReports || []) : (appData.firePendingReports || []);
            const rows=[];
            finalList.filter(r=>String(r.caseId||'').startsWith(prefix)).forEach(r=>rows.push({...r,__collection:finalCollection,__oldId:String(r.caseId),__pending:false}));
            pendingList.filter(r=>String(r.caseId||'').startsWith(prefix)).forEach(r=>rows.push({...r,__collection:pendingCollection,__oldId:String(r.caseId),__pending:true}));
            rows.sort((a,b)=>{
                const na=Number(String(a.__oldId).slice(prefix.length))||0, nb=Number(String(b.__oldId).slice(prefix.length))||0;
                return na-nb || Number(a.timestamp||a.registeredAt||a.submittedAt||0)-Number(b.timestamp||b.registeredAt||b.submittedAt||0);
            });
            const mapping=new Map();
            rows.forEach((r,i)=>{const next=`${prefix}${String(i+1).padStart(3,'0')}`;if(r.__oldId!==next)mapping.set(r.__oldId,next);});
            if(!mapping.size)return mapping;
            const changed=rows.filter(r=>mapping.has(r.__oldId));
            const token=`__REN_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`;
            const staged=[];
            try{
                // 先暫存，避免 003→002、002→001 時與舊文件撞名。
                for(const r of changed){
                    const tempId=`${token}_${dept}_${r.__pending?'pending':'final'}_${r.__oldId}`;
                    const copy={...r};delete copy.__collection;delete copy.__oldId;delete copy.__pending;
                    await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,r.__collection,tempId),{...copy,caseId:tempId});
                    staged.push({r,tempId});
                }
                for(const r of changed) await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,r.__collection,r.__oldId));
                for(const item of staged){
                    const newId=mapping.get(item.r.__oldId),copy={...item.r};delete copy.__collection;delete copy.__oldId;delete copy.__pending;
                    await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,item.r.__collection,newId),{...copy,caseId:newId});
                    await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,item.r.__collection,item.tempId));
                }
                [...finalList,...pendingList].forEach(r=>{const n=mapping.get(String(r.caseId||''));if(n)r.caseId=n;});
                for(const n of (appData.reportNotifications||[])){
                    const mapped=mapping.get(String(n.caseId||''));
                    if(!mapped)continue;
                    const oldId=String(n.caseId);
                    const patch={caseId:mapped};
                    if(String(n.message||'').includes(oldId))patch.message=String(n.message).replaceAll(oldId,mapped);
                    try{await fm.updateDoc(fm.doc(db,'reportNotifications',n.docId),patch);Object.assign(n,patch);}catch(_){}
                }
                return mapping;
            }catch(e){
                for(const item of staged){try{await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,item.r.__collection,item.tempId));}catch(_){} }
                throw e;
            }
        }
        const POLICE_VIOLATION_DEFAULTS=[['故意殺人',45],['劫獄',30],['襲擊公務人員',30],['貪污',30],['搶劫銀行',15],['妨害公務',7],['搶劫珠寶',7],['持有毒品',7],['搶劫超商',5],['損害物品',5],['非法槍枝',5],['盜竊車輛',5],['搶劫保險箱',5],['盜竊房屋',5],['盜竊ATM',5],['假冒公務員',3],['公共危險',3],['公然侮辱',3],['故意傷害',3],['社會秩序',3],['其餘',3]];
        let POLICE_VIOLATION_RULES=POLICE_VIOLATION_DEFAULTS.map(x=>[...x]);
        function formatJailMinutes(minutes){const n=Number(minutes||0);return `${Number.isInteger(n)?n:n.toFixed(1)} 分鐘`;}
        function renderAdminJailRules(){
            const host=document.getElementById('admin-jail-rules-list'); if(!host)return;
            host.innerHTML=POLICE_VIOLATION_RULES.map(([name,min],i)=>`<div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_150px_auto] gap-2 items-center bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3"><input id="jail-rule-name-${i}" value="${name.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white"><input id="jail-rule-min-${i}" type="number" min="0" step="0.1" value="${min}" class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white"><button type="button" onclick="deleteJailRule(${i})" class="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-red-600/10 text-red-400 border border-red-500/20 text-xs">刪除</button></div>`).join('');
        }
        function deleteJailRule(index){if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='admin'){showToast('只有最高管理員可以修改刑期表。','warning','權限不足');return;}POLICE_VIOLATION_RULES.splice(index,1);renderAdminJailRules();}
        function addJailRule(){if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='admin'){showToast('只有最高管理員可以修改刑期表。','warning','權限不足');return;}const name=document.getElementById('new-jail-rule-name')?.value.trim(),minutes=Number(document.getElementById('new-jail-rule-minutes')?.value);if(!name||!Number.isFinite(minutes)||minutes<0){showToast('請填寫條例名稱與有效刑期。','warning','資料不完整');return;}if(POLICE_VIOLATION_RULES.some(x=>x[0]===name)){showToast('此條例已存在。','warning','無法新增');return;}POLICE_VIOLATION_RULES.push([name,minutes]);document.getElementById('new-jail-rule-name').value='';document.getElementById('new-jail-rule-minutes').value='';renderAdminJailRules();}
        async function saveJailRules(){if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='admin'){showToast('只有最高管理員可以更新刑期表。','warning','權限不足');return;}const next=POLICE_VIOLATION_RULES.map((_,i)=>[(document.getElementById(`jail-rule-name-${i}`)?.value||'').trim(),Number(document.getElementById(`jail-rule-min-${i}`)?.value)]).filter(x=>x[0]&&Number.isFinite(x[1])&&x[1]>=0);if(!next.length){showToast('至少保留一筆有效刑期規則。','warning','資料錯誤');return;}const seen=new Set();for(const x of next){if(seen.has(x[0])){showToast(`條例「${x[0]}」重複。`,'warning','資料錯誤');return;}seen.add(x[0]);}try{await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'systemSettings','policeViolationRules'),{rules:next,updatedAt:Date.now(),updatedBy:currentUser.account});POLICE_VIOLATION_RULES=next;appData.policeViolationRules=next.map(x=>[...x]);renderAdminJailRules();if(document.getElementById('modal-report')&&!document.getElementById('modal-report').classList.contains('hidden'))renderViolationOptions('report',getViolationSelection('report').violations);showSubmitSuccessAnimation('刑期表更新成功','警用案件刑期規則已更新。');}catch(e){showToast('刑期表更新失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}}


        function calculatePoliceJailTime(violations,organizedCrime){const base=(Array.isArray(violations)?violations:[]).reduce((sum,name)=>{const row=POLICE_VIOLATION_RULES.find(x=>x[0]===name);return sum+(row?row[1]:0);},0);return Math.round((organizedCrime?base*4/3:base)*10)/10;}
        function renderViolationOptions(prefix='report',selected=[]){const host=document.getElementById(`${prefix}-violation-options`);if(!host)return;const set=new Set(Array.isArray(selected)?selected:[]);host.innerHTML=POLICE_VIOLATION_RULES.map(([name,min])=>`<label data-violation-name="${name.toLowerCase()}" class="violation-option flex items-center justify-between gap-2 min-h-[44px] p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/70 cursor-pointer"><span class="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0"><input type="checkbox" name="${prefix}-violation" value="${name}" ${set.has(name)?'checked':''} onchange="updateViolationJailTotal('${prefix}')" class="w-4 h-4 accent-amber-500"><span class="text-sm text-slate-200 truncate">${name}</span></span><span class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 shrink-0">${min}min</span></label>`).join('');updateViolationJailTotal(prefix);}
        function filterViolationOptions(prefix='report'){const q=(document.getElementById(`${prefix}-violation-search`)?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();document.querySelectorAll(`#${prefix}-violation-options .violation-option`).forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('hidden',!!q&&!el.dataset.violationName.includes(q)));}
        function updateViolationJailTotal(prefix='report'){const violations=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(`input[name="${prefix}-violation"]:checked`)).map(x=>x.value),organized=!!document.getElementById(`${prefix}-organized-crime`)?.checked,total=calculatePoliceJailTime(violations,organized),out=document.getElementById(`${prefix}-jail-total`);if(out)out.textContent=`總計 ${formatJailMinutes(total)}`;return {violations,organizedCrime:organized,jailMinutes:total};}
        function getViolationSelection(prefix='report'){return updateViolationJailTotal(prefix);}


        // Discord 案件通知：警察與消防／EMS 使用不同 Webhook。
        // 使用隱藏表單 POST，避免 GitHub Pages 前端因跨來源 CORS 預檢而導致通知無法送出。
        const NTPC_RP_DISCORD_POLICE_WEBHOOK_URL = "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1556813074176933938/m8-oi0s9rD0MDb3X5Xy3rEjUrdQ0ZYA6v2z6UTiDILFGQMSliGltIjbegmzNTtsEtQJQ";
        const NTPC_RP_DISCORD_FIRE_WEBHOOK_URL = "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1556959309265371155/xXOkB8yRJUn-mka2PP3GUKzsPoddI43aOit9SStBEU6zIAcGExiWOd-4lVgLtIWoHMns";
        // 管理中心 LOG 專用 Webhook：編輯／退件／刪除與裝置自動封鎖事件。
        const NTPC_RP_DISCORD_ADMIN_LOG_WEBHOOK_URL = "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1556969901409964052/iFj6S2juQ6Fas9ms0Lc_R4Ak9ExtmaM-gbkc_SZbqpxYJTWzY393wCOXa0PdQdlQRcgi";
        const NTPC_RP_DISCORD_ADMIN_LOG_ROLE_ID = '1434850635894755409';
        const NTPC_RP_SITE_URL = 'https://gken99903-source.github.io/-NTPC-RP-/';

        function escapeDiscordText(value) {
            return String(value ?? '--').replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/`/g, '\\`');
        }

        // Discord Embed 單一欄位 value 最多 1024 字元；案件內容可能由使用者自由輸入，
        // 因此所有動態欄位都先截斷，避免整個 webhook payload 因單一欄位過長而被 Discord 拒絕。
        function discordFieldValue(value, max=1024) {
            const text = escapeDiscordText(value);
            return text.length > max ? text.slice(0, Math.max(0, max - 1)) + '…' : (text || '--');
        }
        function discordField(name, value, inline=false) {
            return {name: String(name).slice(0,256), value: discordFieldValue(value,1024), inline: !!inline};
        }

        function buildDiscordCasePayload(department, report) {
            const isPolice = department === 'police';
            const isEMS = department === 'ems';
            const title = isEMS ? '🚑 新案件｜EMS 救護報告' : isPolice ? '🚓 新案件｜警察案件報告' : '🚒 新案件｜消防案件報告';
            const fields = [];
            if (isEMS) {
                fields.push(
                    discordField('案件編號', report.id || report.caseId || report.docId, true),
                    discordField('填寫人', `${report.authorName || '--'} (${report.authorId || '--'})`, true),
                    discordField('主訴', report.chiefComplaint, false),
                    discordField('傷患', report.patient, true),
                    discordField('出勤單位／車輛', report.unitVehicle, true),
                    discordField('治療／處置', report.treatment, false)
                );
            } else {
                fields.push(
                    discordField('案件編號', report.caseId, true),
                    discordField('填寫人', `${report.authorName || '--'} (${report.authorId || '--'})`, true),
                    discordField('案件標題', report.title, false)
                );
                if (isPolice) {
                    const crimes = Array.isArray(report.violations) && report.violations.length ? report.violations.join('、') : '未填寫';
                    fields.push(
                        discordField('嫌疑人', `${report.suspectName || '無'} / ${report.suspectId || '無'}`, true),
                        discordField('違反條例', crimes, false),
                        discordField('總監獄時間', formatJailMinutes(Number(report.jailMinutes || 0)), true),
                        discordField('組織犯罪加重⅓', report.organizedCrime ? '是' : '否', true)
                    );
                }
                fields.push(discordField('案件內容', report.content, false));
            }
            fields.push(discordField('勤務網站', `[點我前往勤務網站](${NTPC_RP_SITE_URL})`, false));
            return {
                username: '新北市 RP 3.0 案件通知',
                allowed_mentions: {parse: []},
                embeds: [{
                    title: String(title).slice(0,256),
                    description: '案件已成功寫入雲端資料庫，等待高層審核。',
                    color: isPolice ? 0x2563eb : isEMS ? 0xf43f5e : 0xdc2626,
                    fields: fields.slice(0,25),
                    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
                    footer: { text: '新北市 RP 3.0 公職服務整合系統 V1.8' },
                    url: NTPC_RP_SITE_URL
                }]
            };
        }

        // Discord 案件通知採雙通道：先嘗試 JSON POST；若瀏覽器因 CORS 阻擋，
        // 再自動改用原生 multipart/form-data 隱藏表單。兩者只會成功一次。
        async function sendDiscordCaseNotification(department, report) {
            const webhookUrl = department === 'police' ? NTPC_RP_DISCORD_POLICE_WEBHOOK_URL : NTPC_RP_DISCORD_FIRE_WEBHOOK_URL;
            if (!webhookUrl || !report) return false;
            const payload = buildDiscordCasePayload(department, report);
            let directSucceeded = false;

            try {
                const response = await fetch(webhookUrl + (webhookUrl.includes('?') ? '&' : '?') + 'wait=true', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
                    mode: 'cors',
                    credentials: 'omit'
                });
                if (response.ok) directSucceeded = true;
            } catch (e) {
                console.warn('Discord JSON POST 被瀏覽器阻擋，改用表單備援：', e);
            }
            if (directSucceeded) return true;

            try {
                const targetName = `ntpc_discord_case_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`;
                const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
                iframe.name = targetName;
                iframe.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;width:0;height:0;border:0;visibility:hidden;';
                document.body.appendChild(iframe);
                const form = document.createElement('form');
                form.method = 'POST';
                form.action = webhookUrl + (webhookUrl.includes('?') ? '&' : '?') + 'wait=false';
                form.target = targetName;
                form.enctype = 'multipart/form-data';
                form.encoding = 'multipart/form-data';
                form.style.display = 'none';
                const input = document.createElement('input');
                input.type = 'hidden';
                input.name = 'payload_json';
                input.value = JSON.stringify(payload);
                form.appendChild(input);
                document.body.appendChild(form);
                form.submit();
                setTimeout(() => { try { form.remove(); iframe.remove(); } catch (_) {} }, 10000);
                return true;
            } catch (e) {
                console.warn('Discord 案件通知備援發送失敗：', e);
                return false;
            }
        }

        function sendDiscordWantedNotification(wanted){
            const webhookUrl=NTPC_RP_DISCORD_POLICE_WEBHOOK_URL;
            if(!webhookUrl)return;
            try{
                const targetName=`ntpc_discord_wanted_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`;
                const iframe=document.createElement('iframe'); iframe.name=targetName; iframe.style.cssText='position:absolute;width:0;height:0;border:0;visibility:hidden;'; document.body.appendChild(iframe);
                const form=document.createElement('form'); form.method='POST'; form.action=webhookUrl+(webhookUrl.includes('?')?'&':'?')+'wait=false'; form.target=targetName; form.enctype='application/x-www-form-urlencoded'; form.style.display='none';
                const payload={username:'新北市 RP 3.0 通緝申請通知',embeds:[{title:'🚨 新申請｜警察通緝資料',description:'通緝申請已成功寫入雲端資料庫，等待警察高層審核。',color:0xe11d48,fields:[
                    {name:'被通緝人',value:escapeDiscordText(wanted.name),inline:true},
                    {name:'證件末五碼',value:escapeDiscordText(wanted.id5),inline:true},
                    {name:'發布帳號',value:escapeDiscordText(`${wanted.createdByName||'--'} (${wanted.createdByAccount||'--'})`),inline:true},
                    {name:'發布單位',value:escapeDiscordText(wanted.publishUnit||wanted.department||'--'),inline:true},
                    {name:'通緝原因',value:escapeDiscordText(wanted.reason),inline:false},
                    {name:'所違反條例',value:escapeDiscordText(wanted.law),inline:false},
                    {name:'勤務網站',value:`[點我前往勤務網站](${NTPC_RP_SITE_URL})`,inline:false}
                ],timestamp:new Date().toISOString(),footer:{text:'新北市 RP 3.0 公職服務整合系統 V1.8'},url:NTPC_RP_SITE_URL}]};
                const input=document.createElement('input'); input.type='hidden'; input.name='payload_json'; input.value=JSON.stringify(payload); form.appendChild(input); document.body.appendChild(form); form.submit(); setTimeout(()=>{try{form.remove();iframe.remove();}catch(_){ }},8000);
            }catch(e){console.warn('Discord 通緝申請通知發送失敗：',e);}
        }


        function sendManagementDiscordLog(action, target, detail='', options={}) {
            const webhookUrl = NTPC_RP_DISCORD_ADMIN_LOG_WEBHOOK_URL;
            if (!webhookUrl) return;
            try {
                const mentionRole = !!options.mentionRole;
                const operator = currentUser ? `${currentUser.name || '未知'} (${currentUser.account || '--'})` : '系統自動處理';
                const payload = {
                    username: '新北市 RP 3.0 管理中心 LOG',
                    ...(mentionRole ? {content:`<@&${NTPC_RP_DISCORD_ADMIN_LOG_ROLE_ID}>`} : {}),
                    allowed_mentions: mentionRole ? {roles:[NTPC_RP_DISCORD_ADMIN_LOG_ROLE_ID]} : {parse:[]},
                    embeds: [{
                        title: `🛡️ 管理中心操作紀錄｜${action}`,
                        color: mentionRole ? 0xf59e0b : 0x6366f1,
                        fields: [
                            {name:'操作人員', value:escapeDiscordText(operator).slice(0,1024), inline:true},
                            {name:'操作類型', value:escapeDiscordText(action).slice(0,1024), inline:true},
                            {name:'目標', value:escapeDiscordText(target).slice(0,1024), inline:false},
                            ...(detail ? [{name:'詳細資訊', value:escapeDiscordText(detail).slice(0,1024), inline:false}] : [])
                        ],
                        timestamp:new Date().toISOString(),
                        footer:{text:'新北市 RP 3.0 公職服務整合系統 V1.8'},
                        url:NTPC_RP_SITE_URL
                    }]
                };
                const targetName = `ntpc_admin_log_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`;
                const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
                iframe.name = targetName;
                iframe.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;width:0;height:0;border:0;visibility:hidden;';
                document.body.appendChild(iframe);
                const form = document.createElement('form');
                form.method='POST';
                form.action=webhookUrl + (webhookUrl.includes('?')?'&':'?') + 'wait=false';
                form.target=targetName;
                form.enctype='application/x-www-form-urlencoded';
                form.style.display='none';
                const input=document.createElement('input');
                input.type='hidden';
                input.name='payload_json';
                input.value=JSON.stringify(payload);
                form.appendChild(input);
                document.body.appendChild(form);
                form.submit();
                setTimeout(()=>{try{form.remove();iframe.remove();}catch(_){ }},8000);
            } catch(e) {
                console.warn('管理中心 Discord LOG 發送失敗：',e);
            }
        }

        async function submitReport() {
            const academyMode = !!academyExamSession && academyExamSession.type === currentActiveDepartmentForReport;
            if (!currentUser && !academyMode) {
                showToast('請先登入系統。', 'warning', '需要登入');
                return;
            }
            if (currentUser && isSuspendedUser(currentUser.account)) {
                showToast('你目前於停職調查階段，無法提交案件報告，直到該獎懲被更改或移除。', 'warning', '停職調查限制');
                return;
            }

            const dept = currentActiveDepartmentForReport;
            const authorId = document.getElementById('report-id').value.trim();
            const authorName = document.getElementById('report-author').value.trim();
            const title = document.getElementById('report-title').value.trim();
            const content = document.getElementById('report-content').value.trim();
            if (!authorId || !title || !content) {
                showToast('請完整填寫案件必要欄位。', 'error', '資料不完整');
                return;
            }

            const now = new Date();
            const caseId = generateNextCaseId(dept);
            const videoLinks = [1,2,3].map(n => document.getElementById(`report-video-link-${n}`)?.value.trim()).filter(Boolean);
            const violationData = dept === 'police' ? getViolationSelection('report') : {violations:[], organizedCrime:false, jailMinutes:0};
            // 警察刑期／違反條例為選填，未選擇時以 0 分鐘儲存，不阻擋案件送出。
            const reportObj = {
                caseId,
                authorId,
                authorName,
                // 原始案件填寫者固定保存；後續編輯只更新 lastEditedBy，不覆蓋原始人員。
                createdByAccount: academyMode ? `ACADEMY:${academyExamSession.code}` : currentUser.account,
                createdByName: academyMode ? '警專專生' : (currentUser.name || authorName),
                registeredByAccount: academyMode ? `ACADEMY:${academyExamSession.code}` : currentUser.account,
                registeredByName: academyMode ? '警專專生' : (currentUser.name || authorName),
                registeredAt: now.getTime(),
                suspectId: dept === 'police' ? (document.getElementById('report-suspect-id')?.value.trim() || '無') : '',
                suspectName: dept === 'police' ? (document.getElementById('report-suspect-name')?.value.trim() || '無') : '',
                suspectVehicle: dept === 'police' ? (document.getElementById('report-suspect-vehicle')?.value.trim() || '無') : '',
                postalCode: dept === 'police' ? (document.getElementById('report-postal-code')?.value.trim() || '') : '',
                command: dept === 'police' ? (document.getElementById('report-command')?.value.trim() || '') : '',
                supportUnits: dept === 'police' ? Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#report-support-units input[name="report-support-unit"]:checked')).map(cb => cb.value) : [],
                ...(dept === 'police' ? {violations:violationData.violations, organizedCrime:violationData.organizedCrime, jailMinutes:violationData.jailMinutes} : {}),
                title,
                content,
                videoLinks,
                videoLink: videoLinks[0] || '',
                time: formatDateTime(now.getTime()),
                timestamp: now.getTime(),
                remark: '尚未批示',
                remarks: []
            };

            if (academyMode) {
                reportObj.caseId = caseId;
                reportObj.timestamp = Date.now();
                reportObj.time = formatDateTime(reportObj.timestamp);
                const target = dept === 'police' ? academyExamCache.policeReports : academyExamCache.fireReports;
                target.unshift(reportObj);
                saveAcademyCache();
                closeModal('modal-report');
                renderAcademyCache();
                showSubmitSuccessAnimation('考核案件送出成功', `${caseId} 已保留於本次瀏覽器快取。`);
                return;
            }

            const collectionName = dept === 'police' ? 'policePendingReports' : 'firePendingReports';
            try {
                if (dept === 'police' || dept === 'fire') {
                    reportObj.reviewStatus = 'pending';
                    reportObj.reviewedAt = 0;
                    reportObj.reviewedBy = '';
                    reportObj.rejectionReason = '';
                    reportObj.submittedAt = now.getTime();
                    await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db, collectionName, caseId), reportObj);
                    const pendingTarget = dept === 'police' ? appData.policePendingReports : appData.firePendingReports;
                    pendingTarget.splice(0, pendingTarget.length, ...getUniquePendingRows([{docId: caseId, ...reportObj}, ...pendingTarget], r => r.caseId || r.docId));
                    const deptName = dept === 'police' ? '警局' : '消防局';
                    const notification = { type:'report', department:dept, caseId, recipientAccount:currentUser.account, recipientAccountNormalized:String(currentUser.account||'').trim().toLowerCase(), status:'pending', title:'案件已送出，等待高層審核', message:`案件 ${caseId} 已送入${deptName}高層審核系統。`, timestamp:Date.now(), read:false };
                    const nref = fm.doc(fm.collection(db,'reportNotifications'));
                    await fm.setDoc(nref, notification);
                    appData.reportNotifications.unshift({docId:nref.id,...notification});
                    // Firestore 寫入成功後才發送 Discord 案件通知，避免資料庫失敗卻先通知。
                    sendDiscordCaseNotification(dept, reportObj);
                    closeModal('modal-report');
                    if(dept==='police'){ renderPoliceReports(); renderPoliceReview(); } else { renderFireReports(); renderFireReview(); }
                    renderMyProfile();
                    showSubmitSuccessAnimation('案件送出成功', `案件 ${caseId} 已送出，等待${deptName}高層或管理中心審核。`);
                }
            } catch (e) {
                showToast('提交失敗：' + e.message, 'error', '操作失敗');
            }
        }

        function getReportRemarks(report) {
            return ensureReportRemarks(report);
        }

        function getLatestRemarkText(report) {
            const remarks = getReportRemarks(report);
            if (!remarks.length) return '尚未批示';
            const last = remarks[remarks.length - 1];
            return `${last.authorName || '長官'}：${last.text || ''}`;
        }

        function renderRemarkHistory(report) {
            const history = document.getElementById('remark-history');
            if (!history) return;
            const remarks = getReportRemarks(report);
            if (!remarks.length) {
                history.innerHTML = '<div class="text-xs text-slate-500 text-center py-3">目前尚無長官批示。</div>';
                return;
            }
            history.innerHTML = remarks.map((remark, index) => `
                <div class="rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/70 p-3">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-1">
                        <span class="text-xs font-semibold text-amber-400">${remark.authorName || '長官'} ${remark.authorAccount ? `(${remark.authorAccount})` : ''}</span>
                        <span class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">${remark.time || formatDateTime(remark.timestamp)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="text-xs text-slate-300 whitespace-pre-wrap">${remark.text || ''}</div>
                </div>`).join('');
        }

        function canReviewFireReports(){ return !!currentUser && (currentUser.role==='admin' || ((currentUser.role==='high' || currentUser.role==='senior') && userHasUnit('fire'))); }

        async function approveEMSPendingReport(id){if(!canReviewFireReports()){showToast('只有消防高層、高階成員與管理中心可以審核 EMS 報告。','warning','權限不足');return;}const r=(appData.emsPendingReports||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id));if(!r)return;try{const shared={...r,reviewStatus:'approved',reviewedAt:Date.now(),reviewedBy:currentUser.account};delete shared.rejectionReason;const finalDocId=await resolveReportDocId('emsReports',r,id);const pendingDocId=await resolveReportDocId('emsPendingReports',r,id);await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'emsReports',finalDocId),shared);await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'emsPendingReports',pendingDocId));appData.emsReports.unshift({docId:finalDocId,...shared});appData.emsPendingReports=appData.emsPendingReports.filter(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)!==String(id));await removePendingReportNotifications(id,r.registeredByAccount||r.authorId);const n={type:'report',department:'fire',caseId:id,recipientAccount:r.registeredByAccount||r.authorId,status:'approved',title:'EMS報告審核完成',message:`EMS報告 ${id} 已經消防高層審核完畢並正式送出。`,timestamp:Date.now(),read:false};const nr=fm.doc(fm.collection(db,'reportNotifications'));await fm.setDoc(nr,n);appData.reportNotifications.unshift({docId:nr.id,...n});await logAdminAction('EMS報告消防高層審核完畢並送出',id);renderFireReview();renderEMSReports();renderPersonalNotifications();renderMyProfile();showSubmitSuccessAnimation('EMS審核完成','EMS報告已審核完畢並同步至共享 EMS 報告庫。');}catch(e){showToast('EMS審核送出失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}}
        function openRejectEMSPending(id){if(!canReviewFireReports()){showToast('只有消防高層、高階成員與管理中心可以退件 EMS 報告。','warning','權限不足');return;}document.getElementById('review-reject-case-id').value=id;document.getElementById('review-reject-dept').value='ems';document.getElementById('review-reject-reason').value='';document.getElementById('modal-review-reject').classList.remove('hidden');}
        function viewPendingFireReportDetail(caseId){ const r=(appData.firePendingReports||[]).find(x=>x.caseId===caseId); if(!r)return; viewReportDetailFromObject('fire',r,true,'fire'); }
        async function approveFirePendingReport(caseId){ if(!canReviewFireReports()){showToast('只有消防高層、高階成員與管理中心可以審核案件。','warning','權限不足');return;} const r=(appData.firePendingReports||[]).find(x=>x.caseId===caseId); if(!r)return; try{const shared={...r,reviewStatus:'approved',reviewedAt:Date.now(),reviewedBy:currentUser.account};delete shared.rejectionReason;const finalDocId=await resolveReportDocId('fireReports',r,caseId);const pendingDocId=await resolveReportDocId('firePendingReports',r,caseId);await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'fireReports',finalDocId),shared);await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'firePendingReports',pendingDocId));appData.fireReports.unshift({docId:finalDocId,...shared});appData.firePendingReports=appData.firePendingReports.filter(x=>x.caseId!==caseId);await removePendingReportNotifications(caseId,r.registeredByAccount);const n={type:'report',department:'fire',caseId,recipientAccount:r.registeredByAccount,recipientAccountNormalized:String(r.registeredByAccount||'').trim().toLowerCase(),status:'approved',title:'消防案件已審核完畢',message:`消防案件 ${caseId} 已經高層審核完畢並正式送出。`,timestamp:Date.now(),read:false};const nr=fm.doc(fm.collection(db,'reportNotifications'));await fm.setDoc(nr,n);appData.reportNotifications.unshift({docId:nr.id,...n});await logAdminAction('消防案件審核完畢並送出',caseId);renderFireReview();renderFireReports();renderMyProfile();showSubmitSuccessAnimation('審核完成','消防案件已審核完畢並同步至共享消防案件報告庫。');}catch(e){showToast('審核送出失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}}

                function canReviewPoliceReports(){ return !!currentUser && (currentUser.role==='admin' || ((currentUser.role==='high' || currentUser.role==='senior') && userHasUnit('police'))); }
        function canEditReviewStatus(dept){
            if(!currentUser)return false;
            if(currentUser.role==='admin')return true;
            return currentUser.role==='high' && userHasUnit(dept==='police'?'police':'fire');
        }
        function openEditReviewStatus(dept, caseId, currentStatus='pending'){
            if(!canEditReviewStatus(dept)){showToast('只有該單位高層與管理中心可以編輯審核狀況。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            const id=String(caseId||'');
            const lists=dept==='police'?[appData.policeReports,appData.policePendingReports]:dept==='fire'?[appData.fireReports,appData.firePendingReports]:[appData.emsReports,appData.emsPendingReports];
            const r=[...(lists[0]||[]),...(lists[1]||[])].find(x=>String(x.caseId||x.id||x.docId)===id);
            if(!r){showToast('找不到案件資料。','error','資料不存在');return;}
            document.getElementById('edit-review-status-case-id').value=id; document.getElementById('edit-review-status-dept').value=dept; document.getElementById('edit-review-status-case-label').textContent=id;
            document.getElementById('edit-review-status-value').value=r.reviewStatus||currentStatus||'pending'; document.getElementById('edit-review-status-reason').value=r.rejectionReason||'';
            document.getElementById('modal-edit-review-status').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        async function resolveReportDocId(collectionName, record, key){
            const direct = String(record?.docId || '').trim();
            const fallback = String(key || record?.caseId || record?.id || '').trim();
            if (!direct && !fallback) return '';
            // 先確認記憶中的 docId 是否仍存在，避免案件文件被移動／重建後繼續使用舊 ID。
            if (direct) {
                try {
                    const directSnap = await fm.getDoc(fm.doc(db, collectionName, direct));
                    if (directSnap.exists()) return direct;
                } catch (e) { console.warn('resolveReportDocId direct lookup failed', collectionName, direct, e); }
            }
            if (fallback) {
                try {
                    const snap = await fm.getDocs(fm.query(fm.collection(db, collectionName), fm.where('caseId','==',fallback)));
                    if (!snap.empty) return snap.docs[0].id;
                } catch (e) { console.warn('resolveReportDocId caseId lookup failed', collectionName, fallback, e); }
            }
            // 找不到舊文件時回傳原 docId（若有）或案件編號，讓後續 setDoc({merge:true}) 可以安全補回文件。
            return direct || fallback;
        }

        async function saveEditedReviewStatus(){
            const id=String(document.getElementById('edit-review-status-case-id').value||''), dept=document.getElementById('edit-review-status-dept').value||'police', status=document.getElementById('edit-review-status-value').value||'pending', reason=document.getElementById('edit-review-status-reason').value.trim();
            if(!canEditReviewStatus(dept)){showToast('權限不足。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            if(!id){showToast('找不到案件編號。','error','資料錯誤');return;}
            if(status==='rejected'&&!reason){showToast('改為退件時必須填寫退件原因。','warning','退件原因必填');return;}
            const pendingCol=dept==='police'?'policePendingReports':dept==='fire'?'firePendingReports':'emsPendingReports', reportCol=dept==='police'?'policeReports':dept==='fire'?'fireReports':'emsReports';
            const pendingList=appData[pendingCol]||[], reportList=appData[reportCol]||[];
            const p=pendingList.find(x=>String(x.caseId||x.id||x.docId)===id), a=reportList.find(x=>String(x.caseId||x.id||x.docId)===id), r=p||a;
            if(!r){showToast('找不到案件資料。','error','資料不存在');return;}
            try{
                const now=Date.now(), base={...r,reviewStatus:status,reviewedAt:now,reviewedBy:currentUser.account,updatedAt:now};
                if(status==='rejected')base.rejectionReason=reason; else delete base.rejectionReason;
                if(status==='approved'){
                    const sourcePendingDocId = p ? await resolveReportDocId(pendingCol,p,id) : '';
                    const targetReportDocId = a ? await resolveReportDocId(reportCol,a,id) : (p ? (await resolveReportDocId(pendingCol,p,id)) : id);
                    await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,reportCol,targetReportDocId),base);
                    if(p && sourcePendingDocId) await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,pendingCol,sourcePendingDocId));
                    appData[reportCol]=[{docId:targetReportDocId,...base},...reportList.filter(x=>String(x.caseId||x.id||x.docId)!==id)];
                    appData[pendingCol]=pendingList.filter(x=>String(x.caseId||x.id||x.docId)!==id);
                }else{
                    const targetPendingDocId = p ? await resolveReportDocId(pendingCol,p,id) : id;
                    const sourceReportDocId = a ? await resolveReportDocId(reportCol,a,id) : '';
                    await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,pendingCol,targetPendingDocId),base);
                    if(a && sourceReportDocId) await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,reportCol,sourceReportDocId));
                    appData[pendingCol]=[{docId:targetPendingDocId,...base},...pendingList.filter(x=>String(x.caseId||x.id||x.docId)!==id)];
                    appData[reportCol]=reportList.filter(x=>String(x.caseId||x.id||x.docId)!==id);
                }
                await removePendingReportNotifications(id,base.registeredByAccount||base.authorId);
                const recipient=base.registeredByAccount||base.authorId, label=dept==='ems'?'EMS報告':'案件';
                const n={type:'report',department:dept==='police'?'police':'fire',caseId:id,recipientAccount:recipient,recipientAccountNormalized:String(recipient||'').trim().toLowerCase(),status,title:status==='approved'?`${label}審核完成`:status==='rejected'?`${label}遭退件`:`${label}重新進入審核`,message:status==='approved'?`${label} ${id} 已由高層重新確認為「通過」。`:status==='rejected'?`${label} ${id} 已由高層重新確認為「退件」：${reason}`:`${label} ${id} 已重新調整為「待審核」，等待高層審核。`,timestamp:now,read:false};
                const nr=fm.doc(fm.collection(db,'reportNotifications')); await fm.setDoc(nr,n); appData.reportNotifications.unshift({docId:nr.id,...n});
                await logAdminAction((dept==='ems'?'EMS':dept==='fire'?'消防':'警用')+'編輯案件審核狀況',id);
                closeModal('modal-edit-review-status'); renderPersonalNotifications(); renderMyProfile(); renderPoliceReview(); renderFireReview(); renderPoliceReports(); renderFireReports(); renderEMSReports();
                showSubmitSuccessAnimation('審核狀況已更新',`案件 ${id} 已改為「${status==='approved'?'通過':status==='rejected'?'退件':'待審核'}」。`);
            }catch(e){showToast('更新審核狀況失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}
        }

        }
        async function approveWantedPending(id){

                await logAdminAction('通緝申請警察高層審核通過',id,`被通緝人：${r.name||''}；發布單位：${r.publishUnit||r.department||''}`);
                closeModal('modal-lookup-detail'); renderPoliceReview(); renderLookupSystems(); renderPersonalNotifications(); renderMyProfile();
                showSubmitSuccessAnimation('通緝審核完成','通緝申請已審核通過並正式進入通緝資料庫。');
            }catch(e){showToast('通緝審核失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}
        }

        function renderPoliceReview(){
            const host=document.getElementById('police-review-list'), count=document.getElementById('police-review-count'); if(!host)return;
            if(!canReviewPoliceReports()){host.innerHTML='';if(count)count.textContent='待審 0 件';return;}
            const pending=getUniquePendingRows((appData.policePendingReports||[]).filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='pending'),r=>r.caseId||r.docId).sort((a,b)=>(b.submittedAt||b.timestamp||0)-(a.submittedAt||a.timestamp||0));
            const rejected=getUniquePendingRows((appData.policePendingReports||[]).filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='rejected'),r=>r.caseId||r.docId).sort((a,b)=>(b.reviewedAt||b.updatedAt||0)-(a.reviewedAt||a.updatedAt||0));
            const wantedPending=getUniquePendingRows((appData.wantedPendingRecords||[]).filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='pending'),r=>r.id||r.docId).sort((a,b)=>(b.submittedAt||b.timestamp||0)-(a.submittedAt||a.timestamp||0));
            const wantedRejected=getUniquePendingRows((appData.wantedPendingRecords||[]).filter(r=>r.reviewStatus==='rejected'),r=>r.id||r.docId).sort((a,b)=>(b.reviewedAt||b.updatedAt||0)-(a.reviewedAt||a.updatedAt||0));
            if(count)count.textContent=`待審 ${pending.length+wantedPending.length} 件`;
            if(!pending.length&&!rejected.length&&!wantedPending.length&&!wantedRejected.length){host.innerHTML='<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 p-8 rounded-2xl text-center text-slate-500">目前沒有待審核或退件中的警用案件／通緝申請。</div>';return;}
            const wantedPendingHtml=wantedPending.map(r=>`<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-rose-500/25 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl space-y-4"><div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-800 pb-4"><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center"><span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/20">通緝申請</span><span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/20">待審核</span><h4 class="text-base font-bold text-white">${escapeHtml(r.name||'未命名')}</h4></div><span class="text-xs text-slate-400">${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(r.submittedAt||r.timestamp))}</span></div><div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs bg-slate-950 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800/60"><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">證件末五碼</span><span class="font-mono text-amber-300">${escapeHtml(r.id5||'--')}</span></div><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">發布帳號</span><span class="font-mono text-cyan-400">${escapeHtml(r.createdByAccount||'--')}</span></div><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">發布單位</span><span class="font-medium text-slate-200">${escapeHtml(r.publishUnit||r.department||'--')}</span></div><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">違反條例</span><span class="font-medium text-rose-300">${escapeHtml(r.law||'--')}</span></div></div><div class="text-sm text-slate-300"><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 block mb-1">通緝原因</span>${escapeHtml(r.reason||'--')}</div><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800"><button type="button" onclick="viewPendingWantedDetail('${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]">檢視完整資料</button><button type="button" onclick="approveWantedPending('${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-emerald-600/20 hover:bg-emerald-600/30 text-emerald-400 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]">審核通過</button><button type="button" onclick="openRejectWantedPending('${escapeHtml(r.docId||r.id)}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/20 hover:bg-red-600/30 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]">退件</button></div></div>`).join('');
            const pendingHtml=pending.map(r=>`<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-blue-500/20 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl space-y-4"><div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-800 pb-4"><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center"><span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/20 font-mono">${r.caseId||'--'}</span><span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/20">待審核</span><h4 class="text-base font-bold text-white">${r.title||'未命名案件'}</h4></div><span class="text-xs text-slate-400">${r.time||'--'}</span></div><div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 text-xs bg-slate-950 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800/60"><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">寄件人</span><span class="font-medium text-slate-200">${r.authorName||'--'} (${r.authorId||'--'})</span></div><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">寄件帳號</span><span class="font-mono text-cyan-400">${r.registeredByAccount||'--'}</span></div></div><p class="text-sm text-slate-300 line-clamp-4">${r.content||''}</p><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800"><button type="button" onclick="viewPendingPoliceReportDetail('${r.caseId}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-eye mr-1"></i>檢視完整案件</button><button type="button" onclick="approvePolicePendingReport('${r.caseId}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-emerald-600/20 hover:bg-emerald-600/30 text-emerald-400 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-check mr-1"></i>審核完畢並送出</button><button type="button" onclick="openRejectPolicePending('${r.caseId}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/20 hover:bg-red-600/30 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-rotate-left mr-1"></i>退件</button></div></div>`).join('');
            const rejectedHtml=rejected.map(r=>`<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-red-500/20 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-xl space-y-3"><div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2"><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center"><span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-mono bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400">${r.caseId||'--'}</span><span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-red-500/10 text-red-300">已退件</span><span class="text-sm font-bold text-white">${r.title||'未命名案件'}</span></div><span class="text-xs text-slate-500">${formatDateTime(r.reviewedAt||r.updatedAt)}</span></div><div class="text-sm text-red-200 whitespace-pre-wrap">退件原因：${r.rejectionReason||'未提供退件原因'}</div><button type="button" onclick="openEditReviewStatus('police','${r.caseId}','rejected')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-blue-600/20 text-blue-300 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square mr-1"></i>編輯審核狀況</button></div>`).join('');
            const wantedRejectedHtml=wantedRejected.map(r=>`<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-red-500/20 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-xl space-y-3"><div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2"><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center"><span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-mono bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300">通緝申請</span><span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-red-500/10 text-red-300">已退件</span><span class="text-sm font-bold text-white">${escapeHtml(r.name||'未命名')}</span></div><span class="text-xs text-slate-500">${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(r.reviewedAt||r.updatedAt))}</span></div><div class="text-sm text-red-200 whitespace-pre-wrap">退件原因：${escapeHtml(r.rejectionReason||'未提供退件原因')}</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-400">發布單位：${escapeHtml(r.publishUnit||r.department||'--')}｜發布帳號：${escapeHtml(r.createdByAccount||'--')}</div></div>`).join('');
            host.innerHTML=(wantedPendingHtml?`<div>${wantedPendingHtml}</div>`:'')+(pendingHtml?`<div class="${wantedPendingHtml?'pt-5':''}">${pendingHtml}</div>`:'')+(rejectedHtml?`<div class="pt-5"><div class="text-sm font-bold text-red-300 mb-3">已退件案件（可重新調整審核狀況）</div>${rejectedHtml}</div>`:'')+(wantedRejectedHtml?`<div class="pt-5"><div class="text-sm font-bold text-red-300 mb-3">已退件通緝申請</div>${wantedRejectedHtml}</div>`:'');
        }
        function viewPendingPoliceReportDetail(caseId){ const r=(appData.policePendingReports||[]).find(x=>x.caseId===caseId); if(!r)return; viewReportDetailFromObject('police',r,true); }
        function viewReportDetailFromObject(dept,r,isPending=false){
            document.getElementById('detail-case-badge').textContent=r.caseId||'--'; document.getElementById('detail-title').textContent=r.title||'未命名案件'; document.getElementById('detail-author').textContent=`${r.authorName||'--'} (${r.authorId||'--'})`; const reg=document.getElementById('detail-registered-account'); if(reg)reg.textContent=getRegisteredAccount(r); document.getElementById('detail-suspect-name').textContent=r.suspectName||'--'; document.getElementById('detail-suspect-id').textContent=r.suspectId||'--'; document.getElementById('detail-suspect-vehicle').textContent=r.suspectVehicle||'--'; document.getElementById('detail-suspect-name-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden',dept!=='police'); document.getElementById('detail-suspect-id-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden',dept!=='police'); document.getElementById('detail-suspect-vehicle-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden',dept!=='police'); document.getElementById('detail-police-extra-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden',dept!=='police'); document.getElementById('detail-postal-code').textContent=r.postalCode||'未填'; document.getElementById('detail-command').textContent=r.command||'未填'; document.getElementById('detail-support-units').textContent=(r.supportUnits||[]).join('、')||'無'; document.getElementById('detail-time').textContent=r.time||formatDateTime(r.timestamp); document.getElementById('detail-content').textContent=r.content||''; const rr=getReportRemarks(r),dh=document.getElementById('detail-remark'); dh.innerHTML=rr.length?rr.map(x=>`<div class="mb-3"><span class="text-amber-400 font-semibold">${x.authorName||'長官'}</span><span class="text-slate-500 ml-2">${x.time||formatDateTime(x.timestamp)}</span><div class="mt-1 text-slate-300 whitespace-pre-wrap">${x.text||''}</div></div>`).join(''):'尚未有長官批示。'; const links=getReportVideoLinks(r).map(safeHttpUrl).filter(Boolean),vw=document.getElementById('detail-video-wrapper'),vb=document.getElementById('detail-video-links'); if(links.length){vw.classList.remove('hidden');vb.innerHTML=links.map((url,i)=>`<a href="${escapeHtml(url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="text-xs text-blue-400 hover:underline">影片 ${i+1}</a>`).join('');}else{vw.classList.add('hidden');vb.innerHTML='';} const bar=document.getElementById('detail-action-bar'); if(bar){const reviewAllowed=dept==='fire'?canReviewFireReports():canReviewPoliceReports();bar.innerHTML=isPending&&reviewAllowed?`<button type="button" onclick="${dept==='fire'?`approveFirePendingReport('${r.caseId}')`:`approvePolicePendingReport('${r.caseId}')`};closeModal('modal-report-detail')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-emerald-600/20 text-emerald-400 rounded-lg text-xs">審核完畢並送出</button><button type="button" onclick="${dept==='fire'?`openRejectFirePending('${r.caseId}')`:`openRejectPolicePending('${r.caseId}')`};closeModal('modal-report-detail')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/20 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs">退件</button>`:'';} document.getElementById('modal-report-detail').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        async function approvePolicePendingReport(caseId){
            if(!canReviewPoliceReports()){showToast('只有警察高層、高階成員與管理中心可以審核案件。','warning','權限不足');return;} const r=(appData.policePendingReports||[]).find(x=>x.caseId===caseId); if(!r)return;
            try{ const shared={...r,reviewStatus:'approved',reviewedAt:Date.now(),reviewedBy:currentUser.account}; delete shared.rejectionReason; const finalDocId=await resolveReportDocId('policeReports',r,caseId); const pendingDocId=await resolveReportDocId('policePendingReports',r,caseId); await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'policeReports',finalDocId),shared); await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'policePendingReports',pendingDocId)); appData.policeReports.unshift({docId:finalDocId,...shared}); appData.policePendingReports=appData.policePendingReports.filter(x=>x.caseId!==caseId); await removePendingReportNotifications(caseId,r.registeredByAccount);const n={type:'report',caseId,recipientAccount:r.registeredByAccount,recipientAccountNormalized:String(r.registeredByAccount||'').trim().toLowerCase(),status:'approved',title:'案件已審核完畢',message:`案件 ${caseId} 已經高層審核完畢並正式送出。`,timestamp:Date.now(),read:false}; const nr=fm.doc(fm.collection(db,'reportNotifications')); await fm.setDoc(nr,n); appData.reportNotifications.unshift({docId:nr.id,...n}); await logAdminAction('審核案件完畢並送出',caseId); renderPoliceReview(); renderPoliceReports(); renderMyProfile(); showSubmitSuccessAnimation('審核完成','案件已審核完畢並同步至共享案件報告庫。'); }catch(e){showToast('審核送出失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}
        }
        function openRejectPolicePending(caseId){ if(!canReviewPoliceReports()){showToast('只有警察高層與管理中心可以退件。','warning','權限不足');return;} document.getElementById('review-reject-case-id').value=caseId; document.getElementById('review-reject-dept').value='police'; document.getElementById('review-reject-reason').value=''; document.getElementById('modal-review-reject').classList.remove('hidden'); }
        async function deleteLookupPendingRecord(type,id){
            if(type!=='wanted'||!currentUser)return;
            const r=(appData.wantedPendingRecords||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id));
            if(!r||r.createdByAccount!==currentUser.account&&!isTotalManagementUser()){showToast('只能移除自己發布的退件通緝申請。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            if(!(await showConfirm('確定要移除這筆退件通緝申請嗎？','移除退件申請','確定移除')))return;
            try{await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'wantedPendingRecords',r.docId||id));appData.wantedPendingRecords=appData.wantedPendingRecords.filter(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)!==String(id));renderPersonalNotifications();renderPoliceReview();showToast('退件通緝申請已移除。','success','移除完成');}catch(e){showToast('移除失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}
        }

        function openRejectWantedPending(id){
            if(!canReviewPoliceReports()){showToast('只有警察高層、高階成員與管理中心可以退件通緝申請。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            document.getElementById('review-reject-case-id').value=id; document.getElementById('review-reject-dept').value='wanted'; document.getElementById('review-reject-reason').value=''; document.getElementById('modal-review-reject').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        async function rejectPolicePendingReport(){const caseId=document.getElementById('review-reject-case-id').value;const dept=document.getElementById('review-reject-dept').value||'police';const reason=document.getElementById('review-reject-reason').value.trim();const canReview=dept==='fire'||dept==='ems'?canReviewFireReports():canReviewPoliceReports();if(!canReview){showToast('權限不足。','warning','權限不足');return;}if(!reason){showToast('請填寫退件原因。','warning','退件原因必填');return;}const source=dept==='fire'?appData.firePendingReports:dept==='ems'?appData.emsPendingReports:dept==='wanted'?appData.wantedPendingRecords:appData.policePendingReports;const r=(source||[]).find(x=>String(x.caseId||x.id||x.docId)===String(caseId));if(!r)return;try{const col=dept==='fire'?'firePendingReports':dept==='ems'?'emsPendingReports':dept==='wanted'?'wantedPendingRecords':'policePendingReports';const patch={reviewStatus:'rejected',rejectionReason:reason,reviewedAt:Date.now(),reviewedBy:currentUser.account,updatedAt:Date.now()};const rejectDocId=await resolveReportDocId(col,r,caseId);if(!rejectDocId)throw new Error('找不到案件的 Firestore 文件 ID');await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,col,rejectDocId),patch,{merge:true});r.docId=rejectDocId;Object.assign(r,patch);await removePendingReportNotifications(caseId,r.registeredByAccount||r.authorId);const title=dept==='wanted'?'通緝申請遭退件':dept==='ems'?'EMS報告遭退件':dept==='fire'?'消防案件遭退件':'案件遭退件';const recipient=r.registeredByAccount||r.authorId||r.createdByAccount;const n={type:dept==='wanted'?'wanted':'report',department:'police',caseId,recipientAccount:recipient,recipientAccountNormalized:String(recipient||'').trim().toLowerCase(),status:'rejected',title,message:`${dept==='wanted'?'通緝申請':dept==='ems'?'EMS報告':`案件 ${caseId}`} 已退件：${reason}`,timestamp:Date.now(),read:false};const nr=fm.doc(fm.collection(db,'reportNotifications'));await fm.setDoc(nr,n);appData.reportNotifications.unshift({docId:nr.id,...n});await logAdminAction((dept==='ems'?'EMS':dept==='fire'?'消防':'警用')+'退件案件報告',caseId,reason);closeModal('modal-review-reject');if(dept==='ems'||dept==='fire')renderFireReview();else renderPoliceReview();if(dept==='wanted'){renderLookupSystems();}renderPersonalNotifications();showToast('案件已退件，寄件人可重新編輯送件。','success','退件完成');}catch(e){showToast('退件失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}}
        
        function renderPoliceReports() {
            const container = document.getElementById('police-reports-list');
            const input = document.getElementById('search-police-report');
            const queryVal = input ? input.value.trim().toLowerCase() : '';
            if (!container) return;
            container.innerHTML='';

            const filtered=(appData.policeReports||[]).filter(r=>{
                if(!queryVal) return true;
                const values=[r.suspectId,r.authorId,r.registeredByAccount,r.caseId,r.title,r.authorName].map(v=>String(v||'').toLowerCase());
                return values.some(v=>v.includes(queryVal));
            });
            if(!filtered.length){
                container.innerHTML='<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 p-8 rounded-2xl text-center text-slate-500 text-sm">沒有找到符合條件的警務案件紀錄。</div>';
                return;
            }

            filtered.forEach(r=>{
                const support=(r.supportUnits||[]).join('、')||'無';
                const card=document.createElement('div');
                card.className='bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl space-y-4 hover:border-slate-700 transition';
                card.innerHTML=`
                    <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-800 pb-4">
                        <div class="flex items-center space-x-3 min-w-0"><span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/20 font-mono shrink-0">${r.caseId||'--'}</span><h4 class="text-base font-bold text-white truncate">${r.title||'未命名案件'}</h4></div>
                        <span class="text-xs text-slate-400 shrink-0">${r.time||'--'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4 text-xs bg-slate-950 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800/60">
                        <div><span class="text-slate-500 block">填寫人</span><span class="font-medium text-slate-200">${r.authorName||'--'} (${r.authorId||'--'})</span></div>
                        <div><span class="text-slate-500 block">發布登記帳號</span><span class="font-mono text-cyan-400">${getRegisteredAccount(r)}</span></div>
                        <div><span class="text-slate-500 block">嫌疑人／證件 ID</span><span class="font-medium text-slate-200">${r.suspectName||'無'} / <span class="font-mono text-amber-400">${r.suspectId||'無'}</span></span></div>
                        <div><span class="text-slate-500 block">嫌疑人車輛</span><span class="font-medium text-slate-200">${r.suspectVehicle||'無'}</span></div>
                        <div><span class="text-slate-500 block">案件郵遞區號</span><span class="font-mono text-blue-400">${r.postalCode||'未填'}</span></div>
                        <div><span class="text-slate-500 block">案件指揮</span><span class="font-medium text-slate-200">${r.command||'未填'}</span></div>
                        <div class="sm:col-span-2"><span class="text-slate-500 block">支援單位</span><span class="font-medium text-slate-200">${support}</span></div>
