                        <div><span class="text-slate-500 block">所犯罪名／違反條例</span><span class="font-semibold text-rose-300">${(r.violations||[]).join('、')||'未選擇'}</span></div>
                        <div><span class="text-slate-500 block">總監獄時間</span><span class="font-semibold text-amber-300">${formatJailMinutes(r.jailMinutes)}</span>${r.organizedCrime?'<span class="text-[11px] text-amber-400 ml-2">組織犯罪 +⅓</span>':''}</div>
                        
                        <div class="lg:col-span-4"><span class="text-slate-500 block">長官批示</span><span class="font-medium text-slate-300">${getLatestRemarkText(r)}</span></div>
                    </div>
                    <p class="text-sm text-slate-300 line-clamp-2">${r.content||''}</p>
                    <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/80">
                        <button type="button" onclick="viewReportDetail('police','${r.caseId}')" class="text-xs text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center space-x-1 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-500/10 transition min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-eye mr-1"></i><span>檢視詳細內容與影片</span></button>
                        <div class="mobile-report-actions flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                            ${canModifyReport(r,'police')?`<button type="button" onclick="openEditReportModal('police','${r.caseId}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen mr-1"></i>編輯</button>`:''}
                            ${canModifyReport(r,'police')?`<button type="button" onclick="deleteReport('police','${r.caseId}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/20 hover:bg-red-600/30 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash mr-1"></i>刪除</button>`:''}
                            ${canRemarkReport('police')?`<button type="button" onclick="openRemarkModal('policeReports','${r.caseId}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-amber-600/20 hover:bg-amber-600/30 text-amber-400 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen-nib mr-1"></i>批示備註</button>`:''}${canEditReviewStatus('police')?`<button type="button" onclick="openEditReviewStatus('police','${r.caseId}','approved')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-blue-600/20 hover:bg-blue-600/30 text-blue-300 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-stamp mr-1"></i>編輯審核狀況</button>`:''}
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
                container.appendChild(card);
            });
        }

        function renderPolicePunishments() {
            const container=document.getElementById('police-punishments-list'),summary=document.getElementById('police-punishment-summary-content'),q=(document.getElementById('search-police-punishment')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();if(!container)return;
            const filtered=(appData.policePunishments||[]).filter(p=>{
                if(!q) return true;
                const memberId=String(p.memberId||'').toLowerCase();
                const memberAccount=String(p.memberAccount||'').toLowerCase();
                const member=String(p.memberName||'').toLowerCase();
                return memberId.includes(q)||memberAccount.includes(q)||member.includes(q);
            });container.innerHTML='';
            const totals={嘉獎:0,小功:0,大功:0,警告:0,小過:0,大過:0,停職調查:0};filtered.forEach(p=>getPunishmentItems(p).forEach(item=>{const m=String(item).match(/^(嘉獎|小功|大功|警告|小過|大過)x(\d+)$/);if(m)totals[m[1]]+=Number(m[2]);else if(item==='停職調查')totals.停職調查++;}));
            if(summary)summary.innerHTML=Object.entries(totals).map(([k,v])=>`<div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3"><span class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 block">${k}</span><span class="text-xl font-bold ${['警告','小過','大過','停職調查'].includes(k)?'text-rose-400':'text-emerald-400'}">${v}</span><span class="text-[10px] text-slate-500">次</span></div>`).join('');
            if(!filtered.length){container.innerHTML='<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 p-8 rounded-2xl text-center text-slate-500">沒有找到符合管理編號的獎懲紀錄。</div>';return;}
            filtered.forEach(p=>{const key=p.docId||p.id,items=getPunishmentItems(p),reward=String(p.type||'punishment')==='reward',edit=canModifyPunishment(p),card=document.createElement('div');card.className='bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl space-y-4';card.innerHTML=`<div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-800 pb-4"><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2"><span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold ${reward?'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20':'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/20'} font-mono">${p.id||key}</span><span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold ${reward?'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400':'bg-red-500/20 text-red-400'}">${reward?'獎勵':'懲處'}｜${items.join('、')||'未填寫'}</span></div><span class="text-xs text-slate-400">${p.time||'--'}</span></div><div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 text-xs bg-slate-950 p-3 rounded-xl"><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">被獎懲員警</span><b>${p.memberName||'未知'}（管理編號：${p.memberId||'--'}）</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">發布部門</span>${p.department||'--'}</div><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">登記帳號</span><span class="font-mono text-cyan-400">${p.authorAccount||'舊資料'}</span></div></div><div class="text-sm text-slate-300 line-clamp-3">${p.reason||'未提供事由。'}</div><div class="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-2 border-t border-slate-800 pt-2"><button onclick="viewPunishmentDetail('${key}')" class="px-3 py-2 text-xs text-amber-400 rounded-lg hover:bg-amber-500/10"><i class="fa-solid fa-expand mr-1"></i>點擊放大檢視</button><div class="flex gap-2">${edit?`<button onclick="openEditPunishmentModal('${key}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs">修改</button>`:''}${canDeletePunishment()?`<button onclick="deletePunishment('${key}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/20 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs">移除</button>`:''}</div></div>`;container.appendChild(card);});
        }

        function canUsePolicePunishment() {
            if (!currentUser) return false;
            if (currentUser.role === 'admin') return true;
            const units = currentUser.units || [currentUser.unit];
            return currentUser.role === 'high' && units.includes('police');
        }

        function punishmentHasPoliceAuthority() {
            if (!currentUser) return false;
            if (currentUser.role === 'admin') return true;
            const units = Array.isArray(currentUser.units) ? currentUser.units : [currentUser.unit];
            const normalizedUnits = units.filter(Boolean).map(x => String(x).trim().toLowerCase());
            return currentUser.role === 'high' && (normalizedUnits.includes('police') || !!currentUser.policeDivision || normalizedUnits.some(x => x.includes('警')));
        }

        function canModifyPunishment(p) {
            if (!currentUser || !p) return false;
            if (currentUser.role === 'admin') return true;
            const account = String(currentUser.account || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            const publisher = String(p.authorAccount || p.createdBy || p.authorId || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            return punishmentHasPoliceAuthority() || (!!account && !!publisher && account === publisher);
        }

        function canDeletePunishment(p) {
            return punishmentHasPoliceAuthority();
        }

        function viewPunishmentDetail(punishId) {
            const p=(appData.policePunishments||[]).find(x=>String(x.id||'')===String(punishId)||String(x.docId||'')===String(punishId));if(!p){showToast('找不到這筆警用獎懲資料。','error','資料不存在');return;}const reward=String(p.type||'punishment')==='reward';
            document.getElementById('punish-detail-badge').textContent=p.id||punishId;document.getElementById('punish-detail-title').textContent=`${p.memberName||'未知員警'} 的警用${reward?'獎勵':'懲處'}公告`;document.getElementById('punish-detail-member').textContent=`${p.memberName||'未知員警'}（管理編號：${p.memberId||'--'}）`;document.getElementById('punish-detail-item').textContent=`${reward?'獎勵':'懲處'}｜${getPunishmentItems(p).join('、')||'--'}`;document.getElementById('punish-detail-time').textContent=p.time||'--';document.getElementById('punish-detail-department').textContent=p.department||'--';document.getElementById('punish-detail-author').textContent=`${p.authorName||'--'} (${p.authorPos||'--'})／帳號：${p.authorAccount||'舊資料'}`;document.getElementById('punish-detail-reason').textContent=p.reason||'--';document.getElementById('modal-punishment-detail').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function openEditPunishmentModal(punishId) {
            const p=(appData.policePunishments||[]).find(x=>String(x.id||'')===String(punishId)||String(x.docId||'')===String(punishId));if(!p)return;if(!canModifyPunishment(p)){showToast('權限不足：只有警察高層、管理處或該筆獎懲發布者可以修改。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            document.getElementById('edit-type').value='punishment';document.getElementById('edit-id-key').value=p.docId||p.id;document.getElementById('edit-modal-title').textContent=`修改警用獎懲 (${p.id||p.docId})`;
            const users=appData.users.filter(u=>(u.units||[u.unit]).includes('police'));
            const legacyMember=users.find(u=>String(u.staffNumber||'')===String(p.memberId||''));
            const selectedMember=users.find(u=>String(u.account||'')===String(p.memberAccount||'')) || legacyMember || users.find(u=>String(u.account||'')===String(p.memberId||''));
            const opts=users.map(u=>`<option value="${u.account}" ${selectedMember&&u.account===selectedMember.account?'selected':''}>${u.name} (${u.staffNumber||u.account})</option>`).join(''),type=String(p.type||'punishment')==='reward'?'reward':'punishment';
            document.getElementById('edit-fields-container').innerHTML=`<div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4"><div><label class="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-2">被獎懲員警</label><select id="edit-punish-member" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm text-white"><option value="">-- 請選擇 --</option>${opts}</select></div><div><label class="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-2">被獎懲員警管理編號</label><input id="edit-punish-member-id" value="${(selectedMember?.staffNumber||p.memberId||'')}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white font-mono"></div></div><div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4"><div><label class="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-2">獎懲類型</label><select id="edit-punish-type" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm text-white"><option value="reward" ${type==='reward'?'selected':''}>獎勵</option><option value="punishment" ${type==='punishment'?'selected':''}>懲處</option></select></div><div><label class="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-2">獎懲項目（可複選）</label><div id="edit-punish-item" class="w-full min-h-[48px] bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3"></div></div></div><div><label class="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-2">發布部門</label><input id="edit-punish-department" value="${p.department||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white"></div><div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4"><input id="edit-punish-author-name" value="${p.authorName||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white"><input id="edit-punish-author-pos" value="${p.authorPos||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white"></div><div><label class="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-2">獎懲事由說明</label><textarea id="edit-punish-reason" rows="5" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 text-sm text-white">${p.reason||''}</textarea></div>`;
            populatePunishmentItemOptions('edit-punish-item',type,getPunishmentItems(p));document.getElementById('edit-punish-type').addEventListener('change',e=>populatePunishmentItemOptions('edit-punish-item',e.target.value,[]));document.getElementById('edit-punish-member').addEventListener('change',e=>{
                const selected=e.target.value;
                const user=appData.users.find(u=>u.account===selected);
                document.getElementById('edit-punish-member-id').value=user?.staffNumber||'';
            });document.getElementById('modal-edit').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        async function deletePunishment(punishId) {
            const p = appData.policePunishments.find(x => String(x.id || '') === String(punishId) || String(x.docId || '') === String(punishId));
            if (!p) return;
            if (!canDeletePunishment(p)) {
                showToast('權限不足：只有警察高層與管理處可以移除警用獎懲。', 'warning', '權限不足');
                return;
            }
            if (!(await showConfirm('確定要刪除這筆警用獎懲公告嗎？', '刪除警用獎懲', '確定刪除'))) return;

            try {
                await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db, 'policePunishments', p.docId || p.id));
                const deletedKey = String(p.docId || p.id || punishId);
                appData.policePunishments = appData.policePunishments.filter(x => String(x.docId || x.id || '') !== deletedKey);
                await logAdminAction('移除警用獎懲', p.id || punishId);
                renderPolicePunishments();
                updateAccessLocks();
                showToast('警用獎懲公告已成功移除。', 'success', '操作成功');
            } catch (e) {
                showToast('刪除失敗：' + e.message, 'error', '操作失敗');
            }
        }


        function generateNextEMSId(d=new Date()){const y=d.getFullYear(),m=String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),day=String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0'),p=`EMS-${y}${m}${day}`;let n=0;(appData.emsReports||[]).forEach(r=>{const mt=String(r.id||r.docId||'').match(new RegExp(`^${p}(\\d+)$`));if(mt)n=Math.max(n,Number(mt[1])||0)});return p+String(n+1).padStart(3,'0');}
        function canModifyEMS(r){return !!currentUser&&!!r&&(currentUser.role==='admin'||(currentUser.role==='high'&&userHasUnit('fire'))||String(r.authorId||r.registeredByAccount||'').toLowerCase()===String(currentUser.account||'').toLowerCase())}
        function getEMSRecordForEdit(editId){
            const key=String(editId||'');
            return (appData.emsReports||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===key) || (appData.emsPendingReports||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===key) || null;
        }
        function openEMSReportModal(editId=''){
            const academyMode=!!academyExamSession&&academyExamSession.type==='fire';
            if(!academyMode&&(!currentUser||!userHasUnit('fire'))){showToast('只有消防身分可以使用EMS報告。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            if(currentUser&&isSuspendedUser(currentUser.account)){showToast('你目前於停職調查階段，無法使用EMS報告功能。','warning','停職調查限制');return;}
            const r=academyMode?null:getEMSRecordForEdit(editId);
            if(r&&String(r.reviewStatus||'')==='pending'){showToast('案件目前正在等待消防高層審核，暫時無法修改。','warning','等待審核');return;}
            if(r&&!canModifyEMS(r)){showToast('你無法修改這筆EMS報告。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            document.getElementById('ems-edit-id').value=r?(r.docId||r.id):'';
            document.getElementById('ems-report-id').value=r?(r.id||r.docId):generateNextEMSId();
            for(const [id,key] of Object.entries({'ems-chief-complaint':'chiefComplaint','ems-dispatch-category':'dispatchCategory','ems-patient':'patient','ems-unit-vehicle':'unitVehicle','ems-gcs-e':'gcsE','ems-treatment':'treatment','ems-dispatch-time':'dispatchTime','ems-arrival-time':'arrivalTime','ems-leave-time':'leaveTime','ems-hospital-time':'hospitalTime','ems-return-time':'returnTime'})){document.getElementById(id).value=r?.[key]||'';}
            document.getElementById('ems-author').value=academyMode?'專生考生':`${r?.authorName||currentUser.name} (${r?.authorId||currentUser.account})`;
            document.getElementById('ems-modal-title').innerHTML=`<i class="fa-solid fa-truck-medical text-rose-400 mr-2"></i>${r?'修改EMS救護出勤報告':'EMS救護出勤報告'}`;
            document.getElementById('submit-ems-report-btn').textContent=r?'重新送審':'提交EMS救護出勤報告';
            document.getElementById('modal-ems-report').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        async function submitEMSReport(){
            const academyMode=!!academyExamSession&&academyExamSession.type==='fire';
            if(!academyMode&&(!currentUser||!userHasUnit('fire'))){showToast('只有消防身分可以提交EMS報告。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            if(currentUser&&isSuspendedUser(currentUser.account)){showToast('你目前於停職調查階段，無法使用EMS報告功能。','warning','停職調查限制');return;}
            const editId=document.getElementById('ems-edit-id').value;
            const old=academyMode?null:getEMSRecordForEdit(editId);
            if(old&&String(old.reviewStatus||'')==='pending'){showToast('案件目前正在等待消防高層審核，暫時無法修改。','warning','等待審核');return;}
            if(old&&!canModifyEMS(old)){showToast('你無法修改這筆EMS報告。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            const f={chiefComplaint:document.getElementById('ems-chief-complaint').value.trim(),dispatchCategory:document.getElementById('ems-dispatch-category').value.trim(),patient:document.getElementById('ems-patient').value.trim(),unitVehicle:document.getElementById('ems-unit-vehicle').value.trim(),gcsE:document.getElementById('ems-gcs-e').value.trim(),treatment:document.getElementById('ems-treatment').value.trim(),dispatchTime:document.getElementById('ems-dispatch-time').value.trim(),arrivalTime:document.getElementById('ems-arrival-time').value.trim(),leaveTime:document.getElementById('ems-leave-time').value.trim(),hospitalTime:document.getElementById('ems-hospital-time').value.trim(),returnTime:document.getElementById('ems-return-time').value.trim()};
            if(Object.values(f).some(v=>!v)){showToast('請完整填寫EMS救護出勤報告。','error','資料不完整');return;}
            const id=old?(old.id||old.docId):generateNextEMSId(),now=Date.now();
            const obj={...(old||{}),id,...f,authorId:academyMode?'警專專生':(old?.authorId||currentUser.account),authorName:academyMode?'專生考生':(old?.authorName||currentUser.name),registeredByAccount:academyMode?'':(old?.registeredByAccount||currentUser.account),registeredByName:academyMode?'專生考生':(old?.registeredByName||currentUser.name),timestamp:old?.timestamp||now,time:old?.time||formatDateTime(now),updatedAt:now};
            if(academyMode){academyExamCache.emsReports.unshift(obj);saveAcademyCache();closeModal('modal-ems-report');renderAcademyCache();showSubmitSuccessAnimation('考核 EMS 送出成功',`${id} 已保留於本次瀏覽器快取。`);return;}
            try{
                const pendingObj={...obj,reviewStatus:'pending',rejectionReason:'',reviewedAt:0,reviewedBy:'',submittedAt:now,updatedAt:now};
                await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'emsPendingReports',id),pendingObj);
                if(old&&appData.emsReports.some(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(editId))) await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'emsReports',old.docId||old.id));
                appData.emsPendingReports=appData.emsPendingReports.filter(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)!==String(id));
                appData.emsPendingReports.unshift({docId:id,...pendingObj});
                if(old) appData.emsReports=appData.emsReports.filter(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)!==String(editId));
                await removePendingReportNotifications(id,pendingObj.registeredByAccount);
                const n={type:'report',department:'fire',caseId:id,recipientAccount:pendingObj.registeredByAccount,recipientAccountNormalized:String(pendingObj.registeredByAccount||'').trim().toLowerCase(),status:'pending',title:old?'EMS報告已重新送件':'EMS報告已送出，等待消防高層審核',message:old?`EMS報告 ${id} 已重新送入消防高層審核。`:`EMS報告 ${id} 已送入消防高層審核系統。`,timestamp:now,read:false};
                const nr=fm.doc(fm.collection(db,'reportNotifications')); await fm.setDoc(nr,n); appData.reportNotifications.unshift({docId:nr.id,...n});
                // EMS 與消防共用消防／EMS Discord Webhook。
                sendDiscordCaseNotification('ems', pendingObj);
                await logAdminAction(old?'EMS報告退件後重新送件':'提交EMS報告等待消防高層審核',id);
                closeModal('modal-ems-report');renderEMSReports();renderFireReview();renderPersonalNotifications();renderMyProfile();showSubmitSuccessAnimation(old?'EMS重新送審成功':'EMS報告送審成功',old?`${id} 已重新送入消防高層審核。`:`${id} 已送入消防高層審核系統。`);
            }catch(e){showToast('EMS報告送審失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}
        }
        function renderEMSRemarkHistory(r){const el=document.getElementById('ems-remark-history');if(!el)return;const rr=getReportRemarks(r);el.innerHTML=rr.length?rr.map(x=>`<div class="rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/70 p-3"><div class="flex justify-between"><span class="text-xs text-amber-400">${x.authorName||'高層'}</span><span class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">${x.time||formatDateTime(x.timestamp)}</span></div><div class="text-xs text-slate-300 mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap">${x.text||''}</div></div>`).join(''):'<div class="text-xs text-slate-500">目前尚無高層批示。</div>';}
        function viewEMSReportDetail(id){const r=(appData.emsReports||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id));if(!r)return;document.getElementById('ems-detail-id').textContent=r.id||r.docId;document.getElementById('ems-detail-title').textContent=`${r.patient||'傷患'} 的EMS救護出勤報告`;const fields=[['主訴',r.chiefComplaint],['派遣類別',r.dispatchCategory],['傷患RP名稱 / ID',r.patient],['出勤單位 / 車輛',r.unitVehicle],['GCS評分 E',r.gcsE],['派遣時間',r.dispatchTime],['抵達時間',r.arrivalTime],['離開時間',r.leaveTime],['到院時間',r.hospitalTime],['返隊時間',r.returnTime],['填寫人',`${r.authorName||'--'} (${r.authorId||'--'})`],['建立時間',r.time||formatDateTime(r.timestamp)]];document.getElementById('ems-detail-fields').innerHTML=fields.map(([k,v])=>`<div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3"><span class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 block">${escapeHtml(k)}</span><span class="text-sm text-slate-200 break-words">${escapeHtml(v||'--')}</span></div>`).join('');document.getElementById('ems-detail-treatment').textContent=r.treatment||'--';renderEMSRemarkHistory(r);const actionHost=document.getElementById('ems-detail-action-bar');if(actionHost){const a=[];if(canModifyEMS(r))a.push(`<button type="button" onclick="closeModal('modal-ems-detail');openEMSReportModal('${id}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen mr-1"></i>編輯</button>`);if(canModifyEMS(r))a.push(`<button type="button" onclick="deleteEMSReport('${id}'); closeModal('modal-ems-detail')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/20 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash mr-1"></i>刪除</button>`);if(currentUser&&(currentUser.role==='admin'||(currentUser.role==='high'&&userHasUnit('fire'))) )a.push(`<button type="button" onclick="closeModal('modal-ems-detail');openRemarkModal('emsReports','${id}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-amber-600/20 text-amber-400 rounded-lg text-xs"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen-nib mr-1"></i>高層批示</button>`);actionHost.innerHTML=a.join('');}document.getElementById('modal-ems-detail').classList.remove('hidden');}
        async function deleteEMSReport(id){const r=(appData.emsReports||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id));if(!r||!canModifyEMS(r)){showToast('你無權限刪除這筆EMS報告。','warning','權限不足');return;}if(!(await showConfirm('確定要刪除這筆 EMS 救護出勤報告嗎？','刪除 EMS 報告','確定刪除')))return;try{await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'emsReports',r.docId||r.id));appData.emsReports=appData.emsReports.filter(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)!==String(id));await logAdminAction('刪除EMS救護報告',r.id||id);renderEMSReports();renderMyProfile();showToast('EMS救護出勤報告已刪除。','success','刪除成功');}catch(e){showToast('刪除失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}}
        function renderEMSReports(){const c=document.getElementById('ems-reports-list');if(!c)return;const q=(document.getElementById('search-ems-report')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase(),arr=(appData.emsReports||[]).filter(r=>!q||[r.id,r.patient,r.authorId,r.authorName,r.dispatchCategory].some(v=>String(v||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)));c.innerHTML=arr.length?'':'<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 p-8 rounded-2xl text-center text-slate-500">尚無EMS報告。</div>';arr.forEach(r=>{const key=r.docId||r.id,card=document.createElement('div');card.className='bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl space-y-4';card.innerHTML=`<div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><span class="text-xs text-rose-400 font-mono">${r.id||key}</span><span class="text-xs text-slate-500">${r.time||'--'}</span></div><div class="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-xs"><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">主訴</span>${r.chiefComplaint||'--'}</div><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">傷患</span>${r.patient||'--'}</div><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">出勤單位／車輛</span>${r.unitVehicle||'--'}</div><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">填寫人</span>${r.authorName||'--'} (${r.authorId||'--'})</div></div><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2"><button onclick="viewEMSReportDetail('${key}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300 rounded-lg text-xs">檢視</button>${canModifyEMS(r)?`<button onclick="openEMSReportModal('${key}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs">編輯</button><button onclick="deleteEMSReport('${key}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/20 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs">刪除</button>`:''}${currentUser&&(currentUser.role==='admin'||(currentUser.role==='high'&&userHasUnit('fire')))?`<button onclick="openRemarkModal('emsReports','${key}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-amber-600/20 text-amber-400 rounded-lg text-xs">高層批示</button>`:''}${canEditReviewStatus('ems')?`<button onclick="openEditReviewStatus('ems','${key}','approved')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-blue-600/20 text-blue-300 rounded-lg text-xs"><i class="fa-solid fa-stamp mr-1"></i>編輯審核狀況</button>`:''}</div>`;c.appendChild(card);});}

        function renderFireReports() {
            const container=document.getElementById('fire-reports-list');
            const query=(document.getElementById('search-fire-report')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
            if(!container)return;
            container.innerHTML='';
            const filtered=(appData.fireReports||[]).filter(r=>!query||[r.caseId,r.title,r.authorId,r.authorName,r.registeredByAccount].some(v=>String(v||'').toLowerCase().includes(query)));
            if(!filtered.length){container.innerHTML='<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 p-8 rounded-2xl text-center text-slate-500 text-sm">沒有找到符合條件的消防案件紀錄。</div>';return;}
            filtered.forEach(r=>{
                const card=document.createElement('div');
                card.className='bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl space-y-4 hover:border-slate-700 transition';
                card.innerHTML=`<div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-800 pb-4"><div class="flex items-center space-x-3 min-w-0"><span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-red-500/10 text-red-400 border border-red-500/20 font-mono shrink-0">${r.caseId||'--'}</span><h4 class="text-base font-bold text-white truncate">${r.title||'未命名案件'}</h4></div><span class="text-xs text-slate-400 shrink-0">${r.time||'--'}</span></div><div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs bg-slate-950 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800/60"><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">填寫隊員</span><span class="font-medium text-slate-200">${r.authorName||'--'} (${r.authorId||'--'})</span></div><div><span class="text-slate-500 block">發布登記帳號</span><span class="font-mono text-cyan-400">${getRegisteredAccount(r)}</span></div><div class="sm:col-span-2"><span class="text-slate-500 block">長官批示</span><span class="font-medium text-slate-300">${getLatestRemarkText(r)}</span></div></div><p class="text-sm text-slate-300 line-clamp-2">${r.content||''}</p><div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/80"><button type="button" onclick="viewReportDetail('fire','${r.caseId}')" class="text-xs text-red-400 hover:text-red-300 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center px-3 py-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-red-500/10 transition min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-eye mr-1"></i><span>檢視詳細內容與影片</span></button><div class="mobile-report-actions flex flex-wrap gap-2">${canModifyReport(r,'fire')?`<button type="button" onclick="openEditReportModal('fire','${r.caseId}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]">編輯</button>`:''}${canModifyReport(r,'fire')?`<button type="button" onclick="deleteReport('fire','${r.caseId}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/20 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]">刪除</button>`:''}${canRemarkReport('fire')?`<button type="button" onclick="openRemarkModal('fireReports','${r.caseId}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-amber-600/20 text-amber-400 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]">批示備註</button>`:''}${canEditReviewStatus('fire')?`<button type="button" onclick="openEditReviewStatus('fire','${r.caseId}','approved')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-blue-600/20 text-blue-300 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-stamp mr-1"></i>編輯審核狀況</button>`:''}</div></div>`;
                container.appendChild(card);
            });
        }

        function userHasUnit(unit) { if(!currentUser) return false; if(currentUser.role==='admin') return true; return (currentUser.units||[currentUser.unit]).includes(unit); }
        function canModifyReport(r, dept) { if(!currentUser||!r)return false; if(isSuperAdminAccount(currentUser.account)||currentUser.role==='admin')return true; if(currentUser.role==='high' && dept==='police' && userHasUnit('police'))return true; return String(r.registeredByAccount||r.authorId||'').toLowerCase()===String(currentUser.account||'').toLowerCase(); }
        function canChangeReportCaseId(dept) { return !!currentUser && (currentUser.role==='admin' || (dept==='police' && currentUser.role==='high' && userHasUnit('police'))); }
        function getRegisteredAccount(r) { return r?.registeredByAccount || r?.authorAccount || r?.authorId || '--'; }
        function getReportVideoLinks(r) { return Array.isArray(r?.videoLinks) ? r.videoLinks.filter(Boolean) : (r?.videoLink ? [r.videoLink] : []); }
        function canRemarkReport(dept) { if(!currentUser)return false; if(currentUser.role==='admin')return true; return userHasUnit(dept)&&(currentUser.role==='high'||currentUser.role==='senior'); }
        function canManageClockRecords(){ return !!currentUser&&currentUser.role==='admin'; }

        function viewReportDetail(dept, caseId) {
            const list=dept==='police'?appData.policeReports:appData.fireReports;
            const r=list.find(x=>x.caseId===caseId);
            if(!r){showToast('找不到案件資料。','error','資料不存在');return;}
            document.getElementById('detail-case-badge').textContent=r.caseId||caseId;
            document.getElementById('detail-title').textContent=r.title||'未命名案件';
            document.getElementById('detail-author').textContent=`${r.authorName||'--'} (${r.authorId||'--'})`;
            const reg=document.getElementById('detail-registered-account'); if(reg)reg.textContent=getRegisteredAccount(r);
            document.getElementById('detail-suspect-name').textContent=r.suspectName||'--';
            document.getElementById('detail-suspect-id').textContent=r.suspectId||'--';
            document.getElementById('detail-suspect-vehicle').textContent=r.suspectVehicle||'--';
            document.getElementById('detail-suspect-name-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden',dept!=='police');
            document.getElementById('detail-suspect-id-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden',dept!=='police');
            document.getElementById('detail-suspect-vehicle-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden',dept!=='police');
            const extra=document.getElementById('detail-police-extra-wrap');
            if(extra){extra.classList.toggle('hidden',dept!=='police');}
            if(dept==='police'){
                document.getElementById('detail-postal-code').textContent=r.postalCode||'未填';
                document.getElementById('detail-command').textContent=r.command||'未填';
                document.getElementById('detail-support-units').textContent=(r.supportUnits||[]).join('、')||'無';
            }
            const jailSummary=document.getElementById('detail-jail-summary');
            if(jailSummary){
                const hasJail=dept==='police';
                jailSummary.classList.toggle('hidden',!hasJail);
                jailSummary.innerHTML=hasJail?`<div class="text-xs text-slate-400">所犯罪名／違反條例</div><div class="text-sm text-rose-200 mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap">${(r.violations||[]).join('、')||'未選擇'}</div><div class="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-300 mt-2">總監獄時間：${formatJailMinutes(r.jailMinutes)}${r.organizedCrime?'（組織犯罪加重⅓）':''}</div>`:'';
            }
            document.getElementById('detail-time').textContent=r.time||formatDateTime(r.timestamp);
            document.getElementById('detail-content').textContent=r.content||'';
            const remarks=getReportRemarks(r),detailRemarkEl=document.getElementById('detail-remark');
            detailRemarkEl.innerHTML=remarks.length?remarks.map(x=>`<div class="mb-3 last:mb-0"><div><span class="text-amber-400 font-semibold">${x.authorName||'長官'}</span><span class="text-slate-500 ml-2">${x.time||formatDateTime(x.timestamp)}</span></div><div class="mt-1 text-slate-300 whitespace-pre-wrap">${x.text||''}</div></div>`).join(''):'尚未有長官批示。';

            const links=getReportVideoLinks(r),vidWrap=document.getElementById('detail-video-wrapper'),vidBox=document.getElementById('detail-video-links');
            const safeLinks=links.map(safeHttpUrl).filter(Boolean); if(safeLinks.length){vidWrap.classList.remove('hidden');vidBox.innerHTML=safeLinks.map((url,i)=>`<a href="${escapeHtml(url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 hover:underline"><i class="fa-solid fa-video"></i><span>開啟影片 ${i+1}</span></a>`).join('');}
            else{vidWrap.classList.add('hidden');vidBox.innerHTML='';}

            const actionBar=document.getElementById('detail-action-bar');
            if(actionBar){const actions=[];if(canModifyReport(r,dept))actions.push(`<button type="button" onclick="openEditReportModal('${dept}','${r.caseId}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen mr-1"></i>編輯案件</button>`);if(canModifyReport(r,dept))actions.push(`<button type="button" onclick="deleteReport('${dept}','${r.caseId}');closeModal('modal-report-detail')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/20 hover:bg-red-600/30 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash mr-1"></i>刪除案件</button>`);if(canRemarkReport(dept))actions.push(`<button type="button" onclick="openRemarkModal('${dept==='police'?'policeReports':'fireReports'}','${r.caseId}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-amber-600/20 hover:bg-amber-600/30 text-amber-400 rounded-lg text-xs min-h-[44px]"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen-nib mr-1"></i>新增長官批示</button>`);actionBar.innerHTML=actions.join('');}
            document.getElementById('modal-report-detail').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        async function deleteReport(dept, caseId) {
            if (!currentUser) {
                showToast('請先登入系統。', 'warning', '需要登入');
                return;
            }
            const list = dept === 'police' ? appData.policeReports : appData.fireReports;
            const r = list.find(x => x.caseId === caseId);
            if (!r) return;

            const allowed = canModifyReport(r, dept);
            if (!allowed) {
                showToast('權限不足：只有高層、管理處或案件發布者可以刪除。', 'warning', '權限不足');
                return;
            }
            if (!(await showConfirm('確定要刪除這筆案件報告嗎？', '刪除案件報告', '確定刪除'))) return;
            const collectionName = dept === 'police' ? 'policeReports' : 'fireReports';
            try {
                await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db, collectionName, caseId));
                if (dept === 'police') appData.policeReports = appData.policeReports.filter(x => x.caseId !== caseId);
                else appData.fireReports = appData.fireReports.filter(x => x.caseId !== caseId);

                // 刪除後自動壓縮該日流水號：001 刪除後 002→001、003→002……
                const dateMatch = String(caseId).match(/^(?:NTPD|NTFD)-(\d{8})\d+$/) || String(caseId).match(/^REP-(\d{8})\d+$/);
                if (dateMatch) await renumberDailyCaseReports(dateMatch[1], dept);
                await logAdminAction('刪除案件報告', caseId);
                if (dept === 'police') renderPoliceReports();
                else renderFireReports();
                renderMyProfile();
                showToast('案件已成功刪除。', 'success', '刪除成功');
            } catch (e) {
                showToast('刪除失敗：' + e.message, 'error', '刪除失敗');
            }
        }

        function openEditReportModal(dept, caseId) {
            // 退件後重新編輯：警察與消防都必須從各自的 Pending 集合讀取資料
            const isPending=dept==='police-pending-report' || dept==='fire-pending-report';
            const actualDept=isPending ? (dept==='fire-pending-report' ? 'fire' : 'police') : dept;
            const list=isPending ? (actualDept==='police' ? appData.policePendingReports : appData.firePendingReports) : (actualDept==='police' ? appData.policeReports : appData.fireReports);
            const r=list.find(x=>String(x.caseId)===String(caseId));
            if(!r){showToast('找不到這筆退件案件資料，請重新整理通知中心。','warning','案件不存在');return;}
            if(isPending){
                const owner=String(r.registeredByAccount||r.authorId||r.createdByAccount||'').toLowerCase();
                const me=String(currentUser?.account||'').toLowerCase();
                if(!currentUser || !owner || owner!==me){showToast('只有案件寄件人可以重新編輯送件。','warning','權限不足');return;}
                if(r.reviewStatus==='pending'){showToast('案件目前正在等待審核，暫時無法修改。','warning','等待審核');return;}
            } else if(!canModifyReport(r,actualDept)){showToast('權限不足：只有高層、管理處或案件發布者可以編輯。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            document.getElementById('edit-type').value=isPending?`${actualDept}-pending-report`:`${actualDept}-report`;
            document.getElementById('edit-id-key').value=caseId;
            document.getElementById('edit-modal-title').textContent=`編輯${actualDept==='police'?'警察':'消防'}案件報告 (${caseId})`;
            const canChangeId=!isPending && canChangeReportCaseId(actualDept);
            const videos=getReportVideoLinks(r);
            const container=document.getElementById('edit-fields-container');
            container.innerHTML=`
                <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-2">案件編號</label>
                    <input id="edit-rep-case-id" value="${r.caseId||caseId}" ${canChangeId?'':'readonly'} class="w-full bg-slate-950 ${canChangeId?'':'opacity-60 cursor-not-allowed'} border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white font-mono">
                    ${canChangeId?'<p class="text-[11px] text-amber-400 mt-1">僅管理中心與警察高層可修改案件編號。</p>':''}
                </div>
                <div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-2">案件標題</label><input id="edit-rep-title" value="${r.title||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white"></div>
                ${actualDept==='police'?`<div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4"><div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-2">嫌疑犯姓名</label><input id="edit-rep-sname" value="${r.suspectName||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white"></div><div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-2">身分證件 ID</label><input id="edit-rep-sid" value="${r.suspectId||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white font-mono"></div><div class="sm:col-span-2"><label class="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-2">嫌疑人車輛</label><input id="edit-rep-vehicle" value="${r.suspectVehicle||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white"></div></div><div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4"><div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-2">案件地點郵遞區號</label><input id="edit-rep-postal" value="${r.postalCode||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white font-mono"></div><div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-2">案件指揮</label><input id="edit-rep-command" value="${r.command||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white"></div></div><div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-2">支援單位（可複選）</label><details class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl"><summary class="cursor-pointer px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-300">編輯支援單位</summary><div id="edit-support-units" class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 p-3 border-t border-slate-800">${['偵查隊','霹靂小組','海巡署北部分署第八岸巡隊','交通分隊','新莊分局','消防局第二救災救護大隊'].map(u=>`<label class="flex items-center gap-2 min-h-[44px] p-2 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg"><input type="checkbox" name="edit-support-unit" value="${u}" ${(r.supportUnits||[]).includes(u)?'checked':''}><span class="text-xs">${u}</span></label>`).join('')}</div></details></div><div class="space-y-3 bg-slate-950/70 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl p-4"><div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"><div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">違反條例（可複選、可搜尋，可不填）</label><p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1">組織犯罪刑罰加重⅓。</p></div><span id="edit-rep-jail-total" class="shrink-0 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 text-xs font-semibold">總計 ${formatJailMinutes(r.jailMinutes)}</span></div><input id="edit-rep-violation-search" type="search" oninput="filterViolationOptions('edit-rep')" placeholder="搜尋條例名稱..." class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"><div id="edit-rep-violation-options" class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto pr-1"></div><label class="flex items-center gap-3 min-h-[44px] p-3 rounded-xl border border-amber-500/20 bg-amber-500/5 cursor-pointer"><input type="checkbox" id="edit-rep-organized-crime" onchange="updateViolationJailTotal('edit-rep')" ${r.organizedCrime?'checked':''} class="w-4 h-4 accent-amber-500"><span class="text-sm text-amber-200">組織犯罪刑罰加重⅓</span></label></div>`:``}
                
                <div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-2">詳細經過</label><textarea id="edit-rep-content" rows="5" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 text-sm text-white">${r.content||''}</textarea></div>
                <div class="space-y-2"><label class="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-2">相關影片連結（最多 3 部，可不填）</label>${[1,2,3].map((n,i)=>`<input type="url" id="edit-rep-video-${n}" value="${videos[i]||''}" placeholder="影片連結 ${n}（可留空）" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white">`).join('')}</div>`;
            if(actualDept==='police') renderViolationOptions('edit-rep', r.violations||[]);
            document.getElementById('modal-edit').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function openRemarkModal(collectionKey, docId) {
            if (collectionKey === 'emsReports') {
                if (!currentUser || !(currentUser.role === 'admin' || (currentUser.role === 'high' && userHasUnit('fire')))) {
                    showToast('只有消防高層與管理處可以新增EMS高層批示。', 'warning', '權限不足');
                    return;
                }
                const targetEMS = (appData.emsReports || []).find(x => String(x.docId || x.id) === String(docId));
                if (!targetEMS) return;
                document.getElementById('remark-target-type').value = 'emsReports';
                document.getElementById('remark-target-id-key').value = docId;
                document.getElementById('remark-text-input').value = '';
                renderRemarkHistory(targetEMS);
                document.getElementById('modal-remark').classList.remove('hidden');
                return;
            }
            if (!canRemarkReport(collectionKey === 'policeReports' ? 'police' : 'fire')) {
                showToast('只有高層、管理處或高階成員可以新增長官批示。', 'warning', '權限不足');
                return;
            }
            const targetList = collectionKey === 'policeReports' ? appData.policeReports : appData.fireReports;
            const target = targetList.find(x => x.caseId === docId);
            if (!target) return;
            document.getElementById('remark-target-type').value = collectionKey;
            document.getElementById('remark-target-id-key').value = docId;
            document.getElementById('remark-text-input').value = '';
            renderRemarkHistory(target);
            document.getElementById('modal-remark').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        async function submitRemark() {
            const collectionKey = document.getElementById('remark-target-type').value;
            const docId = document.getElementById('remark-target-id-key').value;
            const remarkText = document.getElementById('remark-text-input').value.trim();

            if (!remarkText) {
                alert('請輸入批示內容！');
                return;
            }

            if (collectionKey === 'emsReports') {
                const targetEMS=(appData.emsReports||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(docId)); if(!targetEMS)return;
                if(!currentUser || !(currentUser.role==='admin'||(currentUser.role==='high'&&userHasUnit('fire')))){showToast('只有消防高層與管理處可以批示EMS報告。','warning','權限不足');return;}
                const rr=getReportRemarks(targetEMS), now=new Date(); rr.push({authorName:currentUser.name,authorAccount:currentUser.account,role:currentUser.role,text:remarkText,timestamp:now.getTime(),time:formatDateTime(now.getTime())}); targetEMS.remarks=rr; targetEMS.remark=`${currentUser.name}: ${remarkText}`;
                try{const targetDocId=await resolveReportDocId('emsReports',targetEMS,docId); await fm.updateDoc(fm.doc(db,'emsReports',targetDocId),{remarks:rr,remark:targetEMS.remark}); targetEMS.docId=targetDocId;closeModal('modal-remark');renderEMSReports();showToast('EMS高層批示已新增。','success','批示成功');}catch(e){showToast('儲存失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');} return;
            }

            const targetList = collectionKey === 'policeReports' ? appData.policeReports : appData.fireReports;
            const target = targetList.find(x => x.caseId === docId);
            if (!target) return;

            const remarks = getReportRemarks(target);
            const now = new Date();
            const newRemark = {
                authorName: currentUser.name,
                authorAccount: currentUser.account,
                role: currentUser.role,
                text: remarkText,
                timestamp: now.getTime(),
                time: formatDateTime(now.getTime())
            };
            remarks.push(newRemark);
            target.remarks = remarks;
            target.remark = `${newRemark.authorName}: ${newRemark.text}`;

            try {
                const targetDocId=await resolveReportDocId(collectionKey,target,docId); await fm.updateDoc(fm.doc(db, collectionKey, targetDocId), { remarks: target.remarks, remark: target.remark }); target.docId=targetDocId;
                closeModal('modal-remark');
                if (collectionKey === 'policeReports') renderPoliceReports();
                else renderFireReports();
                showToast('長官批示已新增。', 'success', '批示成功');
            } catch (e) {
                showToast('儲存失敗：' + e.message, 'error', '操作失敗');
            }
        }

        async function submitEdit() {
            const editType=document.getElementById('edit-type').value;
            const idKey=document.getElementById('edit-id-key').value;

            if(editType.endsWith('-clock')){
                const dept=editType.split('-')[0],list=dept==='police'?appData.policeClocks:appData.fireClocks,c=list.find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(idKey));
                if(!canManageClockRecords()||!c){showToast('管理處權限不足。','warning','權限不足');return;}
                c.account=document.getElementById('edit-clock-account').value.trim();c.name=document.getElementById('edit-clock-name').value.trim();c.status=document.getElementById('edit-clock-status').value.trim();c.vehicle=document.getElementById('edit-clock-vehicle').value.trim();c.time=document.getElementById('edit-clock-time').value.trim();c.remark=document.getElementById('edit-clock-remark').value.trim();
                try{await fm.updateDoc(fm.doc(db,dept==='police'?'policeClocks':'fireClocks',c.docId||c.id),{account:c.account,name:c.name,status:c.status,vehicle:c.vehicle,time:c.time,remark:c.remark});await logAdminAction('編輯打卡紀錄',`${dept}:${c.docId||c.id}`);closeModal('modal-edit');dept==='police'?renderPoliceClocks():renderFireClocks();showToast('打卡紀錄已更新。','success','更新成功');}catch(e){showToast('更新失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}
                return;
            }

            if(editType==='punishment'){
                const p=(appData.policePunishments||[]).find(x=>String(x.id||'')===String(idKey)||String(x.docId||'')===String(idKey));if(!p)return;
                if(!canModifyPunishment(p)){showToast('權限不足：無法修改這筆獎懲。','warning','權限不足');return;}
                const memberId=document.getElementById('edit-punish-member-id').value.trim();const memberAcc=document.getElementById('edit-punish-member').value;const type=document.getElementById('edit-punish-type').value;const items=getSelectedPunishmentItems('edit-punish-item');const department=document.getElementById('edit-punish-department').value.trim();const authorName=document.getElementById('edit-punish-author-name').value.trim();const authorPos=document.getElementById('edit-punish-author-pos').value.trim();const reason=document.getElementById('edit-punish-reason').value.trim();
                if(!memberId||!authorName||!reason||!['reward','punishment'].includes(type)||!items.length){showToast('請完整填寫獎懲類型與至少一個獎懲項目。','warning','資料不完整');return;}
                const foundU=appData.users.find(u=>u.account===memberAcc)||appData.users.find(u=>String(u.staffNumber||'')===String(memberId));
                p.memberId=memberId;p.memberAccount=memberAcc||foundU?.account||p.memberAccount||'';p.memberName=foundU?foundU.name:(p.memberName||'未知員警');p.type=type;p.items=items;p.item=items.join('、');p.department=department;p.authorName=authorName;p.authorPos=authorPos;p.reason=reason;
                try{await fm.updateDoc(fm.doc(db,'policePunishments',p.docId||p.id),{memberId:p.memberId,memberAccount:p.memberAccount||'',memberName:p.memberName,type:p.type,items:p.items,item:p.item,department:p.department,authorName:p.authorName,authorPos:p.authorPos,reason:p.reason,updatedAt:Date.now(),updatedBy:currentUser?.account||''});await logAdminAction('編輯警用獎懲',p.id||p.docId);closeModal('modal-edit');renderPolicePunishments();updateAccessLocks();showToast('警用獎懲修改已成功儲存。','success','修改成功');}catch(e){showToast('更新失敗：'+e.message,'error','修改失敗');}
                return;
            }

            if(editType.includes('report')){
                const isPending = editType.endsWith('-pending-report');
                const dept=editType.split('-')[0];
                const list=isPending ? (dept==='police'?appData.policePendingReports:appData.firePendingReports) : (dept==='police'?appData.policeReports:appData.fireReports);
                const r=list.find(x=>x.caseId===idKey); if(!r)return;
                if(isPending){
                    if(!currentUser || String(r.registeredByAccount||'').toLowerCase()!==String(currentUser.account||'').toLowerCase()){showToast('只有案件寄件人可以修改待審核案件。','warning','權限不足');return;}
                    if(r.reviewStatus==='pending'){showToast('案件目前正在等待審核，暫時無法修改。','warning','等待審核');return;}
                } else if(!canModifyReport(r,dept)){showToast('權限不足：只有高層、管理處或案件發布者可以編輯。','warning','權限不足');return;}
                const requestedCaseId=(document.getElementById('edit-rep-case-id')?.value||r.caseId).trim();
                const nextCaseId=canChangeReportCaseId(dept)?requestedCaseId:r.caseId;
                if(!nextCaseId){showToast('案件編號不可為空白。','warning','案件編號錯誤');return;}
                const casePrefix=dept==='police'?'NTPD-':'NTFD-';
                // 案件編號只驗證前綴與數字長度：警察 NTPD-＋至少 10 碼數字；消防 NTFD-＋至少 10 碼數字。
                const caseIdPattern=dept==='police'?/^NTPD-\d{10,}$/:/^NTFD-\d{10,}$/;
                if(!caseIdPattern.test(nextCaseId)){showToast(`案件編號格式錯誤，${dept==='police'?'警察請使用 NTPD-':'消防請使用 NTFD-'} 加至少 10 碼數字。`,'warning','案件編號錯誤');return;}
                if(nextCaseId!==r.caseId){
                    const duplicate=[...(appData.policeReports||[]),...(appData.fireReports||[])].some(x=>x.caseId===nextCaseId&&x.caseId!==r.caseId);
                    if(duplicate){showToast('新的案件編號已存在，請重新輸入。','warning','案件編號重複');return;}
                }
                r.title=document.getElementById('edit-rep-title').value.trim();r.content=document.getElementById('edit-rep-content').value.trim();
                if(dept==='police'){r.suspectName=document.getElementById('edit-rep-sname').value.trim();r.suspectId=document.getElementById('edit-rep-sid').value.trim();r.suspectVehicle=document.getElementById('edit-rep-vehicle').value.trim();r.postalCode=document.getElementById('edit-rep-postal')?.value.trim()||'';r.command=document.getElementById('edit-rep-command')?.value.trim()||'';r.supportUnits=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#edit-support-units input[name="edit-support-unit"]:checked')).map(cb=>cb.value);}else{r.suspectName='';r.suspectId='';r.suspectVehicle='';}
                if(dept==='police'){const vd=getViolationSelection('edit-rep');r.violations=vd.violations;r.organizedCrime=vd.organizedCrime;r.jailMinutes=vd.jailMinutes;}else{delete r.violations;delete r.organizedCrime;delete r.jailMinutes;}
                r.videoLinks=[1,2,3].map(n=>document.getElementById(`edit-rep-video-${n}`)?.value.trim()).filter(Boolean);r.videoLink=r.videoLinks[0]||'';
                try{
                    const collectionName=isPending?(dept==='police'?'policePendingReports':'firePendingReports'):(dept==='police'?'policeReports':'fireReports');
                    const originalAuthorId=r.authorId||r.createdByAccount||r.registeredByAccount||r.authorAccount||currentUser.account;
                    const originalAuthorName=r.authorName||r.createdByName||r.registeredByName||currentUser.name;
                    const payload={caseId:nextCaseId,title:r.title,content:r.content,authorId:originalAuthorId,authorName:originalAuthorName,suspectName:r.suspectName,suspectId:r.suspectId,suspectVehicle:r.suspectVehicle||'',postalCode:r.postalCode||'',command:r.command||'',supportUnits:r.supportUnits||[],...(dept==='police'?{violations:r.violations||[],organizedCrime:!!r.organizedCrime,jailMinutes:Number(r.jailMinutes||0)}:{}),videoLinks:r.videoLinks||[],videoLink:r.videoLink||'',createdByAccount:r.createdByAccount||r.registeredByAccount||originalAuthorId,createdByName:r.createdByName||r.registeredByName||originalAuthorName,registeredByAccount:r.registeredByAccount||r.createdByAccount||originalAuthorId,registeredByName:r.registeredByName||r.createdByName||originalAuthorName,lastEditedByAccount:currentUser.account,lastEditedByName:currentUser.name,lastEditedAt:Date.now()};
                    if(isPending){
                        const pendingPayload={...payload,reviewStatus:'pending',rejectionReason:'',reviewedAt:0,reviewedBy:'',submittedAt:Date.now(),updatedAt:Date.now()};
                        const pendingCollection = dept==='police'?'policePendingReports':'firePendingReports';
                        const pendingDocId = await resolveReportDocId(pendingCollection, r, r.caseId);
                        if(!pendingDocId) throw new Error('找不到退件案件的 Firestore 文件 ID');
                        await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,pendingCollection,pendingDocId),pendingPayload,{merge:true});
                        r.docId=pendingDocId;
                        Object.assign(r,pendingPayload);
                        await removePendingReportNotifications(nextCaseId,currentUser.account);const notification={type:'report',caseId:nextCaseId,recipientAccount:currentUser.account,recipientAccountNormalized:String(currentUser.account||'').trim().toLowerCase(),status:'pending',title:'案件已重新送件',message:`案件 ${nextCaseId} 已重新送入高層審核。`,timestamp:Date.now(),read:false};
                        const nref=fm.doc(fm.collection(db,'reportNotifications')); await fm.setDoc(nref,notification); appData.reportNotifications.unshift({docId:nref.id,...notification});
                        await logAdminAction('案件退件後重新送件',nextCaseId); closeModal('modal-edit'); renderMyProfile(); dept==='police'?renderPoliceReview():renderFireReview(); showSubmitSuccessAnimation('重新送件成功',`案件 ${nextCaseId} 已重新送出，等待高層審核。`);
                    } else if(nextCaseId!==r.caseId){
                        const oldDocId=await resolveReportDocId(collectionName,r,r.caseId);
                        await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,collectionName,nextCaseId),{...r,...payload});
                        if(oldDocId && oldDocId!==nextCaseId) await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,collectionName,oldDocId));
                        r.caseId=nextCaseId;r.docId=nextCaseId;
                        await logAdminAction('編輯案件報告',nextCaseId);closeModal('modal-edit');dept==='police'?renderPoliceReports():renderFireReports();renderMyProfile();showToast('案件修改已成功儲存。','success','修改成功');
                    }else{
                        const actualDocId=await resolveReportDocId(collectionName,r,r.caseId);
                        if(!actualDocId) throw new Error('找不到案件的 Firestore 文件 ID');
                        await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,collectionName,actualDocId),payload,{merge:true});
                        r.docId=actualDocId;
                        await logAdminAction('編輯案件報告',nextCaseId);closeModal('modal-edit');dept==='police'?renderPoliceReports():renderFireReports();renderMyProfile();showToast('案件修改已成功儲存。','success','修改成功');
                    }
                }catch(e){showToast('更新失敗：'+e.message,'error','修改失敗');}
            }
        }

        function localDateKey(timestamp){const d=new Date(Number(timestamp||0));if(!Number.isFinite(d.getTime()))return '';return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`;}
        function renderPoliceClocks() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('police-clocks-tbody');
            const dateFilter = document.getElementById('police-clocks-date-filter')?.value || '';
            const statusFilter = document.getElementById('police-clocks-status-filter')?.value || 'all';
            const countEl = document.getElementById('police-current-duty-count');
            if (!tbody) return;

            const dutyCount = latestDutyCount(appData.policeClocks || []);
            if (countEl) countEl.textContent = String(dutyCount);

            const filtered = (appData.policeClocks || []).filter(c => {
                if (dateFilter && localDateKey(c.timestamp) !== dateFilter) return false;
                if (statusFilter !== 'all' && String(c.status || '') !== statusFilter) return false;
                return true;
            }).sort((a,b) => Number(b.timestamp || 0) - Number(a.timestamp || 0));

            tbody.innerHTML = '';
            if (filtered.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="6" class="p-6 text-center text-slate-500">尚無符合目前篩選條件的警察出勤打卡紀錄。</td></tr>`;
                return;
            }

            filtered.forEach(c => {
                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.className = "hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition";
                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td class="p-4 font-medium text-white">${escapeHtml(c.name || '--')} (${escapeHtml(c.account || '--')})</td>
                    <td class="p-4"><span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold ${c.status === '上班' ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20' : 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/20'}">${escapeHtml(c.status || '--')}</span></td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-slate-300 font-mono">${escapeHtml(c.vehicle || '無')}</td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-slate-300 text-xs">${escapeHtml(c.time || formatDateTime(c.timestamp))}</td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-slate-400 text-xs">${escapeHtml(c.remark || '無')}</td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-right">
                        ${canManageClockRecords() ? `<div class="flex gap-2 justify-end"><button type="button" onclick="openEditClockModal('police', '${String(c.docId || c.id || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" class="min-h-[40px] px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs">編輯</button><button type="button" onclick="deleteClockRecord('police', '${String(c.docId || c.id || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" class="min-h-[40px] px-2.5 py-1 bg-red-600/20 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs">刪除</button></div>` : ''}
                    </td>
                `;
                tbody.appendChild(tr);
            });
        }

        function renderFireClocks() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('fire-clocks-tbody');
            const dateFilter = document.getElementById('fire-clocks-date-filter').value;
            if (!tbody) return;

            tbody.innerHTML = '';
            const filtered = appData.fireClocks.filter(c => {
                if (!dateFilter) return true;
                return c.time.startsWith(dateFilter);
            });

            if (filtered.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="6" class="p-6 text-center text-slate-500">尚無符合條件的消防出勤打卡紀錄。</td></tr>`;
                return;
            }

            filtered.forEach(c => {
                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.className = "hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition";
                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td class="p-4 font-medium text-white">${escapeHtml(c.name||'--')} (${escapeHtml(c.account||'--')})</td>
                    <td class="p-4"><span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold ${c.status === '上班' ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20' : 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/20'}">${c.status}</span></td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-slate-300 font-mono">${escapeHtml(c.vehicle||'無')}</td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-slate-300 text-xs">${escapeHtml(c.time||'--')}</td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-slate-400 text-xs">${escapeHtml(c.remark||'無')}</td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-right">
                        ${canManageClockRecords() ? `<div class="flex gap-2 justify-end"><button onclick="openEditClockModal('fire', '${c.docId || c.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs">編輯</button><button onclick="deleteClockRecord('fire', '${c.docId || c.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-red-600/20 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs">刪除</button></div>` : ''}
                    </td>
                `;
                tbody.appendChild(tr);
            });
        }

        async function deleteClockRecord(dept, clockId) {
            if (!canManageClockRecords()) { showToast('只有管理處可以刪除打卡紀錄。','warning','權限不足'); return; }
            if (!(await showConfirm('確定要刪除這筆打卡紀錄嗎？', '刪除打卡紀錄', '確定刪除'))) return;
            const collectionName = dept === 'police' ? 'policeClocks' : 'fireClocks';
            try {
                await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db, collectionName, clockId));
                if (dept === 'police') appData.policeClocks = appData.policeClocks.filter(x => String(x.docId || x.id) !== String(clockId));
                else appData.fireClocks = appData.fireClocks.filter(x => String(x.docId || x.id) !== String(clockId));

                if (dept === 'police') renderPoliceClocks();
                else renderFireClocks();
                showSubmitSuccessAnimation('移除成功', '打卡紀錄已成功移除。');
            } catch (e) {
                showToast('刪除失敗：' + e.message, 'error', '操作失敗');
            }
        }

        function openEditClockModal(dept,id){if(!canManageClockRecords()){showToast('只有管理處可以編輯打卡紀錄。','warning','權限不足');return;}const c=(dept==='police'?appData.policeClocks:appData.fireClocks).find(x=>String(x.id||x.docId)===String(id));if(!c)return;document.getElementById('edit-type').value=dept+'-clock';document.getElementById('edit-id-key').value=c.docId||c.id;document.getElementById('edit-modal-title').textContent='編輯打卡紀錄';document.getElementById('edit-fields-container').innerHTML=`<input id="edit-clock-account" value="${c.account||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm text-white"><input id="edit-clock-name" value="${c.name||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm text-white"><input id="edit-clock-status" value="${c.status||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm text-white"><input id="edit-clock-vehicle" value="${c.vehicle||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm text-white"><input id="edit-clock-time" value="${c.time||''}" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm text-white"><textarea id="edit-clock-remark" class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3 text-sm text-white">${c.remark||''}</textarea>`;document.getElementById('modal-edit').classList.remove('hidden');}

        function calculateMemberHours(dept) {
            const memberEl = document.getElementById(`${dept}-calc-member`);
            const startInput = document.getElementById(`${dept}-calc-start`).value;
            const endInput = document.getElementById(`${dept}-calc-end`).value;
            const resultBox = document.getElementById(`${dept}-calc-result`);

            const query = String(memberEl?.value || '').trim();
            if (!query || !startInput || !endInput) {
                alert(dept === 'police' ? '請輸入員警編號／登入帳號並輸入起始與結束年月日區間！' : '請完整選擇人員並輸入起始與結束年月日區間！');
                return;
            }

            const startDate = new Date(startInput + 'T00:00:00');
            const endDate = new Date(endInput + 'T23:59:59');

            if (startDate > endDate) {
                alert('起始日期不得晚於結束日期！');
                return;
            }

            const clocks = dept === 'police' ? (appData.policeClocks || []) : (appData.fireClocks || []);
            const q = query.toLowerCase();

            // 警察查詢改為手動輸入：可比對員警編號 staffNumber、登入帳號 account，
            // 同時支援舊打卡資料直接使用的 account 欄位。
            let matchedAccounts = new Set([query]);
            let foundU = null;
            if (dept === 'police') {
                foundU = (appData.users || []).find(u =>
                    String(u.staffNumber || '').trim().toLowerCase() === q ||
                    String(u.account || '').trim().toLowerCase() === q
                );
                if (foundU) {
                    if (foundU.account) matchedAccounts.add(String(foundU.account).trim());
                    if (foundU.staffNumber) matchedAccounts.add(String(foundU.staffNumber).trim());
                }
            } else {
                foundU = (appData.users || []).find(u => String(u.account || '').trim().toLowerCase() === q);
                if (foundU?.account) matchedAccounts.add(String(foundU.account).trim());
            }

            const userClocks = clocks.filter(c => {
                const account = String(c.account || '').trim();
                const staffNumber = String(c.staffNumber || '').trim();
                const matchesPerson = matchedAccounts.has(account) ||
                    matchedAccounts.has(staffNumber) ||
                    account.toLowerCase() === q ||
                    staffNumber.toLowerCase() === q ||
                    (foundU && (
                        account.toLowerCase() === String(foundU.account || '').trim().toLowerCase() ||
                        staffNumber.toLowerCase() === String(foundU.staffNumber || '').trim().toLowerCase()
                    ));
                return matchesPerson && Number(c.timestamp || 0) >= startDate.getTime() && Number(c.timestamp || 0) <= endDate.getTime();
            });
            userClocks.sort((a, b) => Number(a.timestamp || 0) - Number(b.timestamp || 0));

            let totalMinutes = 0;
            let lastOnDutyTime = null;

            userClocks.forEach(c => {
                if (c.status === '上班') {
                    lastOnDutyTime = Number(c.timestamp || 0);
                } else if (c.status === '下班' && lastOnDutyTime !== null) {
                    const diff = Number(c.timestamp || 0) - lastOnDutyTime;
                    if (diff > 0) totalMinutes += diff / (1000 * 60);
                    lastOnDutyTime = null;
                }
            });

            const totalHours = (totalMinutes / 60).toFixed(1);
            const displayId = foundU?.staffNumber || foundU?.account || query;
            const displayName = foundU?.name || userClocks[0]?.name || query;

            resultBox.classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById(`${dept}-calc-member-name`).textContent = `${displayName} (${displayId})`;
            document.getElementById(`${dept}-calc-total-hours`).textContent = `${totalHours} 小時`;
            document.getElementById(`${dept}-calc-details`).textContent = `統計區間：${startInput} 至 ${endInput} | 共 ${userClocks.length} 筆打卡紀錄`;
        }

        const USER_PERMISSION_DEFINITIONS = {
            normal: '一般基層人員',
            high: '高層長官',
            senior: '高階成員',
            admin: '最高管理員',
            academyInstructor: '警專教官'
        };
        const USER_ROLE_PRIORITY = ['admin','high','senior','normal'];
        function getUserPermissions(u) {
            if (!u) return [];
            const raw = Array.isArray(u.permissions) && u.permissions.length ? u.permissions : (u.role ? [u.role] : []);
            const perms = [...new Set(raw.filter(x => USER_PERMISSION_DEFINITIONS[x]))];
            if (u.role && USER_PERMISSION_DEFINITIONS[u.role] && !perms.includes(u.role)) perms.push(u.role);
            return perms;
        }
        function getEffectiveRole(u) {
            const perms = getUserPermissions(u);
            return USER_ROLE_PRIORITY.find(x => perms.includes(x)) || 'normal';
        }
        function hasUserPermission(u, permission) { return getUserPermissions(u).includes(permission); }
        function isAcademyInstructor(u=currentUser) { return !!u && (hasUserPermission(u,'academyInstructor') || getEffectiveRole(u)==='admin'); }
        function canAccessManagementCenter(u=currentUser) { return !!u && (getEffectiveRole(u)==='admin' || hasUserPermission(u,'academyInstructor')); }
        function permissionDisplayText(u) {
            return getUserPermissions(u).map(p => USER_PERMISSION_DEFINITIONS[p]).join('、') || '未設定';
        }
        function getUnitDisplayNames(u) {
            const units = u.units || [u.unit]; const names=[];
            if (units.includes('police')) names.push(`警察／${u.policeDivision || '未指定分隊'}`);
            if (units.includes('fire')) names.push(`消防／${u.fireDivision || '未指定單位'}`);
            if (units.includes('admin')) names.push('總管理處');
            return names.join('、') || '未指定';
        }
        function syncUnitDivisionVisibility(prefix) {
            const p=document.querySelector(`input[name="${prefix}-user-units"][value="police"]`), f=document.querySelector(`input[name="${prefix}-user-units"][value="fire"]`);
            const ps=document.getElementById(`${prefix}-user-police-division`), fs=document.getElementById(`${prefix}-user-fire-division`);
            if (ps) { const enabled=!!p?.checked; ps.disabled=!enabled; ps.classList.toggle('opacity-50', !enabled); }
            if (fs) { const enabled=!!f?.checked; fs.disabled=!enabled; fs.classList.toggle('opacity-50', !enabled); }
        }
        function validateUnitSelections(units, policeDivision, fireDivision) {
            if (units.includes('police') && !policeDivision) { showToast('選擇警察身分時，必須指定警察所屬單位。','warning','資料不完整'); return false; }
            if (units.includes('fire') && !fireDivision) { showToast('選擇消防身分時，必須指定消防所屬單位。','warning','資料不完整'); return false; }
            return true;
        }

        function getDeviceInfo(){const n=window.navigator||{};const sc=window.screen||{};const ua=n.userAgent||'';const browser=/Edg\//.test(ua)?'Edge':/Chrome\//.test(ua)?'Chrome':/Firefox\//.test(ua)?'Firefox':/Safari\//.test(ua)&&!/Chrome\//.test(ua)?'Safari':'其他';return {userAgent:ua,platform:n.platform||'',browser,screen:`${sc.width||0}x${sc.height||0}`,touchPoints:n.maxTouchPoints||0};}
        function getDeviceLabel(d){return `${d?.platform||'未知平台'} / ${d?.browser||'其他瀏覽器'}`;}
        function getDeviceFingerprint(device=getDeviceInfo()){const ua=String(device?.userAgent||'');const browser=device?.browser||( /Edg\//.test(ua)?'Edge':/Chrome\//.test(ua)?'Chrome':/Firefox\//.test(ua)?'Firefox':/Safari\//.test(ua)&&!/Chrome\//.test(ua)?'Safari':'其他');return btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent([ua,device?.platform||'',browser,device?.screen||'',device?.touchPoints||0].join('|')))).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g,'').slice(0,80);}
        // 裝置層級識別：刻意不包含帳號、IP 或瀏覽器名稱，讓同一裝置更換帳號／瀏覽器時仍可套用自動封鎖。
        // 仍僅使用目前系統已取得的 platform、螢幕尺寸與觸控資訊；瀏覽器無法保證跨瀏覽器辨識同一實體裝置。
        function getDeviceKey(device=getDeviceInfo()){return btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent([device?.platform||'',device?.screen||'',device?.touchPoints||0].join('|')))).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g,'').slice(0,80);}
        function isCurrentDeviceBlocked(){
            const device=getDeviceInfo(),fp=getDeviceFingerprint(device),deviceKey=getDeviceKey(device);
            return (appData.blockedDevices||[]).some(x=>x.active!==false && (String(x.fingerprint||'')===String(fp) || (x.deviceKey && String(x.deviceKey)===String(deviceKey))));
        }
        function findDeviceSessionByFingerprint(fingerprint){
            return (appData.loginSessions||[]).find(x=>{const xfp=x.fingerprint||getDeviceFingerprint(x.device||{});return String(xfp)===String(fingerprint);});
        }
        async function clearLoginFailureRecord(deviceKey){
            if(!deviceKey||!fm||!db)return;
            try{await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'loginFailures',deviceKey));}catch(e){}
            appData.loginFailures=(appData.loginFailures||[]).filter(x=>String(x.docId||x.deviceKey)!==String(deviceKey));
        }
        async function registerLoginFailure(device){
            if(!fm||!db)return false;
            const now=Date.now(),windowStart=now-5*60*1000,deviceKey=getDeviceKey(device),fingerprint=getDeviceFingerprint(device);
            try{
                const local=(appData.loginFailures||[]).find(x=>String(x.docId||x.deviceKey)===String(deviceKey));
                let attempts=Array.isArray(local?.attempts)?local.attempts.filter(t=>Number(t)>windowStart):[];
                // 以雲端最新資料為準，避免重新整理頁面後遺失失敗次數。
                const failureDoc=await fm.getDoc(fm.doc(db,'loginFailures',deviceKey));
                if(failureDoc.exists()){
                    const cloud=failureDoc.data()||{};
                    attempts=Array.isArray(cloud.attempts)?cloud.attempts.filter(t=>Number(t)>windowStart):attempts;
                }
                attempts.push(now);
                const payload={deviceKey,fingerprint,device,attempts,lastFailedAt:now};
                await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'loginFailures',deviceKey),payload,{merge:true});
                const idx=(appData.loginFailures||[]).findIndex(x=>String(x.docId||x.deviceKey)===String(deviceKey));
                if(idx>=0)appData.loginFailures[idx]={docId:deviceKey,...payload};else appData.loginFailures.push({docId:deviceKey,...payload});
                if(attempts.length>=8){
                    const existing=(appData.blockedDevices||[]).find(x=>x.active!==false&&String(x.deviceKey||'')===String(deviceKey));
                    if(!existing){
                        const obj={fingerprint,deviceKey,active:true,autoBlocked:true,reason:'5分鐘內連續輸入錯誤密碼達8次',blockedAt:now,blockedBy:'系統自動封鎖',device};
                        await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'blockedDevices',fingerprint),obj,{merge:true});
                        const bi=(appData.blockedDevices||[]).findIndex(x=>String(x.docId||x.fingerprint)===String(fingerprint));
                        if(bi>=0)appData.blockedDevices[bi]={docId:fingerprint,...obj};else appData.blockedDevices.push({docId:fingerprint,...obj});
                        await logAdminAction('自動封鎖登入裝置','5分鐘內錯誤密碼8次／'+deviceKey,`裝置已因 5 分鐘內連續輸入錯誤帳號／密碼達 8 次而自動上鎖。裝置：${getDeviceLabel(device)}；螢幕：${device?.screen||'--'}。`);
                        sendManagementDiscordLog('裝置自動上鎖','5分鐘內錯誤帳號／密碼達 8 次',`裝置識別：${deviceKey}；裝置：${getDeviceLabel(device)}；螢幕：${device?.screen||'--'}；系統已自動建立封鎖紀錄。`,{mentionRole:true});
                    }
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            }catch(e){console.warn('login failure audit',e);return false;}
        }
        async function recordLoginSession(user){if(!user||!fm||!db)return;const now=Date.now(),device=getDeviceInfo(),fingerprint=getDeviceFingerprint(device),deviceKey=getDeviceKey(device),id=`LOGIN-${String(user.account).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_')}-${now}`,session={id,account:user.account,name:user.name,loginAt:now,time:formatDateTime(now),device,fingerprint,deviceKey};try{await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'loginSessions',id),session);await fm.updateDoc(fm.doc(db,'users',user.docId||user.account),{online:true,lastSeen:now,lastLoginAt:now,lastDevice:device});user.online=true;user.lastSeen=now;user.lastLoginAt=now;user.lastDevice=device;appData.loginSessions.unshift({docId:id,...session});}catch(e){console.warn('login audit',e);}}
        async function pruneOldLoginSessions() {
            if (!currentUser || currentUser.role !== 'admin' || !fm || !db) return;
            const cutoff = Date.now() - 3 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
            const oldSessions = (appData.loginSessions || []).filter(x => Number(x.loginAt || 0) > 0 && Number(x.loginAt) < cutoff);
            if (!oldSessions.length) return;
            for (const session of oldSessions) {
                try { await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db, 'loginSessions', session.docId || session.id)); } catch (e) { console.warn('login session cleanup', e); }
            }
            appData.loginSessions = (appData.loginSessions || []).filter(x => Number(x.loginAt || 0) >= cutoff);
        }

        async function updateCurrentUserPresence(online){if(!currentUser||!fm||!db)return;const now=Date.now();currentUser.online=!!online;currentUser.lastSeen=online?now:0;const localUser=appData.users.find(u=>String(u.account)===String(currentUser.account));if(localUser){localUser.online=currentUser.online;localUser.lastSeen=currentUser.lastSeen;}try{await fm.updateDoc(fm.doc(db,'users',currentUser.docId||currentUser.account),{online:!!online,lastSeen:online?now:0});}catch(e){}}
