        function isUserOnline(u){return !!u?.online&&Number(u.lastSeen||0)>0&&(Date.now()-Number(u.lastSeen))<90000;}
        async function refreshAdminOnlineUsers(){if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='admin'||!fm||!db)return;try{const snap=await fm.getDocs(fm.collection(db,'users'));appData.users=snap.docs.map(d=>{const data=d.data();return {docId:d.id,...data,units:data.units||(data.unit?[data.unit]:['admin']),permissions:Array.isArray(data.permissions)&&data.permissions.length?data.permissions:(data.role?[data.role]:[])};});if(currentUser){const fresh=appData.users.find(u=>String(u.account)===String(currentUser.account));if(fresh)currentUser=fresh;}renderAdminOnlineUsers();}catch(e){renderAdminOnlineUsers();}}
        function startAdminPresenceRefresh(){clearInterval(window.__adminPresenceTimer);window.__adminPresenceTimer=setInterval(()=>{if(currentUser&&currentUser.role==='admin'&&!document.getElementById('tab-admin')?.classList.contains('hidden'))refreshAdminOnlineUsers();},15000);}
        function stopAdminPresenceRefresh(){clearInterval(window.__adminPresenceTimer);window.__adminPresenceTimer=null;}
        function renderAdminOnlineUsers(){const c=document.getElementById('admin-online-list');if(!c)return;c.innerHTML=(appData.users||[]).map(u=>{const on=isUserOnline(u);const name=escapeHtml(u.name||'--'),account=escapeHtml(u.account||'--'),staff=u.staffNumber?`／${escapeHtml(u.staffNumber)}`:'';return `<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between"><div><b class="text-white">${name}</b><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono">${account}${staff}</div></div><span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs ${on?'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400':'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400'}">● ${on?'在線':'離線'}</span></div>`;}).join('');}
        function renderAdminDevices(){const t=document.getElementById('admin-devices-tbody');if(!t)return;const since=Date.now()-3*24*60*60*1000,rows=(appData.loginSessions||[]).filter(x=>Number(x.loginAt||0)>=since).sort((a,b)=>b.loginAt-a.loginAt);const blocked=(row)=>{const fp=row.fingerprint||getDeviceFingerprint(row.device||{}),dk=row.deviceKey||getDeviceKey(row.device||{});return (appData.blockedDevices||[]).some(x=>x.active!==false&&(String(x.fingerprint||'')===String(fp)||(x.deviceKey&&String(x.deviceKey)===String(dk))));};t.innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(x=>`<tr><td class="p-3 font-mono text-white">${x.account}</td><td class="p-3 text-slate-300">${formatDateTime(x.loginAt,x.time)}</td><td class="p-3 text-slate-300">${getDeviceLabel(x.device)}</td><td class="p-3 text-slate-400">${x.device?.screen||'--'}</td><td class="p-3 text-slate-400 font-mono">${x.fingerprint||getDeviceFingerprint(x.device)}</td><td class="p-3 text-right">${blocked(x)?`<button onclick="unblockDevice('${x.fingerprint||getDeviceFingerprint(x.device)}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-600/20 text-emerald-400 rounded-lg text-xs">解封裝置</button>`:`<button onclick="blockDevice('${x.fingerprint||getDeviceFingerprint(x.device)}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-red-600/20 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs">封鎖裝置</button>`}</td></tr>`).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="6" class="p-6 text-center text-slate-500">最近 3 日尚無登入裝置紀錄。</td></tr>';const clear=document.getElementById('clear-all-login-sessions-btn');if(clear)clear.classList.toggle('hidden',!isSuperAdminAccount(currentUser?.account));const bc=document.getElementById('admin-blocked-devices-list');if(bc){const br=(appData.blockedDevices||[]).filter(x=>x.active!==false);bc.innerHTML=br.length?br.map(x=>`<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-red-500/10 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-mono text-xs text-red-300">${x.fingerprint||'--'}</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">封鎖人員：${x.blockedBy||'--'} ／ ${formatDateTime(x.blockedAt)}</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">${getDeviceLabel(x.device)} ／ ${x.device?.screen||'--'}</div><div class="text-xs ${x.autoBlocked?'text-amber-300':'text-slate-500'} mt-1">${x.reason||'管理員手動封鎖'}</div></div><button onclick="unblockDevice('${x.fingerprint}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600/20 text-emerald-400 rounded-lg text-xs">解封</button></div>`).join(''):'<div class="text-xs text-slate-500 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4">目前沒有被封鎖的裝置。</div>';}}

        async function deleteAllLoginSessions(){if(!isSuperAdminAccount(currentUser?.account)){showToast('只有 Dsuensx 可以刪除所有登入紀錄。','warning','權限不足');return;}if(!(await showConfirm('確定要一鍵刪除所有登入紀錄嗎？此操作會留下管理稽核紀錄。','刪除全部登入紀錄','確定刪除')))return;try{for(const x of [...(appData.loginSessions||[])]){await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'loginSessions',x.docId||x.id));}appData.loginSessions=[];await logAdminAction('一鍵刪除所有登入紀錄','loginSessions/*');renderAdminDevices();showToast('所有登入紀錄已刪除。','success','操作完成');}catch(e){showToast('刪除登入紀錄失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}}

        async function blockDevice(fingerprint){
            if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='admin'){showToast('只有最高管理員可以封鎖裝置。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            if(!fingerprint)return;
            const session=findDeviceSessionByFingerprint(fingerprint),targetDevice=session?.device||getDeviceInfo(),deviceKey=getDeviceKey(targetDevice),existing=(appData.blockedDevices||[]).find(x=>String(x.fingerprint)===String(fingerprint)||String(x.deviceKey||'')===String(deviceKey));
            if(existing&&existing.active!==false){showToast('此裝置目前已在封鎖名單中。','warning','裝置已封鎖');return;}
            if(!(await showConfirm('確定要封鎖此裝置嗎？系統只會使用現有的裝置、瀏覽器與螢幕資訊，不會蒐集 IP。','封鎖登入裝置','確定封鎖')))return;
            const now=Date.now(),obj={fingerprint,deviceKey,active:true,autoBlocked:false,blockedAt:now,blockedBy:currentUser.account,device:session?.device||existing?.device||targetDevice};
            try{
                await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'blockedDevices',fingerprint),obj);
                const idx=(appData.blockedDevices||[]).findIndex(x=>String(x.fingerprint)===String(fingerprint));
                if(idx>=0) appData.blockedDevices[idx]={docId:fingerprint,...obj}; else appData.blockedDevices.push({docId:fingerprint,...obj});
                await logAdminAction('封鎖登入裝置',fingerprint);renderAdminDevices();showToast('裝置已封鎖。','success','操作完成');
            }catch(e){showToast('封鎖失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}
        }
        async function unblockDevice(fingerprint){
            if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='admin'){showToast('只有最高管理員可以解封裝置。','warning','權限不足');return;}
            if(!(await showConfirm('確定要解封此裝置嗎？解封後，該裝置即可再次嘗試登入。','解封登入裝置','確定解封')))return;
            try{
                const target=(appData.blockedDevices||[]).find(x=>String(x.fingerprint||'')===String(fingerprint));
                const targetDeviceKey=target?.deviceKey||getDeviceKey(target?.device||{});
                const matches=(appData.blockedDevices||[]).filter(x=>x.active!==false&&(String(x.fingerprint||'')===String(fingerprint)||(targetDeviceKey&&String(x.deviceKey||'')===String(targetDeviceKey))));
                for(const row of matches){
                    const docId=row.docId||row.fingerprint;
                    if(docId)await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'blockedDevices',docId),{active:false,unblockedAt:Date.now(),unblockedBy:currentUser.account},{merge:true});
                    row.active=false;row.unblockedAt=Date.now();row.unblockedBy=currentUser.account;
                }
                await logAdminAction('解封登入裝置',targetDeviceKey||fingerprint);renderAdminDevices();showToast('裝置已解封。','success','操作完成');
            }catch(e){showToast('解封失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}
        }

        async function submitFeedback(){if(!currentUser){showToast('請先登入後再回報。','warning','需要登入');return;}const subject=document.getElementById('feedback-subject').value.trim(),details=document.getElementById('feedback-details').value.trim();if(!subject||!details){showToast('請填寫主旨與詳細資訊。','error','資料不完整');return;}const now=Date.now(),id=`FB-${now}`,obj={id,subject,details,account:currentUser.account,name:currentUser.name,staffNumber:currentUser.staffNumber||'',time:formatDateTime(now),timestamp:now,status:'未處理'};try{await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'feedbackReports',id),obj);appData.feedbackReports.unshift({docId:id,...obj});closeModal('modal-feedback');showToast('回報已送至管理中心。','success','回報成功');renderAdminFeedback();}catch(e){showToast('回報送出失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}}
        function openFeedbackModal(){if(!currentUser){showToast('請先登入後再回報。','warning','需要登入');return;}document.getElementById('feedback-account-preview').textContent=`${currentUser.name} (${currentUser.account})`;document.getElementById('modal-feedback').classList.remove('hidden');}
        function renderAdminFeedback(){const c=document.getElementById('admin-feedback-list');if(!c)return;const rows=(appData.feedbackReports||[]).sort((a,b)=>b.timestamp-a.timestamp);c.innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(r=>{const id=String(r.docId||r.id||'').replace(/'/g,"\'");const subject=escapeHtml(r.subject||'無主旨'),name=escapeHtml(r.name||'--'),account=escapeHtml(r.account||'--'),staff=r.staffNumber?`／${escapeHtml(r.staffNumber)}`:'',details=escapeHtml(r.details||''),note=escapeHtml(r.adminNote||''),status=escapeHtml(r.status||'未處理'),time=escapeHtml(formatDateTime(r.timestamp,r.time));const noteValue=String(r.adminNote||'').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');return `<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 space-y-3"><div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2"><div><h4 class="font-bold text-white">${subject}</h4><div class="text-xs text-cyan-400 font-mono mt-1">${name} (${account})${staff}</div></div><span class="text-xs text-slate-500">${time}</span></div><div class="bg-slate-950 rounded-xl p-4 text-sm text-slate-300 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">${details}</div><div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-[160px_1fr_auto] gap-2"><select id="fb-status-${escapeHtml(id)}" class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white"><option ${status==='未處理'?'selected':''}>未處理</option><option ${status==='處理中'?'selected':''}>處理中</option><option ${status==='已處理'?'selected':''}>已處理</option></select><input id="fb-note-${escapeHtml(id)}" value="${noteValue}" placeholder="管理員備注..." class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white"><div class="flex gap-2"><button type="button" onclick="saveFeedbackAdmin('${id}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-blue-600/20 text-blue-400 rounded-xl text-xs">儲存備注</button><button type="button" onclick="deleteFeedback('${id}')" class="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600/20 text-red-400 rounded-xl text-xs">刪除訊息</button></div></div>${note?`<div class="text-xs text-amber-300 bg-amber-500/5 border border-amber-500/10 rounded-xl p-3">管理員備注：${note}</div>`:''}</div>`}).join(''):'<div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-8 text-center text-slate-500">目前沒有回報內容。</div>';}
        async function saveFeedbackAdmin(id){if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='admin')return;const r=appData.feedbackReports.find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id));if(!r)return;const status=document.getElementById(`fb-status-${id}`)?.value||'未處理',adminNote=document.getElementById(`fb-note-${id}`)?.value.trim()||'';try{await fm.updateDoc(fm.doc(db,'feedbackReports',r.docId||r.id),{status,adminNote,adminNoteBy:currentUser.account,adminNoteAt:Date.now()});r.status=status;r.adminNote=adminNote;r.adminNoteBy=currentUser.account;r.adminNoteAt=Date.now();await logAdminAction('備注／更新意見回報',r.docId||r.id);renderAdminFeedback();showToast('回報備注已儲存。','success','儲存成功');}catch(e){showToast('儲存失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}}
        async function deleteFeedback(id){if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='admin')return;const r=appData.feedbackReports.find(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)===String(id));if(!r)return;if(!(await showConfirm('確定刪除此回報訊息嗎？刪除動作會留下稽核紀錄。','刪除回報訊息','確定刪除')))return;try{await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'feedbackReports',r.docId||r.id));appData.feedbackReports=appData.feedbackReports.filter(x=>String(x.docId||x.id)!==String(id));await logAdminAction('刪除意見／異常回報',id);renderAdminFeedback();showToast('回報訊息已刪除。','success','刪除成功');}catch(e){showToast('刪除失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');}}

        function renderAdminUsers() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('admin-users-tbody');
            if (!tbody) return;

            tbody.innerHTML = '';
            const roleRank = {admin:0, high:1, senior:2, normal:3};
            const users = [...(appData.users || [])].sort((a,b)=>{
                const rr=(roleRank[a.role]??99)-(roleRank[b.role]??99);
                if(rr) return rr;
                const au=getUnitDisplayNames(a)||'未指定', bu=getUnitDisplayNames(b)||'未指定';
                return au.localeCompare(bu,'zh-Hant') || String(a.name||'').localeCompare(String(b.name||''),'zh-Hant');
            });
            users.forEach(u => {
                const units = u.units || [u.unit];
                const unitNames = getUnitDisplayNames(u);
                const roleText = permissionDisplayText(u);

                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.className = "hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition";
                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td class="p-4 font-mono font-bold text-white">${u.account}</td>
                    <td class="p-4 font-mono text-cyan-400">${u.staffNumber||'--'}</td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-slate-200">${u.name}</td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-slate-300">${unitNames}</td>
                    <td class="p-4"><span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold ${getEffectiveRole(u) === 'admin' ? 'bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-400 border border-purple-500/20' : getEffectiveRole(u) === 'high' ? 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/20' : getEffectiveRole(u) === 'senior' ? 'bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-400 border border-cyan-500/20' : 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20'}">${roleText}</span></td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-right space-x-2">
                        ${(!isSuperAdminAccount(u.account) || (currentUser && isSuperAdminAccount(currentUser.account))) ? `<button type="button" onclick="openEditUserModal('${u.account}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs transition">編輯</button>` : ''}
                        ${!isSuperAdminAccount(u.account) ? `<button type="button" onclick="deleteUser('${u.account}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-red-600/20 hover:bg-red-600/30 text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs transition">刪除</button>` : ''}
                    </td>
                `;
                tbody.appendChild(tr);
            });
        }

        function openCreateUserModal() {
            if (!currentUser || currentUser.role !== 'admin') {
                showToast('只有最高管理員可以使用此功能。', 'warning', '權限不足');
                return;
            }
            document.getElementById('new-user-account').value = '';
            document.getElementById('new-user-name').value = '';
            document.getElementById('new-user-password').value = ''; document.getElementById('new-user-staff-number').value = '';
            document.querySelectorAll('input[name="new-user-units"]').forEach(cb => cb.checked = false); document.querySelectorAll('input[name="new-user-permissions"]').forEach(cb => cb.checked = cb.value==='normal'); document.getElementById('new-user-police-division').value=''; document.getElementById('new-user-fire-division').value=''; syncUnitDivisionVisibility('new'); document.getElementById('modal-create-user').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

            async function submitNewUser() {
            if (!currentUser || currentUser.role !== 'admin') { showToast('只有最高管理員可以建立帳號。','warning','權限不足'); return; }
            const account=document.getElementById('new-user-account').value.trim(), name=document.getElementById('new-user-name').value.trim(), pass=document.getElementById('new-user-password').value.trim(), staffNumber=document.getElementById('new-user-staff-number')?.value.trim()||'';
            const permissions=[...new Set(Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('input[name="new-user-permissions"]:checked')).map(x=>x.value))];
            const role=USER_ROLE_PRIORITY.find(x=>permissions.includes(x))||'normal';
            const units=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('input[name="new-user-units"]:checked')).map(x=>x.value);
            const policeDivision=document.getElementById('new-user-police-division').value, fireDivision=document.getElementById('new-user-fire-division').value;
            if (!account || !name || !pass || !units.length || !permissions.length) { showToast('請完整填寫帳號、名稱、密碼並至少勾選一個單位。','error','資料不完整'); return; }
            if (units.length>2) { showToast('每個帳號最多只能勾選兩個單位。','warning','資料不符合'); return; }
            if (isSuperAdminAccount(account)) { showToast('Dsuensx 為系統最高權限保護帳號。','warning','操作受限'); return; }
            if (!validateUnitSelections(units,policeDivision,fireDivision)) return;
            if (appData.users.some(u=>String(u.account).toLowerCase()===account.toLowerCase())) { showToast('此帳號／編號已存在。','warning','帳號重複'); return; }
            const u={account,name,pass,role,permissions,units,staffNumber,policeDivision:units.includes('police')?policeDivision:'',fireDivision:units.includes('fire')?fireDivision:''};
            try { await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'users',account),u); appData.users.push(u); closeModal('modal-create-user'); renderAdminUsers(); populateMemberSelects(); showToast('新公職帳號建立成功。','success','建立成功'); }
            catch(e){ showToast('建立失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗'); }
        }

        function openEditUserModal(account) {
            if (!currentUser || currentUser.role !== 'admin') {
                showToast('只有最高管理員可以編輯帳號資料。', 'warning', '權限不足');
                return;
            }
            const u = appData.users.find(x => x.account === account);
            if (isSuperAdminAccount(account) && (!currentUser || !isSuperAdminAccount(currentUser.account))) {
                showToast('Dsuensx 最高權限帳號受到保護，僅本人可以修改。', 'warning', '權限不足');
                return;
            }
            if (!u) return;

            document.getElementById('edit-user-original-account').value = u.account;
            document.getElementById('edit-user-account-input').value = u.account;
            document.getElementById('edit-user-name-input').value = u.name;
            const editPermissions=getUserPermissions(u); document.querySelectorAll('input[name="edit-user-permissions"]').forEach(cb=>cb.checked=editPermissions.includes(cb.value));
            document.getElementById('edit-user-password-input').value = ''; document.getElementById('edit-user-staff-number-input').value = u.staffNumber || ''; const ea=document.getElementById('edit-user-account-input'); if(ea){ea.readOnly=u.role==='admin';ea.classList.toggle('opacity-60',u.role==='admin');}

            const units = u.units || [u.unit];
            document.querySelectorAll('input[name="edit-user-units"]').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = units.includes(cb.value); }); document.getElementById('edit-user-police-division').value=u.policeDivision||''; document.getElementById('edit-user-fire-division').value=u.fireDivision||''; syncUnitDivisionVisibility('edit');

            document.getElementById('modal-edit-user').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        async function submitEditUser() {
            const origAccount=document.getElementById('edit-user-original-account').value, superTarget=isSuperAdminAccount(origAccount), superOperator=!!currentUser&&isSuperAdminAccount(currentUser.account);
            if (!currentUser || currentUser.role!=='admin') { showToast('只有最高管理員可以修改帳號資料。','warning','權限不足'); return; }
            if (superTarget && !superOperator) { showToast('Dsuensx 最高權限帳號受到保護，僅本人可以修改。','warning','權限不足'); return; }
            let account=document.getElementById('edit-user-account-input').value.trim(), name=document.getElementById('edit-user-name-input').value.trim();
            let permissions=[...new Set(Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('input[name="edit-user-permissions"]:checked')).map(x=>x.value))];
            let role=USER_ROLE_PRIORITY.find(x=>permissions.includes(x))||'normal';
            const pass=document.getElementById('edit-user-password-input').value.trim(); const staffNumber=document.getElementById('edit-user-staff-number-input')?.value.trim()||'';
            let units=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('input[name="edit-user-units"]:checked')).map(x=>x.value);
            const policeDivision=document.getElementById('edit-user-police-division').value || '新莊分局', fireDivision=document.getElementById('edit-user-fire-division').value;
            if (superTarget) { account='Dsuensx'; role='admin'; permissions=['admin']; units=['admin','police']; }
            if (appData.users.find(x=>x.account===origAccount)?.role==='admin' || role==='admin') account=origAccount;
            if (!account || !name || !units.length || !permissions.length) { showToast('請完整填寫帳號、名稱並至少勾選一個單位。','error','資料不完整'); return; }
            if (units.length>2 && !superTarget) { showToast('每個帳號最多只能勾選兩個單位。','warning','資料不符合'); return; }
            if (!validateUnitSelections(units,policeDivision,fireDivision)) return;
            const u=appData.users.find(x=>x.account===origAccount); if(!u) return;
            // 修改前先記錄需要稽核的欄位；密碼只記錄「已變更」，絕不寫入舊／新密碼。
            const oldAccount=String(u.account||origAccount);
            const oldStaffNumber=String(u.staffNumber||'');
            const oldPass=String(u.pass||'');
            const changedFields=[];
            if(oldAccount!==String(account)) changedFields.push(`帳號：${oldAccount} → ${account}`);
            if(oldStaffNumber!==String(staffNumber)) changedFields.push(`編號：${oldStaffNumber||'未設定'} → ${staffNumber||'未設定'}`);
            if(pass && pass!==oldPass) changedFields.push('密碼：已變更（不顯示密碼內容）');
            u.account=account; u.name=name; u.role=role; u.permissions=permissions; u.units=units; u.staffNumber=staffNumber; u.policeDivision=units.includes('police')?policeDivision:''; u.fireDivision=units.includes('fire')?fireDivision:''; if(pass)u.pass=pass; if(superTarget)u.protected=true;
            try { const docId=u.docId||origAccount; if(!superTarget&&origAccount!==account) await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'users',docId)); await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db,'users',superTarget?docId:account),{account:u.account,name:u.name,pass:u.pass,role:u.role,permissions:u.permissions,units:u.units,staffNumber:u.staffNumber||'',policeDivision:u.policeDivision||'',fireDivision:u.fireDivision||'',online:u.online||false,lastSeen:u.lastSeen||0,lastLoginAt:u.lastLoginAt||0,lastDevice:u.lastDevice||null,...(superTarget?{protected:true}:{})}); u.docId=superTarget?docId:account; if(superOperator&&superTarget)currentUser={...u};
                if(changedFields.length){
                    const logTarget=oldAccount!==String(account)?`${oldAccount} → ${account}`:oldAccount;
                    await logAdminAction('編輯帳號資訊',logTarget,`管理員帳號：${currentUser?.account||'未知'}；${changedFields.join('；')}`);
                }
                closeModal('modal-edit-user'); renderAdminUsers(); populateMemberSelects(); updateUserBadge(); updateAccessLocks(); showToast('帳號資料更新成功。','success','操作成功'); }
            catch(e){ showToast('更新失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗'); }
        }

        async function deleteUser(account) {
            if (!currentUser || currentUser.role !== 'admin') {
                showToast('只有最高管理員可以刪除帳號。', 'warning', '權限不足');
                return;
            }
            if (isSuperAdminAccount(account)) {
                showToast('Dsuensx 最高權限帳號無法刪除。', 'warning', '操作受限');
                return;
            }
            if (!(await showConfirm(`確定要刪除帳號 ${account} 嗎？`, '刪除使用者帳號', '確定刪除'))) return;
            try {
                await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db, 'users', account));
                appData.users = appData.users.filter(x => x.account !== account);
                renderAdminUsers();
                populateMemberSelects();
                showToast('帳號已刪除。', 'success', '操作成功');
            } catch (e) {
                showToast('刪除失敗：' + e.message, 'error', '操作失敗');
            }
        }

        async function logAdminAction(action, target, detail='', options={}) {
            const now = new Date();
            const logObj = {
                id: 'LOG-' + Date.now(),
                action,
                operator: currentUser ? `${currentUser.name} (${currentUser.account})` : '未知',
                target,
                detail: detail || '',
                time: now.toLocaleString('zh-TW', { hour12: false }),
                timestamp: now.getTime()
            };
            try {
                await fm.setDoc(fm.doc(db, 'adminLogs', logObj.id), logObj);
                appData.adminLogs.unshift(logObj);
            } catch (e) {
                console.error("Log error", e);
            }
            // 個人通知中心的移除／一鍵刪除不會呼叫此函式，因此不會出現在管理中心 Discord LOG。
            const actionText=String(action||'');
            const shouldDiscord = typeof options?.discord === 'boolean' ? options.discord : /編輯|退件|刪除|移除/.test(actionText);
            if(shouldDiscord){ sendManagementDiscordLog(actionText,target,detail); }
        }

        async function deleteAllAdminLogs(){
            if(!isSuperAdminAccount(currentUser?.account)){
                showToast('只有 Dsuensx 可以刪除所有管理中心 LOG 紀錄。','warning','權限不足');
                return;
            }
            if(!(await showConfirm('確定要一鍵刪除所有管理中心 LOG 紀錄嗎？此操作無法復原。','刪除全部管理中心紀錄','確定刪除'))) return;
            try{
                const snap=await fm.getDocs(fm.collection(db,'adminLogs'));
                for(const d of snap.docs){
                    await fm.deleteDoc(fm.doc(db,'adminLogs',d.id));
                }
                appData.adminLogs=[];
                renderAdminLogs();
                showToast('所有管理中心 LOG 紀錄已刪除。','success','操作完成');
            }catch(e){
                showToast('刪除管理中心 LOG 失敗：'+e.message,'error','操作失敗');
            }
        }

        function renderAdminLogs() {
            const clear=document.getElementById('clear-all-admin-logs-btn');
            if(clear) clear.classList.toggle('hidden',!isSuperAdminAccount(currentUser?.account));
            const tbody = document.getElementById('admin-logs-tbody');
            if (!tbody) return;

            tbody.innerHTML = '';
            const oneWeekAgo = Date.now() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
            const recentLogs = appData.adminLogs.filter(l => l.timestamp >= oneWeekAgo);

            if (recentLogs.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="p-6 text-center text-slate-500">最近一周內無編輯或移除紀錄。</td></tr>`;
                return;
            }

            recentLogs.forEach(l => {
                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.className = "hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition";
                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td class="p-4 font-semibold text-purple-400">${escapeHtml(l.action||'--')}</td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-slate-200">${escapeHtml(l.operator||'--')}</td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-slate-300 font-mono">${escapeHtml(l.target||'--')}</td>
                    <td class="p-4 text-slate-400 text-xs">${formatDateTime(l.timestamp, l.time)}</td>
                `;
                tbody.appendChild(tr);
            });
        }

        function estimateJsonBytes(value) {
            try {
                return new Blob([JSON.stringify(value)]).size;
            } catch (e) {
                return JSON.stringify(value || '').length;
            }
        }

        function renderAdminStorage() {
            const groups = [
                { label:'警務文件', icon:'fa-shield', lists:[appData.policeReports, appData.policePendingReports, appData.policePunishments] },
                { label:'消防文件', icon:'fa-fire-extinguisher', lists:[appData.fireReports, appData.firePendingReports, appData.emsReports, appData.emsPendingReports] },
                { label:'市民資訊', icon:'fa-id-card', lists:[appData.citizenRecords] },
                { label:'車輛資訊', icon:'fa-car-side', lists:[appData.vehicleRecords] }
            ];
            const allCollections = [appData.users,appData.policeReports,appData.policePendingReports,appData.fireReports,appData.firePendingReports,appData.emsReports,appData.emsPendingReports,appData.policePunishments,appData.policeClocks,appData.fireClocks,appData.adminLogs,appData.announcements,appData.blockedDevices,appData.adminClocks,appData.feedbackReports,appData.loginSessions,appData.reportNotifications,appData.citizenRecords,appData.vehicleRecords,appData.wantedPendingRecords,appData.wantedRecords];
            const totalDocs = allCollections.reduce((sum,list)=>sum+(list||[]).length,0);
            const approxBytes = allCollections.reduce((sum,list)=>sum+(list||[]).reduce((inner,item)=>inner+Math.max(1024,estimateJsonBytes(item)),0),0);
            const payloadBytes = allCollections.reduce((sum,list)=>sum+(list||[]).reduce((inner,item)=>inner+estimateJsonBytes(item),0),0);
            const maxBytes=1024*1024*1024;
            const percentNumber=Math.min(100,Math.max(0,(approxBytes/maxBytes)*100));
            const percentText=percentNumber>=0.01?percentNumber.toFixed(2):percentNumber>=0.0001?percentNumber.toFixed(4):(totalDocs>0?percentNumber.toFixed(6):'0.0000');
            const freeMb=Math.max(0,(maxBytes-approxBytes)/(1024*1024));
            const formatBytes=bytes=>bytes<1024?`${bytes.toFixed(0)} B`:bytes<1024*1024?`${(bytes/1024).toFixed(1)} KB`:bytes<1024*1024*1024?`${(bytes/(1024*1024)).toFixed(2)} MB`:`${(bytes/(1024*1024*1024)).toFixed(2)} GB`;
            const categoryList=document.getElementById('storage-category-list');
            if(categoryList){categoryList.innerHTML=groups.map(g=>{const rows=g.lists.flatMap(list=>list||[]);const bytes=rows.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+Math.max(1024,estimateJsonBytes(item)),0);const percent=approxBytes>0?(bytes/approxBytes)*100:0;return `<div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3"><div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"><div class="flex items-center gap-2"><i class="fa-solid ${g.icon} text-purple-400"></i><span class="font-semibold text-slate-200">${g.label}</span></div><span class="text-xs text-slate-400">${rows.length.toLocaleString()} 筆</span></div><div class="flex items-center justify-between mt-2 text-[11px]"><span class="text-slate-500">估算占用 ${formatBytes(bytes)}</span><span class="text-purple-400 font-mono">${percent.toFixed(2)}%</span></div><div class="w-full bg-slate-900 rounded-full h-1.5 mt-2 overflow-hidden"><div class="bg-purple-500 h-full rounded-full" style="width:${Math.min(100,percent)}%"></div></div></div>`;}).join('');}
            const usedTextEl=document.getElementById('storage-used-text'),percentTextEl=document.getElementById('storage-percent-text'),progressEl=document.getElementById('storage-progress-bar'),docsEl=document.getElementById('storage-docs-count'),freeEl=document.getElementById('storage-free-text');
            if(usedTextEl)usedTextEl.textContent=`當前已使用：約 ${formatBytes(approxBytes)}`;
            if(percentTextEl){percentTextEl.textContent=`${percentText}%`;percentTextEl.title=`依目前載入資料估算：${payloadBytes.toLocaleString()} bytes 文件內容`;}
            if(progressEl)progressEl.style.width=`${Math.max(percentNumber,totalDocs>0?0.5:0)}%`;
            if(docsEl)docsEl.textContent=totalDocs;
            if(freeEl)freeEl.textContent=`${freeMb.toFixed(1)} MB`;
        }
    </script>

Firebase Functions functions/index.js
const { onDocumentWritten } = require('firebase-functions/v2/firestore');
const { defineSecret } = require('firebase-functions/params');
const admin = require('firebase-admin');

admin.initializeApp();

// 建議使用 Firebase Secret Manager，不要把 Discord Webhook 寫進 GitHub HTML。
const DISCORD_POLICE_WEBHOOK = defineSecret('DISCORD_POLICE_WEBHOOK');
const DISCORD_FIRE_WEBHOOK = defineSecret('DISCORD_FIRE_WEBHOOK');
const DISCORD_ADMIN_LOG_WEBHOOK = defineSecret('DISCORD_ADMIN_LOG_WEBHOOK');

const REGION = 'asia-east1';
const SITE_URL = 'https://gken99903-source.github.io/-NTPC-RP-/';

function text(value, fallback = '--') {
  const s = String(value ?? '').trim();
  return s || fallback;
}

function limit(value, max = 1024) {
  const s = text(value);
  return s.length > max ? `${s.slice(0, max - 1)}…` : s;
}

function field(name, value, inline = false) {
  return { name: String(name).slice(0, 256), value: limit(value, 1024), inline: !!inline };
}

function buildPayload(department, report) {
  const isPolice = department === 'police';
  const isEMS = department === 'ems';
  const fields = [];
  const title = isEMS ? '🚑 新案件｜EMS 救護報告' : isPolice ? '🚓 新案件｜警察案件報告' : '🚒 新案件｜消防案件報告';

  if (isEMS) {
    fields.push(
      field('案件編號', report.id || report.caseId || report.docId, true),
      field('填寫人', `${text(report.authorName)} (${text(report.authorId)})`, true),
      field('主訴', report.chiefComplaint),
      field('傷患', report.patient, true),
      field('出勤單位／車輛', report.unitVehicle, true),
      field('治療／處置', report.treatment)
    );
  } else {
    fields.push(
      field('案件編號', report.caseId, true),
      field('填寫人', `${text(report.authorName)} (${text(report.authorId)})`, true),
      field('案件標題', report.title),
      field('案件內容', report.content)
    );
    if (isPolice) {
      fields.push(
        field('嫌疑人', report.suspect),
        field('違反條例', Array.isArray(report.violations) ? report.violations.join('、') : report.violations),
        field('刑期', report.jailMinutes != null ? `${report.jailMinutes} 分鐘` : '--', true),
        field('組織犯罪', report.organizedCrime ? '是' : '否', true)
      );
    }
  }

  return {
    username: 'NTPC-RP 勤務系統',
    allowed_mentions: { parse: [] },
    embeds: [{
      title,
      description: `案件已進入${isPolice ? '警察' : '消防／EMS'}高層審核流程。`,
      color: isPolice ? 3447003 : isEMS ? 15158332 : 15105570,
      fields,
      footer: { text: '新北市 RP 公職單位勤務系統' },
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      url: SITE_URL
    }]
  };
}

async function postDiscord(webhookUrl, payload) {
  if (!webhookUrl) throw new Error('Discord Webhook Secret 未設定');
  const response = await fetch(`${webhookUrl}${webhookUrl.includes('?') ? '&' : '?'}wait=true`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    const body = await response.text().catch(() => '');
    throw new Error(`Discord HTTP ${response.status}${body ? `: ${body.slice(0, 500)}` : ''}`);
  }
}

function departmentForCollection(collection) {
  if (collection === 'policePendingReports') return 'police';
  if (collection === 'firePendingReports') return 'fire';
  if (collection === 'emsPendingReports') return 'ems';
  return null;
}

exports.ntpcrpCaseToDiscord = onDocumentWritten(
  {
    document: '{collection}/{docId}',
    region: REGION,
    secrets: [DISCORD_POLICE_WEBHOOK, DISCORD_FIRE_WEBHOOK],
    retry: true,
  },
  async (event) => {
    const collection = event.params.collection;
    const department = departmentForCollection(collection);
    if (!department || !event.data?.after?.exists) return;

    const after = event.data.after.data() || {};
    const before = event.data.before?.exists ? (event.data.before.data() || {}) : null;

    // 只處理「新送審」或「退件後重新送審」。
    if (after.reviewStatus !== 'pending') return;
    if (after.discordNotificationSent === true) return;
    if (before && before.reviewStatus === 'pending' && after.submittedAt === before.submittedAt) return;

    const webhook = department === 'police'
      ? DISCORD_POLICE_WEBHOOK.value()
      : DISCORD_FIRE_WEBHOOK.value();

    await postDiscord(webhook, buildPayload(department, after));

    // 成功後才標記，失敗時 Cloud Functions retry，避免案件已寫入但 Discord 沒收到。
    await event.data.after.ref.set({
      discordNotificationSent: true,
      discordNotificationSentAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      discordNotificationError: admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete()
    }, { merge: true });
  }
);

  }

    "source": "functions"
  }
}
部署說明 README
NTPC-RP Discord 後端通知部署說明

這次改版的核心：
GitHub Pages 只負責把案件寫入 Firebase Firestore。
Firebase Cloud Functions 監聽：
- policePendingReports
- firePendingReports
- emsPendingReports

當案件是「新送審」或「退件後重新送審」時，Cloud Function 才發送 Discord。
成功後才在案件文件寫入 discordNotificationSent=true；Discord 失敗會由 Cloud Functions 重試。

一、準備
1. 安裝 Node.js 20 或以上。
2. 安裝 Firebase CLI：
   npm install -g firebase-tools
3. 登入：
   firebase login

二、進入此資料夾
cd ntpcrp-discord-functions

三、設定 Discord Webhook Secret
請先在 Discord 重新產生三組新的 Webhook，不要使用已經公開過的舊 URL。

警察：
firebase functions:secrets:set DISCORD_POLICE_WEBHOOK

消防／EMS：
firebase functions:secrets:set DISCORD_FIRE_WEBHOOK

管理 LOG（本次案件通知功能未使用，但預留）：
firebase functions:secrets:set DISCORD_ADMIN_LOG_WEBHOOK

四、安裝 Functions 套件
cd functions
npm install
cd ..

五、部署
firebase deploy --only functions:ntpcrpCaseToDiscord

六、部署成功後
把 index.html 替換到 GitHub Pages 原本網站的首頁檔案。

注意：
- 不需要把 Discord Webhook 再寫回 HTML。
- 不要把新的 Webhook 貼到 GitHub 公開 repository。
- 本版本會使用 Firebase 專案 ntpcrp-cb0725。
- Cloud Function 區域為 asia-east1。

七、測試
1. 發送一件警察案件。
2. 確認 policePendingReports 出現案件。
3. 等待數秒，警察 Discord 頻道應收到通知。
4. 查看案件文件是否出現 discordNotificationSent=true。
5. 再測一件消防案件與 EMS 案件。

如果 Discord 發送失敗，Function 會進行重試；不應因此刪除或影響 Firebase 案件資料。
已複製
